    df = df[df["num_mismatches"] > 0].copy()
    log_filter(logger, f"{guide_id} on-target sites (mismatches == 0)", n_before, len(df))

    # Assign constant guide_seq to all remaining (off-target) rows
    df["guide_seq"] = guide_seq_value

    # PAM
    df["pam_seq"] = df[pam_col].astype(str).str.strip().str.upper().str[:3]

    # Strand: sense/antisense → +/−
    _STRAND_MAP = {"sense": "+", "antisense": "-", "+": "+", "-": "-"}
    df["strand"] = (
        df[strand_col].astype(str).str.strip().str.lower().map(_STRAND_MAP)
        .fillna(df[strand_col].astype(str).str.strip())
    )

    # read_count = sum of all replicate columns (captured before any new cols)
    df["read_count"] = (
        df[rep_cols].apply(pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce").fillna(0).sum(axis=1)
    )

    # Constant metadata
    df["guide_id"] = guide_id
    df["cell_type"] = "K562"
    df["dataset"] = "discover_seq_k562"
    df["genome_build"] = "hg38"

    return df


def parse_discover_seq_k562(
    s1_path: Path = _DISCOVER_SEQ_S1_RAW,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Parse DISCOVER-seq K562 data from Wienert et al. 2019 (Science).

    Reads three DISCOVER sheets from Supplementary Table S1:

    * ``DISCOVER VEGFA`` — header row 2 (0-indexed); 57 total sites
    * ``DISCOVER RNF2``  — header row 1; 0 off-targets after filtering
    * ``DISCOVER HBB``   — header row 1; NaN separator rows dropped

    DISCOVER-seq identifies in-cell DSBs via MRE11 ChIP-seq.  Coordinates are
    already hg38; no liftover is applied.

    Expected content (3 sgRNAs in K562):
    - VEGFA : 56 off-target sites  (57 total; 1 on-target removed)
    - RNF2  :  0 off-target sites
    - HBB   :  4 off-target sites  (WT Cas9 + HiFi Cas9 rows kept separately)

    **POSITIVES ONLY.**  Negatives must be generated via Cas-OFFinder.

    Parameters
    ----------
    s1_path : Path
        Path to Supplementary Table S1 (``wienert2019_discover_seq_S1.xlsx``).
        The file must contain sheets named ``'DISCOVER VEGFA'``,
        ``'DISCOVER RNF2'``, and ``'DISCOVER HBB'``.
        Default: ``data/raw/wienert2019_discover_seq_S1.xlsx``.

    Returns
    -------
    pd.DataFrame
        Standardised off-target table (see module docstring for schema).

    Raises
    ------
    FileNotFoundError
        With download instructions if the file is missing.
    ValueError
        If a required column cannot be found; message lists actual columns.
    """
    logger.info(
        "=== Parsing DISCOVER-seq K562 (Wienert 2019) from %s ===", s1_path
    )

    if not s1_path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"DISCOVER-seq S1 file not found: {s1_path}\n\n"
            "Download instructions:\n"
            "  1. Open https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6589096/\n"
            "  2. Scroll to 'Supplementary Materials'.\n"
            f"  3. Download Table S1 → save as: {_DISCOVER_SEQ_S1_RAW}\n"
        )

    xl = pd.ExcelFile(s1_path, engine="openpyxl")
    logger.info("Sheets in S1: %s", xl.sheet_names)

    frames: list[pd.DataFrame] = []

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # DISCOVER VEGFA  (header=2: rows 0-1 are title/span, row 2 is header)
    # Columns: Unnamed:0 (off-target gene), Chr:Start-End, Cutsite, Strand,
    #          PAM, Guide sequence, Mismatches, Rep1 (BW43), Rep2 (BW42),
    #          Rep3 (BW16), HiFi Cas9 (BW28)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if "DISCOVER VEGFA" in xl.sheet_names:
        df_vegfa = xl.parse("DISCOVER VEGFA", header=2, engine="openpyxl")
        logger.info(
            "DISCOVER VEGFA: %d rows, columns: %s", len(df_vegfa), list(df_vegfa.columns)
        )
        parsed_vegfa = _parse_discover_sheet(
            df_vegfa,
            guide_id="VEGFA",
            coord_col="Chr:Start-End",
            strand_col="Strand",
            pam_col="PAM",
            guide_seq_col="Guide sequence",
            mismatches_col="Mismatches",
        )
        frames.append(parsed_vegfa)
        logger.info("VEGFA off-targets after filtering: %d", len(parsed_vegfa))
    else:
        logger.warning("Sheet 'DISCOVER VEGFA' not found in %s; skipping.", s1_path)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # DISCOVER RNF2  (header=1: row 0 is title, row 1 is header)
    # Columns: gRNA, gene, Hg38 coordinates, cut site, Strand, PAM,
    #          Guide sequence, mismatches, Rep1 (BW19), Rep2 (BW27)
    # All rows have mismatches=0 (on-target only) → 0 off-targets returned.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if "DISCOVER RNF2" in xl.sheet_names:
        df_rnf2 = xl.parse("DISCOVER RNF2", header=1, engine="openpyxl")
        logger.info(
            "DISCOVER RNF2: %d rows, columns: %s", len(df_rnf2), list(df_rnf2.columns)
        )
        parsed_rnf2 = _parse_discover_sheet(
            df_rnf2,
            guide_id="RNF2",
            coord_col="Hg38 coordinates",
            strand_col="Strand",
            pam_col="PAM",
            guide_seq_col="Guide sequence",
            mismatches_col="mismatches",
        )
        frames.append(parsed_rnf2)
        logger.info("RNF2 off-targets after filtering: %d", len(parsed_rnf2))
    else:
        logger.warning("Sheet 'DISCOVER RNF2' not found in %s; skipping.", s1_path)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # DISCOVER HBB  (header=1: row 0 is title, row 1 is header)
    # Columns: gRNA, gene, Cas9, Hg38 coordinates, cut site, (Unnamed),
    #          Strand, PAM, Guide sequence, mismatches, Rep1 (BW29), Rep2 (BW13)
    # Rows 3-4 (0-indexed in data) are NaN separator rows between WT and HiFi
    # Cas9 sections — dropped by _parse_discover_sheet via coord_col notna().
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if "DISCOVER HBB" in xl.sheet_names:
        df_hbb = xl.parse("DISCOVER HBB", header=1, engine="openpyxl")
        logger.info(
            "DISCOVER HBB: %d rows, columns: %s", len(df_hbb), list(df_hbb.columns)
        )
        parsed_hbb = _parse_discover_sheet(
            df_hbb,
            guide_id="HBB",
            coord_col="Hg38 coordinates",
            strand_col="Strand",
            pam_col="PAM",
            guide_seq_col="Guide sequence",
            mismatches_col="mismatches",
        )
        frames.append(parsed_hbb)
        logger.info("HBB off-targets after filtering: %d", len(parsed_hbb))
    else:
        logger.warning("Sheet 'DISCOVER HBB' not found in %s; skipping.", s1_path)

    if not frames:
        raise ValueError(
            f"No DISCOVER sheets found in {s1_path}. "
            "Expected at least one of: 'DISCOVER VEGFA', 'DISCOVER RNF2', "
            "'DISCOVER HBB'."
        )

    # Filter out fully-empty frames before concat to avoid FutureWarning about
    # all-NA columns affecting dtype inference.
    non_empty = [f for f in frames if not f.empty]
    if not non_empty:
        # All sheets had 0 off-targets (e.g. RNF2-only run)
        df_all = pd.DataFrame(columns=frames[0].columns if frames else [])
    else:
        df_all = pd.concat(non_empty, ignore_index=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Normalise chromosome names
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    n_before = len(df_all)
    df_all, valid_mask = _apply_normalise_chrom(df_all, "chrom")
    df_all = df_all[valid_mask].copy()
    log_filter(logger, "non-standard contigs", n_before, len(df_all))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Assemble standardised output
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    out = df_all[_OUTPUT_COLUMNS].copy()
    for col, dtype in _OUTPUT_DTYPES.items():
        out[col] = out[col].astype(dtype)

    logger.info(
        "DISCOVER-seq K562 final: %d rows, %d guides: %s",
        len(out),
        out["guide_id"].nunique(),
        sorted(out["guide_id"].unique()),
    )
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument(
        "--guide-seq",
        type=Path,
        default=_GUIDE_SEQ_RAW,
        help=f"Path to Tsai 2015 GUIDE-seq TSV (default: {_GUIDE_SEQ_RAW})",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--change-seq",
        type=Path,
        default=_CHANGE_SEQ_RAW,
        help=f"Path to Lazzarotto 2020 CHANGE-seq XLSX (default: {_CHANGE_SEQ_RAW})",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--deepcrispr",
        type=Path,
        default=_DEEPCRISPR_RAW,
        metavar="PATH",
        help=f"Path to Chuai 2018 DeepCRISPR Additional File 7 (default: {_DEEPCRISPR_RAW})",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--deepcrispr-genome-build",
        choices=["hg19", "hg38"],
        default="hg19",
        metavar="{hg19,hg38}",
        help="Genome build of DeepCRISPR source coordinates (default: hg19). "
             "Pass hg38 to skip liftover if the file was already lifted.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--discover-seq-s1",
        type=Path,
        default=_DISCOVER_SEQ_S1_RAW,
        metavar="PATH",
        help=f"Path to Wienert 2019 Supplementary Table S1 (default: {_DISCOVER_SEQ_S1_RAW})",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--discover-seq-s3",
        type=Path,
        default=_DISCOVER_SEQ_S3_RAW,
        metavar="PATH",
        help=f"Path to Wienert 2019 Supplementary Table S3 (default: {_DISCOVER_SEQ_S3_RAW}). "
             "Pass the same path as --discover-seq-s1 if both tables are in one workbook.",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--out-dir",
        type=Path,
        default=_PROCESSED,
        help=f"Output directory for parquet files (default: {_PROCESSED})",
    )
    return p.parse_args()


def main() -> None:
    args = _parse_args()
    args.out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # --- GUIDE-seq ---
    gs_out = args.out_dir / "guide_seq_tsai2015.parquet"
    try:
        df_gs = parse_guide_seq(args.guide_seq)
        df_gs.to_parquet(gs_out, index=False)
        logger.info("Saved GUIDE-seq → %s  (%d rows)", gs_out, len(df_gs))
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        logger.error("%s", e)
        sys.exit(1)

    # --- CHANGE-seq (optional — skip with instructions if absent) ---
    cs_out = args.out_dir / "change_seq_lazzarotto2020.parquet"
    if not args.change_seq.exists():
        logger.warning(
            "CHANGE-seq file not found: %s\n"
            "Skipping CHANGE-seq parsing.\n"
            "Download instructions:\n"
            "  https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7652380/\n"
            "  → Supplementary Material → SuppTables.xlsx\n"
            "  → save as data/raw/lazzarotto2020_change_seq_supp_tables.xlsx",
            args.change_seq,
        )
    else:
        try:
            df_cs = parse_change_seq(args.change_seq)
            df_cs.to_parquet(cs_out, index=False)
            logger.info("Saved CHANGE-seq → %s  (%d rows)", cs_out, len(df_cs))
        except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError) as e:
            logger.error("%s", e)

    # --- DeepCRISPR K562 (optional — skip with instructions if absent) ---
    dc_out = args.out_dir / "deepcrispr_k562.parquet"
    if not args.deepcrispr.exists():
        logger.warning(
            "DeepCRISPR file not found: %s\n"
            "Skipping DeepCRISPR parsing.\n"
            "Download instructions:\n"
            "  https://genomebiology.biomedcentral.com/articles/10.1186/s13059-018-1459-4\n"
            "  → Additional files → Additional file 7\n"
            "  → save as data/raw/chuai2018_deepcrispr_k562.xlsx",
            args.deepcrispr,
        )
    else:
        try:
            df_dc = parse_deepcrispr_k562(
                args.deepcrispr,
                src_genome_build=args.deepcrispr_genome_build,
            )
            df_dc.to_parquet(dc_out, index=False)
            logger.info("Saved DeepCRISPR K562 → %s  (%d rows)", dc_out, len(df_dc))
        except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError) as e:
            logger.error("%s", e)

    # --- DISCOVER-seq K562 (optional — skip with instructions if absent) ---
    ds_out = args.out_dir / "discover_seq_k562.parquet"
    if not args.discover_seq_s1.exists():
        logger.warning(
            "DISCOVER-seq S1 file not found: %s\n"
            "Skipping DISCOVER-seq parsing.\n"
            "Download instructions:\n"
            "  https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6589096/\n"
            "  → Supplementary Materials\n"
            "  → Table S1 → save as data/raw/wienert2019_discover_seq_S1.xlsx",
            args.discover_seq_s1,
        )
    else:
        try:
            df_ds = parse_discover_seq_k562(args.discover_seq_s1)
            df_ds.to_parquet(ds_out, index=False)
            logger.info("Saved DISCOVER-seq K562 → %s  (%d rows)", ds_out, len(df_ds))
        except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError) as e:
            logger.error("%s", e)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== scripts/03_generate_candidates.py ===
"""
scripts/03_generate_candidates.py — Enumerate candidate off-target sites with Cas-OFFinder.

For each unique guide RNA in the training datasets (GUIDE-seq, DeepCRISPR K562,
DISCOVER-seq K562), run Cas-OFFinder against hg38 to find all candidate off-target
sites within the mismatch threshold.

CHANGE-seq guides are excluded by default: that assay is performed on naked DNA
and cannot be used as positive labels for in-cell cleavage (naked DNA, no chromatin context).
Pass --include-change-seq only if you need the extra candidate sites.

Usage
-----
    source venv/Scripts/activate
    python scripts/03_generate_candidates.py [options]

    --genome PATH           Path to per-chrom FASTA directory (default: data/raw/genome/chroms)
    --max-mismatches N      Cas-OFFinder mismatch threshold (default: 4)
    --output PATH           Output parquet (default: data/processed/candidates_all.parquet)
    --include-change-seq    Also run Cas-OFFinder for CHANGE-seq guides (110 extra guides)
    --guide-seq-only        Only run for GUIDE-seq guides (9 guides; faster for testing)

Output schema
-------------
    guide_id            str         e.g. "VEGFA_site2"
    guide_seq           str         20-nt protospacer (no PAM, uppercase)
    chrom               str         normalized chromosome, e.g. "chr6"
    start               int64       0-based start (BED convention)
    strand              str         "+" or "-"
    off_target_seq      str         20-nt off-target spacer (no PAM)
    pam_seq             str         3-nt PAM observed at the site
    num_mismatches      int64       spacer mismatches (0 = on-target; removed)
    mismatch_positions  object      list[int], 1-based positions of mismatches
    source_datasets     str         comma-separated datasets this guide appears in
    primary_dataset     str         primary source dataset (for exclude_datasets filtering)

Design notes
------------
* Cas-OFFinder is run ONCE per unique guide_seq.  If the same guide_seq appears
  under multiple guide_ids (e.g. "VEGFA_site2" in both GUIDE-seq and DISCOVER-seq),
  the candidate rows are duplicated so each guide_id gets its own rows.  This
  is necessary because positive labels are assigned per-guide_id in script 04.
* On-target sites (num_mismatches == 0) are removed by find_offtargets
  (remove_ontarget=True) per project protocol.
* All data drops are logged for the paper Methods section.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import logging
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

# --- path setup so src imports work when run as a script ---
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from src.offtarget_finder import find_offtargets
from src.utils import get_logger

logger = get_logger("generate_candidates")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Paths
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_RAW = _ROOT / "data" / "raw"
_PROCESSED = _ROOT / "data" / "processed"

_GENOME_DEFAULT = _RAW / "genome" / "chroms"  # directory of per-chrom FASTAs (Cas-OFFinder v2 requires this)
_GUIDE_SEQ_PATH = _PROCESSED / "guide_seq_tsai2015.parquet"
_CHANGE_SEQ_PATH = _PROCESSED / "change_seq_lazzarotto2020.parquet"
_DEEPCRISPR_PATH = _PROCESSED / "deepcrispr_k562.parquet"
_DISCOVER_SEQ_PATH = _PROCESSED / "discover_seq_k562.parquet"
_CANDIDATES_OUT = _PROCESSED / "candidates_all.parquet"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _load_guides_from_parquet(
    path: Path,
    dataset_name: str,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Load (guide_id, guide_seq, dataset) from a processed parquet.

    Returns a DataFrame with columns: guide_id, guide_seq, dataset.
    Drops guide_ids with missing guide_seq.
    """
    if not path.exists():
        logger.warning("Parquet not found, skipping: %s", path)
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=["guide_id", "guide_seq", "dataset"])

    df = pd.read_parquet(path, columns=["guide_id", "guide_seq"])
    before = len(df)
    df = df.dropna(subset=["guide_seq"])
    if len(df) < before:
        logger.warning(
            "[%s] Dropped %d rows with missing guide_seq",
            dataset_name, before - len(df),
        )

    # Unique (guide_id, guide_seq) pairs
    df = df.drop_duplicates(subset=["guide_id", "guide_seq"])
    df["dataset"] = dataset_name
    logger.info("[%s] %d unique guide_ids loaded", dataset_name, len(df))
    return df[["guide_id", "guide_seq", "dataset"]]


def _run_casoffinder_for_guide(
    guide_id: str,
    guide_seq: str,
    genome_path: Path,
    max_mismatches: int,
) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
    """Run Cas-OFFinder for a single guide and return a tidy DataFrame.

    Returns None if Cas-OFFinder fails for this guide.
    Renames output column 'guide' → 'guide_seq' and adds 'guide_id'.
    """
    try:
        df = find_offtargets(
            guide_seq,
            genome_path,
            max_mismatches=max_mismatches,
            remove_ontarget=True,
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error(
            "Cas-OFFinder failed for guide_id=%s guide_seq=%s: %s",
            guide_id, guide_seq, exc,
        )
        return None

    if df.empty:
        logger.warning("No candidates found for guide_id=%s", guide_id)
        return df

    # find_offtargets returns column 'guide' (the input sequence); rename to guide_seq
    df = df.rename(columns={"guide": "guide_seq"})
    df["guide_id"] = guide_id
    logger.info(
        "  guide_id=%-25s  candidates=%d  max_mm=%d",
        guide_id, len(df), df["num_mismatches"].max() if len(df) else 0,
    )
    return df


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Run Cas-OFFinder for all training guides → candidates_all.parquet"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--genome", type=Path, default=_GENOME_DEFAULT,
        help="Path to directory of per-chrom FASTA files (default: data/raw/genome/chroms)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--max-mismatches", type=int, default=4,
        help="Cas-OFFinder mismatch threshold (default: 4)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output", type=Path, default=_CANDIDATES_OUT,
        help="Output parquet path (default: data/processed/candidates_all.parquet)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--include-change-seq", action="store_true",
        help="Also run Cas-OFFinder for CHANGE-seq guides (110 guides, slow)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--guide-seq-only", action="store_true",
        help="Only run for GUIDE-seq guides (9 guides; useful for quick testing)",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    genome_path: Path = args.genome
    if not genome_path.exists():
        logger.error("Genome directory not found: %s", genome_path)
        logger.error(
            "Expected a directory of per-chromosome FASTA files (chr1.fa, chr2.fa, ...).\n"
            "Split hg38.fa first — see the genome split script or README."
        )
        sys.exit(1)

    # ---- Collect guides from each dataset -----------------------------------
    guide_frames: list[pd.DataFrame] = []

    guide_seq_guides = _load_guides_from_parquet(_GUIDE_SEQ_PATH, "guide_seq_tsai2015")
    guide_frames.append(guide_seq_guides)

    if not args.guide_seq_only:
        deepcrispr_guides = _load_guides_from_parquet(_DEEPCRISPR_PATH, "deepcrispr_k562")
        discover_guides = _load_guides_from_parquet(_DISCOVER_SEQ_PATH, "discover_seq_k562")
        guide_frames.extend([deepcrispr_guides, discover_guides])

        if args.include_change_seq:
            change_guides = _load_guides_from_parquet(
                _CHANGE_SEQ_PATH, "change_seq_lazzarotto2020"
            )
            guide_frames.append(change_guides)
            logger.warning(
                "Including CHANGE-seq: %d guides. "
                "Remember: CHANGE-seq sites are NOT valid in-cell positives (naked DNA, no chromatin context).",
                len(change_guides),
            )

    all_guides = pd.concat(guide_frames, ignore_index=True)

    # ---- Build guide_seq → set of (guide_id, datasets) mapping -------------
    # Group by guide_seq so we run Cas-OFFinder ONCE per unique sequence.
    # Multiple guide_ids sharing the same guide_seq get separate output rows.
    guide_seq_to_rows: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
    for _, row in all_guides.iterrows():
        seq = row["guide_seq"]
        if seq not in guide_seq_to_rows:
            guide_seq_to_rows[seq] = []
        guide_seq_to_rows[seq].append(
            {"guide_id": row["guide_id"], "dataset": row["dataset"]}
        )

    # For guides with the same guide_id but different datasets, merge datasets
    # into source_datasets and keep the first (primary) dataset
    guide_id_to_info: dict[str, dict] = {}
    guide_id_to_seq: dict[str, str] = {}
    for seq, rows in guide_seq_to_rows.items():
        for r in rows:
            gid = r["guide_id"]
            if gid not in guide_id_to_info:
                guide_id_to_info[gid] = {"datasets": [r["dataset"]], "primary": r["dataset"]}
                guide_id_to_seq[gid] = seq
            else:
                if r["dataset"] not in guide_id_to_info[gid]["datasets"]:
                    guide_id_to_info[gid]["datasets"].append(r["dataset"])

    total_unique_seqs = len(set(guide_id_to_seq.values()))
    total_guide_ids = len(guide_id_to_seq)
    logger.info(
        "Total: %d unique guide_ids across %d unique sequences — "
        "will run Cas-OFFinder %d times",
        total_guide_ids, total_unique_seqs, total_unique_seqs,
    )

    # ---- Run Cas-OFFinder per unique guide_seq, duplicate for each guide_id -
    # We group guide_ids by their guide_seq to run the binary only once per seq.
    seq_to_guide_ids: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for gid, seq in guide_id_to_seq.items():
        seq_to_guide_ids.setdefault(seq, []).append(gid)

    all_candidates: list[pd.DataFrame] = []
    failed: list[str] = []

    for i, (guide_seq, guide_ids) in enumerate(seq_to_guide_ids.items(), start=1):
        logger.info(
            "Running Cas-OFFinder %d/%d  guide_seq=%s  guide_ids=%s",
            i, len(seq_to_guide_ids), guide_seq, guide_ids,
        )
        # Run once for this sequence (use first guide_id as label for the run)
        primary_gid = guide_ids[0]
        df = _run_casoffinder_for_guide(
            primary_gid, guide_seq, genome_path, args.max_mismatches
        )
        if df is None:
            failed.extend(guide_ids)
            continue
        if df.empty:
            continue

        # Duplicate for each guide_id sharing this sequence
        for gid in guide_ids:
            gdf = df.copy()
            gdf["guide_id"] = gid
            info = guide_id_to_info[gid]
            gdf["source_datasets"] = ",".join(info["datasets"])
            gdf["primary_dataset"] = info["primary"]
            all_candidates.append(gdf)

    if failed:
        logger.error("Cas-OFFinder failed for %d guide_ids: %s", len(failed), failed)

    if not all_candidates:
        logger.error("No candidates generated — check genome path and Cas-OFFinder installation.")
        sys.exit(1)

    # ---- Assemble and save --------------------------------------------------
    combined = pd.concat(all_candidates, ignore_index=True)

    # Enforce correct dtypes
    combined["start"] = combined["start"].astype("int64")
    combined["num_mismatches"] = combined["num_mismatches"].astype("int64")

    # Column ordering
    ordered_cols = [
        "guide_id", "guide_seq", "chrom", "start", "strand",
        "off_target_seq", "pam_seq", "num_mismatches", "mismatch_positions",
        "source_datasets", "primary_dataset",
    ]
    extra_cols = [c for c in combined.columns if c not in ordered_cols]
    combined = combined[ordered_cols + extra_cols]

    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    combined.to_parquet(args.output, index=False)

    logger.info(
        "Saved %d candidate sites for %d guide_ids → %s",
        len(combined), combined["guide_id"].nunique(), args.output,
    )
    logger.info(
        "Mismatch distribution:\n%s",
        combined["num_mismatches"].value_counts().sort_index().to_string(),
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== scripts/04_extract_features.py ===
"""
scripts/04_extract_features.py — Extract sequence, AlphaGenome, and ENCODE features.

Builds per-cell-type feature matrices for K562 and HEK293, then concatenates
them into a single combined feature matrix for model training.

Requires
--------
* data/processed/candidates_all.parquet  (from script 03)
* data/processed/guide_seq_tsai2015.parquet
* data/processed/deepcrispr_k562.parquet
* data/processed/discover_seq_k562.parquet
* config.py with ALPHAGENOME_API_KEY (for Model C features)
* data/experimental_chromatin/*.bigWig   (for Model B features)

Usage
-----
    source venv/Scripts/activate
    python scripts/04_extract_features.py [options]

    --candidates PATH   Candidates parquet (default: data/processed/candidates_all.parquet)
    --bigwig-dir PATH   ENCODE BigWig directory (default: data/experimental_chromatin)
    --ag-cache-dir PATH AlphaGenome cache directory (default: data/features/ag_cache)
    --output-dir PATH   Output directory (default: data/features)
    --no-ag             Skip AlphaGenome features (produce sequence-only + ENCODE)
    --no-encode         Skip ENCODE features (produce sequence-only + AlphaGenome)
    --cell-types LIST   Comma-separated cell types to process (default: K562,HEK293)

Outputs
-------
    data/features/feature_matrix_K562.parquet
    data/features/feature_matrix_HEK293.parquet
    data/features/feature_matrix_combined.parquet   ← used by script 05

Cell-type notes
---------------
* K562 (EFO:0002067): full AlphaGenome tracks + all 5 ENCODE assays
* HEK293 (EFO:0001182): AlphaGenome has no DNase/ATAC → those features = NaN
  HEK293 ENCODE: missing DNase + H3K27me3 BigWig → those features = NaN
* U2OS: no AlphaGenome tracks; no ENCODE BigWig data downloaded.
  U2OS rows (from GUIDE-seq) are NOT included by default.  They are useful
  only for Model A (sequence-only).  To include them, add U2OS to --cell-types
  and adjust positives loading logic.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import logging
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

# --- path setup so src imports work when run as a script ---
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from src.feature_matrix import build_feature_matrix
from src.utils import get_logger

logger = get_logger("extract_features")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Paths
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_PROCESSED = _ROOT / "data" / "processed"
_FEATURES = _ROOT / "data" / "features"

_CANDIDATES_DEFAULT = _PROCESSED / "candidates_all.parquet"
_GUIDE_SEQ_PATH = _PROCESSED / "guide_seq_tsai2015.parquet"
_DEEPCRISPR_PATH = _PROCESSED / "deepcrispr_k562.parquet"
_DISCOVER_SEQ_PATH = _PROCESSED / "discover_seq_k562.parquet"
_BIGWIG_DIR_DEFAULT = _ROOT / "data" / "experimental_chromatin"
_AG_CACHE_DEFAULT = _FEATURES / "ag_cache"

# AlphaGenome ontology terms
_ONTOLOGY: dict[str, str] = {
    "K562": "EFO:0002067",
    "HEK293": "EFO:0001182",
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _load_positives(
    guide_seq_path: Path,
    deepcrispr_path: Path,
    discover_seq_path: Path,
) -> dict[str, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Load and split positives by cell type.

    Returns a dict: cell_type → positives DataFrame with columns
    (guide_id, chrom, start) — only the columns required by label_candidates.

    K562 positives: DeepCRISPR K562 + DISCOVER-seq K562.
    HEK293 positives: GUIDE-seq rows where cell_type == "HEK293".
    """
    positives_by_ct: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}

    # --- GUIDE-seq → split by cell_type ---
    if guide_seq_path.exists():
        gs = pd.read_parquet(guide_seq_path)
        hek_pos = gs[gs["cell_type"] == "HEK293"][["guide_id", "chrom", "start"]].copy()
        if len(hek_pos) > 0:
            positives_by_ct["HEK293"] = hek_pos
            logger.info("HEK293 positives from GUIDE-seq: %d sites", len(hek_pos))
        else:
            logger.warning("No HEK293 rows found in GUIDE-seq parquet.")
    else:
        logger.warning("GUIDE-seq parquet not found: %s", guide_seq_path)

    # --- DeepCRISPR K562 ---
    k562_frames: list[pd.DataFrame] = []
    if deepcrispr_path.exists():
        dc = pd.read_parquet(deepcrispr_path)[["guide_id", "chrom", "start"]]
        k562_frames.append(dc)
        logger.info("K562 positives from DeepCRISPR: %d sites", len(dc))
    else:
        logger.warning("DeepCRISPR parquet not found: %s", deepcrispr_path)

    # --- DISCOVER-seq K562 ---
    if discover_seq_path.exists():
        ds = pd.read_parquet(discover_seq_path)[["guide_id", "chrom", "start"]]
        k562_frames.append(ds)
        logger.info("K562 positives from DISCOVER-seq: %d sites", len(ds))
    else:
        logger.warning("DISCOVER-seq parquet not found: %s", discover_seq_path)

    if k562_frames:
        k562_pos = pd.concat(k562_frames, ignore_index=True).drop_duplicates()
        positives_by_ct["K562"] = k562_pos
        logger.info("K562 positives total: %d sites", len(k562_pos))
    else:
        logger.warning("No K562 positives loaded.")

    return positives_by_ct


def _build_guide_dataset_map(
    guide_seq_path: Path,
    deepcrispr_path: Path,
    discover_seq_path: Path,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, str]]:
    """Build per-cell-type guide_id → dataset_name mappings.

    Returns a nested dict: cell_type → {guide_id: dataset_name}.
    Used to set the 'dataset' column on candidates before feature extraction
    so that model.py's exclude_datasets parameter works correctly.
    """
    mapping: dict[str, dict[str, str]] = {"K562": {}, "HEK293": {}}

    if guide_seq_path.exists():
        gs = pd.read_parquet(guide_seq_path, columns=["guide_id", "cell_type"])
        for gid in gs[gs["cell_type"] == "HEK293"]["guide_id"].unique():
            mapping["HEK293"][gid] = "guide_seq_tsai2015"

    if deepcrispr_path.exists():
        dc = pd.read_parquet(deepcrispr_path, columns=["guide_id"])
        for gid in dc["guide_id"].unique():
            mapping["K562"][gid] = "deepcrispr_k562"

    if discover_seq_path.exists():
        ds = pd.read_parquet(discover_seq_path, columns=["guide_id"])
        for gid in ds["guide_id"].unique():
            # DISCOVER-seq takes priority over DeepCRISPR if guide appears in both
            mapping["K562"][gid] = "discover_seq_k562"

    return mapping


def _load_ag_model():
    """Load the AlphaGenome DnaClient from config.ALPHAGENOME_API_KEY."""
    try:
        import config as cfg  # type: ignore
    except ImportError:
        logger.error(
            "config.py not found.  Create config.py with ALPHAGENOME_API_KEY = '...'."
        )
        return None

    api_key = getattr(cfg, "ALPHAGENOME_API_KEY", None)
    if not api_key:
        logger.error("ALPHAGENOME_API_KEY not set in config.py.")
        return None

    try:
        import src.alphagenome_client as ag_mod  # type: ignore
        model = ag_mod.create_client(api_key)
        logger.info("AlphaGenome client created.")
        return model
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("Failed to create AlphaGenome client: %s", exc)
        return None


def _load_encode_manifest(bigwig_dir: Path):
    """Build ENCODE BigWig manifest from the given directory."""
    import src.encode_client as enc_mod  # type: ignore

    if not bigwig_dir.exists():
        logger.warning("BigWig directory not found: %s", bigwig_dir)
        return None

    manifest = enc_mod.build_manifest(bigwig_dir)
    available = list(manifest.files.keys()) if hasattr(manifest, "files") else []
    logger.info("ENCODE manifest built.  Available cell-type+assay keys: %s", available)
    return manifest


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Extract sequence + chromatin features → per-cell-type feature matrices"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--candidates", type=Path, default=_CANDIDATES_DEFAULT,
        help="Candidates parquet from script 03 (default: data/processed/candidates_all.parquet)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--bigwig-dir", type=Path, default=_BIGWIG_DIR_DEFAULT,
        help="ENCODE BigWig directory (default: data/experimental_chromatin)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--ag-cache-dir", type=Path, default=_AG_CACHE_DEFAULT,
        help="AlphaGenome cache directory (default: data/features/ag_cache)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-dir", type=Path, default=_FEATURES,
        help="Output directory for feature matrix parquets (default: data/features)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-ag", action="store_true",
        help="Skip AlphaGenome features (sequence + ENCODE only)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-encode", action="store_true",
        help="Skip ENCODE features (sequence + AlphaGenome only)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--cell-types", type=str, default="K562,HEK293",
        help="Comma-separated cell types to process (default: K562,HEK293)",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    cell_types = [ct.strip() for ct in args.cell_types.split(",")]
    logger.info("Processing cell types: %s", cell_types)

    # ---- Validate candidates -----------------------------------------------
    if not args.candidates.exists():
        logger.error(
            "Candidates parquet not found: %s\n"
            "Run script 03 first: python scripts/03_generate_candidates.py",
            args.candidates,
        )
        sys.exit(1)

    candidates = pd.read_parquet(args.candidates)
    logger.info("Loaded %d candidate sites for %d guides", len(candidates),
                candidates["guide_id"].nunique())

    # ---- Load positives and dataset mappings --------------------------------
    positives_by_ct = _load_positives(
        _GUIDE_SEQ_PATH, _DEEPCRISPR_PATH, _DISCOVER_SEQ_PATH
    )
    guide_dataset_map = _build_guide_dataset_map(
        _GUIDE_SEQ_PATH, _DEEPCRISPR_PATH, _DISCOVER_SEQ_PATH
    )

    # ---- Load chromatin data sources ----------------------------------------
    ag_model = None
    if not args.no_ag:
        ag_model = _load_ag_model()
        if ag_model is None:
            logger.warning(
                "AlphaGenome client unavailable.  "
                "Feature matrices will lack Model C (AG) features.  "
                "Pass --no-ag to suppress this warning."
            )
    else:
        logger.info("Skipping AlphaGenome features (--no-ag)")

    encode_manifest = None
    if not args.no_encode:
        encode_manifest = _load_encode_manifest(args.bigwig_dir)
        if encode_manifest is None:
            logger.warning(
                "ENCODE manifest unavailable.  "
                "Feature matrices will lack Model B (ENCODE) features."
            )
    else:
        logger.info("Skipping ENCODE features (--no-encode)")

    args.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.ag_cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ---- Build per-cell-type feature matrices --------------------------------
    feature_matrix_paths: list[Path] = []

    for cell_type in cell_types:
        logger.info("=" * 60)
        logger.info("Building feature matrix for cell_type=%s", cell_type)
        logger.info("=" * 60)

        if cell_type not in positives_by_ct:
            logger.error(
                "No positives loaded for cell_type=%s — skipping.  "
                "Check that processed parquets exist.",
                cell_type,
            )
            continue

        positives = positives_by_ct[cell_type]

        # Filter candidates to guides that have positives in this cell type
        valid_guide_ids = set(positives["guide_id"].unique())
        ct_candidates = candidates[candidates["guide_id"].isin(valid_guide_ids)].copy()
        logger.info(
            "Filtered to %d guides with %s positives: %d candidates",
            len(valid_guide_ids), cell_type, len(ct_candidates),
        )

        if ct_candidates.empty:
            logger.error("No candidates for %s after guide_id filter.", cell_type)
            continue

        # Assign dataset column based on which source dataset each guide is from
        ct_guide_dataset = guide_dataset_map.get(cell_type, {})
        ct_candidates["dataset"] = (
            ct_candidates["guide_id"]
            .map(ct_guide_dataset)
            .fillna("other")
        )

        # Select AlphaGenome ontology term for this cell type
        ag_ontology = _ONTOLOGY.get(cell_type) if ag_model is not None else None

        out_path = args.output_dir / f"feature_matrix_{cell_type}.parquet"

        fm = build_feature_matrix(
            ct_candidates,
            positives,
            cell_type=cell_type,
            encode_manifest=encode_manifest,
            ag_model=ag_model,
            ag_ontology_term=ag_ontology,
            ag_cache_dir=args.ag_cache_dir,
            output_path=out_path,
        )

        n_pos = int(fm["label"].sum()) if "label" in fm.columns else 0
        logger.info(
            "Cell type %s complete: %d rows, %d positive (%.1f%%)",
            cell_type, len(fm), n_pos,
            100.0 * n_pos / len(fm) if len(fm) else 0.0,
        )
        feature_matrix_paths.append(out_path)

    # ---- Combine all cell-type matrices into one file -----------------------
    if not feature_matrix_paths:
        logger.error("No feature matrices were built — check errors above.")
        sys.exit(1)

    logger.info("Concatenating %d cell-type matrices → combined", len(feature_matrix_paths))
    combined_frames = [pd.read_parquet(p) for p in feature_matrix_paths]
    combined = pd.concat(combined_frames, ignore_index=True)

    combined_path = args.output_dir / "feature_matrix_combined.parquet"
    combined.to_parquet(combined_path, index=False)

    n_pos_total = int(combined["label"].sum()) if "label" in combined.columns else 0
    logger.info(
        "Combined feature matrix: %d rows × %d columns | "
        "%d positive (%.1f%%) | %d negative | saved → %s",
        len(combined), len(combined.columns),
        n_pos_total,
        100.0 * n_pos_total / len(combined) if len(combined) else 0.0,
        len(combined) - n_pos_total,
        combined_path,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== scripts/05_train_models.py ===
"""
scripts/05_train_models.py — Train and evaluate the three-model comparison.

Loads the combined feature matrix from script 04 and runs full
leave-one-GUIDE-out (LOGO) cross-validation for all three model types:

* Model A — sequence-only baseline (all cell types)
* Model B — sequence + ENCODE experimental chromatin (K562 + HEK293)
* Model C — sequence + AlphaGenome predicted chromatin (K562 + HEK293)

n_splits is set to the number of unique guides (LOGO CV): each fold holds
out exactly one guide.  Model A uses ~23 folds; Models B/C auto-reduce to
~14 folds (guides present in K562 + HEK293).

Saves the trained Model C for use in the cell-type transfer demo (script 06).

Requires
--------
* data/features/feature_matrix_combined.parquet  (from script 04)

Outputs
-------
    data/models/model_c.pkl              Trained Model C (XGBClassifier + feature_cols)
    data/models/cv_results.pkl           Full CVResult objects for A, B, C
    data/models/metrics_summary.csv      Per-fold and mean metrics for all models
    data/models/ablation_results.csv     Feature ablation (seq+DNase/ATAC, +histone, +CTCF, full)
    data/results/per_guide_auprc.csv     Per-guide AUPRC for Models A, B, C
    data/results/statistical_tests.csv   Paired t-test + Wilcoxon for A-C, B-C, A-B
    figures/fig2_pr_curves.png           PR curve comparison figure

Critical rules enforced
-----------------------
* Leave-one-GUIDE-out CV (StratifiedGroupKFold by guide_id)
* Primary metric = AUPRC, not ROC-AUC
* scale_pos_weight = n_neg/n_pos for class imbalance
* random_state=42 everywhere for reproducibility
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import logging
import pickle
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

# --- path setup so src imports work when run as a script ---
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from src.model import (
    CVResult,
    ModelComparison,
    compare_models,
    prepare_model_data,
    run_ablation,
    run_three_model_comparison,
)
from src.utils import get_logger

logger = get_logger("train_models")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Paths
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_FEATURES = _ROOT / "data" / "features"
_MODELS = _ROOT / "data" / "models"
_RESULTS = _ROOT / "data" / "results"
_FIGURES = _ROOT / "figures"

_FEATURE_MATRIX_DEFAULT = _FEATURES / "feature_matrix_combined.parquet"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _save_metrics_csv(cv_results: dict[str, CVResult], output_path: Path) -> None:
    """Export per-fold and mean metrics for all models to CSV."""
    rows: list[dict] = []

    for model_type, result in cv_results.items():
        for fold in result.folds:
            guide_str = ",".join(fold.test_guide_ids) if fold.test_guide_ids else ""
            rows.append({
                "model_type": model_type,
                "row_type": "fold",
                "fold": fold.fold,
                "guide_id": guide_str,
                "n_train": fold.n_train,
                "n_test": fold.n_test,
                "n_pos_test": fold.n_pos_test,
                "auprc": fold.auprc,
                "auroc": fold.auroc,
                "precision_at_top": fold.precision_at_top,
                "recall_at_top": fold.recall_at_top,
                "enrichment_at_top": fold.enrichment_at_top,
            })
        rows.append({
            "model_type": model_type,
            "row_type": "mean",
            "fold": "mean",
            "guide_id": "",
            "n_train": None,
            "n_test": None,
            "n_pos_test": None,
            "auprc": result.mean_auprc,
            "auroc": result.mean_auroc,
            "precision_at_top": None,
            "recall_at_top": None,
            "enrichment_at_top": None,
        })
        rows.append({
            "model_type": model_type,
            "row_type": "std",
            "fold": "std",
            "guide_id": "",
            "n_train": None,
            "n_test": None,
            "n_pos_test": None,
            "auprc": result.std_auprc,
            "auroc": result.std_auroc,
            "precision_at_top": None,
            "recall_at_top": None,
            "enrichment_at_top": None,
        })

    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)
    logger.info("Metrics saved → %s", output_path)


def _build_per_guide_table(cv_results: dict[str, CVResult]) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Build per-guide AUPRC table from LOGO CV results.

    For each guide, extracts AUPRC from the fold that held it out.
    Includes summary rows for median and IQR (25th/75th percentile).
    """
    # Gather guide → (auprc, n_sites, n_pos) per model
    model_maps: dict[str, dict[str, dict]] = {}
    for model_type, result in cv_results.items():
        guide_map: dict[str, dict] = {}
        for fm in result.folds:
            for gid in fm.test_guide_ids:
                guide_map[gid] = {
                    "n_sites": fm.n_test,
                    "n_positives": fm.n_pos_test,
                    "auprc": fm.auprc,
                }
        model_maps[model_type] = guide_map

    # Collect all guide IDs across all models
    all_guides = sorted(
        set().union(*(m.keys() for m in model_maps.values()))
    )

    rows: list[dict] = []
    for gid in all_guides:
        row: dict = {"guide_id": gid}
        # Use the first model that has this guide for n_sites / n_positives
        for mt in cv_results:
            if gid in model_maps[mt]:
                row["n_sites"] = model_maps[mt][gid]["n_sites"]
                row["n_positives"] = model_maps[mt][gid]["n_positives"]
                break
        for mt in cv_results:
            val = model_maps[mt].get(gid, {}).get("auprc", float("nan"))
            row[f"auprc_{mt}"] = val
        rows.append(row)

    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)

    # Summary rows
    for label, func in [("median", np.nanmedian), ("IQR_25", lambda x: np.nanpercentile(x, 25)),
                         ("IQR_75", lambda x: np.nanpercentile(x, 75))]:
        summary: dict = {"guide_id": label, "n_sites": None, "n_positives": None}
        for mt in cv_results:
            col = f"auprc_{mt}"
            vals = df[col].dropna().values
            summary[col] = float(func(vals)) if len(vals) > 0 else float("nan")
        df = pd.concat([df, pd.DataFrame([summary])], ignore_index=True)

    return df


def _save_statistical_tests(
    cv_results: dict[str, CVResult],
    output_path: Path,
) -> list[ModelComparison]:
    """Compute and save all pairwise model comparisons."""
    comparisons = [
        ("A", "C"),
        ("B", "C"),
        ("A", "B"),
    ]
    results: list[ModelComparison] = []
    rows: list[dict] = []

    for a_key, b_key in comparisons:
        if a_key not in cv_results or b_key not in cv_results:
            continue
        comp = compare_models(cv_results[a_key], cv_results[b_key])
        results.append(comp)
        rows.append({
            "comparison": f"{a_key}_vs_{b_key}",
            "delta_auprc": comp.delta_auprc,
            "n_paired": comp.n_paired,
            "ttest_p_two_sided": comp.p_value,
            "ttest_p_one_sided": comp.ttest_p_one_sided,
            "wilcoxon_p_two_sided": comp.wilcoxon_p_two_sided,
            "wilcoxon_p_one_sided": comp.wilcoxon_p_one_sided,
            "significant": comp.significant,
        })

    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)
    logger.info("Statistical tests saved → %s", output_path)
    return results


def _run_ablation_analysis(
    feature_matrix: pd.DataFrame,
    n_splits: int,
    *,
    include_missingness: bool,
    exclude_datasets: list[str] | None,
    output_path: Path,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
    """Run ablation with the four requested feature subsets for Model C."""
    from src.feature_matrix import AG_FEATURE_NAMES, SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES

    # Prepare Model C data
    X_c, y_c, groups_c, feat_c = prepare_model_data(
        feature_matrix, "C",
        cell_types=["K562", "HEK293"],
        exclude_datasets=exclude_datasets,
        include_missingness_indicators=include_missingness,
    )

    seq_feats = list(SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES)

    # DNase/ATAC/accessibility AG features + their _missing indicators
    dnase_atac_ag = [f for f in AG_FEATURE_NAMES
                     if any(k in f for k in ("dnase", "atac", "accessibility"))]
    dnase_atac_missing = [f + "_missing" for f in dnase_atac_ag]

    # Histone AG features
    histone_ag = [f for f in AG_FEATURE_NAMES
                  if any(k in f for k in ("h3k4me3", "h3k27me3", "h3k9me3", "h3k27ac"))]
    histone_missing = [f + "_missing" for f in histone_ag]

    # CTCF AG features
    ctcf_ag = [f for f in AG_FEATURE_NAMES if "ctcf" in f]
    ctcf_missing = [f + "_missing" for f in ctcf_ag]

    subsets: dict[str, list[str]] = {
        "seq + DNase/ATAC": seq_feats + dnase_atac_ag + (dnase_atac_missing if include_missingness else []),
        "seq + histone": seq_feats + histone_ag + (histone_missing if include_missingness else []),
        "seq + CTCF": seq_feats + ctcf_ag + (ctcf_missing if include_missingness else []),
        "full Model C": list(feat_c),
    }

    logger.info("Running ablation with %d subsets, n_splits=%d ...", len(subsets), n_splits)
    ablation_results = run_ablation(
        X_c, y_c, groups_c, feat_c,
        feature_subsets=subsets,
        n_splits=n_splits,
        random_state=42,
    )

    # Save
    rows = []
    for name, metrics in ablation_results.items():
        n_feats = len([c for c in subsets[name] if c in feat_c])
        rows.append({"subset": name, "n_features": n_feats, **metrics})
    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    df.to_csv(output_path, index=False)
    logger.info("Ablation results saved → %s", output_path)
    return ablation_results


def _print_summary(
    cv_results: dict[str, CVResult],
    comparisons: list[ModelComparison],
    ablation: dict[str, dict[str, float]] | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Print comprehensive results to stdout."""
    n_folds = {mt: len(r.folds) for mt, r in cv_results.items()}

    print("\n" + "=" * 72)
    print("SafeGenome — Leave-One-Guide-Out CV Results")
    print("=" * 72)
    print(f"{'Model':<12} {'Folds':>6} {'Mean AUPRC':>12} {'Std AUPRC':>12} {'Mean AUROC':>12}")
    print("-" * 72)
    for model_type, result in cv_results.items():
        print(
            f"Model {model_type:<6} "
            f"{n_folds[model_type]:>6} "
            f"{result.mean_auprc:>12.4f} "
            f"{result.std_auprc:>12.4f} "
            f"{result.mean_auroc:>12.4f}"
        )
    print("=" * 72)

    # Statistical tests
    print("\n" + "=" * 100)
    print("Statistical Comparisons (paired by guide ID)")
    print("=" * 100)
    print(f"{'Pair':<10} {'Delta':>8} {'N':>4} "
          f"{'t 2-sided':>10} {'t 1-sided':>10} "
          f"{'W 2-sided':>10} {'W 1-sided':>10} {'Sig':>5}")
    print("-" * 100)
    for comp in comparisons:
        print(
            f"{comp.model_a_type} vs {comp.model_b_type:<4} "
            f"{comp.delta_auprc:>+8.4f} "
            f"{comp.n_paired:>4} "
            f"{comp.p_value:>10.4f} "
            f"{comp.ttest_p_one_sided:>10.4f} "
            f"{comp.wilcoxon_p_two_sided:>10.4f} "
            f"{comp.wilcoxon_p_one_sided:>10.4f} "
            f"{'*' if comp.significant else '':>5}"
        )
    print("=" * 100)
    print("  t = paired t-test | W = Wilcoxon signed-rank")
    print("  1-sided: H1 = second model > first model")
    print("  * = any two-sided p < 0.05")

    # Ablation
    if ablation:
        print("\n" + "=" * 60)
        print("Feature Ablation (Model C data, LOGO CV)")
        print("=" * 60)
        print(f"{'Subset':<25} {'Mean AUPRC':>12} {'Std AUPRC':>12}")
        print("-" * 60)
        for name, metrics in ablation.items():
            print(f"{name:<25} {metrics['mean_auprc']:>12.4f} {metrics['std_auprc']:>12.4f}")
        print("=" * 60)

    print()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Train three-model comparison and save Model C for cell-type transfer"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--feature-matrix", type=Path, default=_FEATURE_MATRIX_DEFAULT,
        help="Combined feature matrix parquet (default: data/features/feature_matrix_combined.parquet)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-dir", type=Path, default=_MODELS,
        help="Output directory for model artifacts (default: data/models)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--figures-dir", type=Path, default=_FIGURES,
        help="Output directory for figures (default: figures)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--exclude-datasets", type=str, default=None,
        help="Comma-separated dataset names to exclude (e.g. deepcrispr_k562)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-missingness", action="store_true",
        help="Do not include missingness indicator features (_missing columns)",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    # ---- Load feature matrix ------------------------------------------------
    if not args.feature_matrix.exists():
        logger.error(
            "Feature matrix not found: %s\n"
            "Run script 04 first: python scripts/04_extract_features.py",
            args.feature_matrix,
        )
        sys.exit(1)

    logger.info("Loading feature matrix: %s", args.feature_matrix)
    feature_matrix = pd.read_parquet(args.feature_matrix)
    logger.info(
        "Feature matrix: %d rows × %d columns",
        len(feature_matrix), len(feature_matrix.columns),
    )

    if "label" not in feature_matrix.columns:
        logger.error(
            "Feature matrix lacks 'label' column.  "
            "Re-run script 04 — the matrix may be from a partial run."
        )
        sys.exit(1)

    n_pos = int(feature_matrix["label"].sum())
    n_neg = len(feature_matrix) - n_pos
    logger.info(
        "Class balance: %d positive (%.1f%%), %d negative",
        n_pos, 100.0 * n_pos / len(feature_matrix) if len(feature_matrix) else 0.0, n_neg,
    )

    # ---- LOGO CV: n_splits = number of unique guides ------------------------
    n_unique_guides = feature_matrix["guide_id"].nunique()
    n_splits = n_unique_guides
    logger.info(
        "LOGO CV: %d unique guides total → n_splits=%d (B/C will auto-reduce "
        "to the subset of guides present in K562+HEK293)",
        n_unique_guides, n_splits,
    )

    # Parse exclude_datasets
    exclude_datasets: list[str] | None = None
    if args.exclude_datasets:
        exclude_datasets = [d.strip() for d in args.exclude_datasets.split(",")]
        logger.info("Excluding datasets: %s", exclude_datasets)

    include_missingness = not args.no_missingness

    # ---- Create output directories ------------------------------------------
    args.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.figures_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    _RESULTS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ---- Run three-model comparison -----------------------------------------
    logger.info(
        "Starting three-model comparison — LOGO CV (%d folds) ...",
        n_splits,
    )
    logger.info("Missingness indicators: %s", include_missingness)

    cv_results = run_three_model_comparison(
        feature_matrix,
        n_splits=n_splits,
        exclude_datasets=exclude_datasets,
        include_missingness_indicators=include_missingness,
        output_dir=args.output_dir,
        run_shap=False,
        run_ablation_flag=False,  # we run ablation ourselves below
    )

    # ---- Statistical tests (t-test + Wilcoxon, A-C / B-C / A-B) ------------
    comparisons = _save_statistical_tests(
        cv_results,
        _RESULTS / "statistical_tests.csv",
    )

    # ---- Ablation analysis --------------------------------------------------
    ablation = _run_ablation_analysis(
        feature_matrix,
        n_splits=n_splits,
        include_missingness=include_missingness,
        exclude_datasets=exclude_datasets,
        output_path=args.output_dir / "ablation_results.csv",
    )

    # ---- Print summary -------------------------------------------------------
    _print_summary(cv_results, comparisons, ablation)

    # ---- Save metrics CSV ---------------------------------------------------
    metrics_path = args.output_dir / "metrics_summary.csv"
    _save_metrics_csv(cv_results, metrics_path)

    # ---- Per-guide AUPRC table -----------------------------------------------
    per_guide_df = _build_per_guide_table(cv_results)
    per_guide_path = _RESULTS / "per_guide_auprc.csv"
    per_guide_df.to_csv(per_guide_path, index=False)
    logger.info("Per-guide AUPRC saved → %s", per_guide_path)

    # Print it
    print("\n" + "=" * 85)
    print("Per-Guide AUPRC (LOGO CV)")
    print("=" * 85)
    print(per_guide_df.to_string(index=False, float_format="%.4f"))
    print("=" * 85)

    # ---- Save PR curve figure -----------------------------------------------
    try:
        from src.model import plot_pr_curves
        import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

        fig = plot_pr_curves(
            cv_results,
            output_path=args.figures_dir / "fig2_pr_curves.png",
            title="SafeGenome: Model A vs B vs C — Precision-Recall Curves (LOGO CV)",
        )
        plt.close(fig)
        logger.info("PR curve figure saved → %s", args.figures_dir / "fig2_pr_curves.png")
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("Could not save PR curve figure: %s", exc)

    # ---- Save all CVResult objects (for analysis notebooks) -----------------
    cv_results_path = args.output_dir / "cv_results.pkl"
    with open(cv_results_path, "wb") as f:
        pickle.dump(cv_results, f)
    logger.info("Full CVResult objects saved → %s", cv_results_path)

    # ---- Save Model C for cell-type transfer (script 06) --------------------
    if "C" not in cv_results:
        logger.error("Model C was not trained — check that AlphaGenome features are present.")
        sys.exit(1)

    result_c = cv_results["C"]
    model_c_artifact = {
        "model": result_c.final_model,
        "feature_cols": result_c.feature_cols,
        "model_type": "C",
        "mean_auprc": result_c.mean_auprc,
        "std_auprc": result_c.std_auprc,
    }

    model_c_path = args.output_dir / "model_c.pkl"
    with open(model_c_path, "wb") as f:
        pickle.dump(model_c_artifact, f)
    logger.info(
        "Model C (AUPRC=%.4f±%.4f) saved → %s",
        result_c.mean_auprc, result_c.std_auprc, model_c_path,
    )

    logger.info("Script 05 complete.  Next: python scripts/06_cell_type_transfer.py")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== scripts/06_cell_type_transfer.py ===
"""
scripts/06_cell_type_transfer.py — Cross-cell-type CRISPR off-target risk scoring.

Loads the trained Model C from script 05 and applies it to score candidate
off-target sites across multiple cell types using AlphaGenome predicted
chromatin.  This is the core demonstration of SafeGenome's key capability:
predicting cell-type-specific off-target risk WITHOUT experimental chromatin data.

Requires
--------
* data/models/model_c.pkl              (from script 05)
* data/processed/candidates_all.parquet (from script 03)
* config.py with ALPHAGENOME_API_KEY

Usage
-----
    source venv/Scripts/activate
    python scripts/06_cell_type_transfer.py [options]

    --model PATH            Model C pickle (default: data/models/model_c.pkl)
    --candidates PATH       Candidates parquet (default: data/processed/candidates_all.parquet)
    --ag-cache-dir PATH     AlphaGenome cache (default: data/features/ag_cache)
    --output-dir PATH       Output directory (default: data/results/cell_type_transfer)
    --cell-types LIST       Comma-separated "Name:OntologyTerm" pairs
                            (default: K562:EFO:0002067,HEK293:EFO:0001182,
                                      CD4_T_cell:CL:0000624,Hepatocyte:CL:0000182)
    --guide-ids LIST        Comma-separated guide_ids to score (default: all)
    --top-n-heatmap N       Number of sites to show in heatmap (default: 50)
    --validate              Also compute AUPRC against K562/HEK293 positives

Outputs (written to --output-dir)
----------------------------------
    cell_type_transfer_scores.csv       Risk scores for all sites × cell types
    cell_type_score_matrix.csv          Wide matrix: sites × cell types
    cell_type_rank_correlation.csv      Spearman rank correlation matrix
    transfer_validation.csv             AUPRC per cell type (if --validate)
    figures/fig3_cell_type_heatmap.png
    figures/cell_type_rank_correlation_heatmap.png

Biological interpretation
--------------------------
The score matrix and heatmap show how off-target cleavage risk varies by
cell type.  Sites with uniformly high risk across all cell types should be
flagged regardless of clinical application.  Sites with high risk only in
specific cell types (e.g. K562 but not T cells) warrant cell-type-specific
safety evaluation.

The rank correlation heatmap shows how similar the risk rankings are across
cell types.  High correlation indicates the guide's off-target landscape is
similar across cell types (likely driven by sequence accessibility shared by
most cell types).  Low correlation indicates cell-type-specific chromatin
drives differential risk.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import logging
import pickle
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

# --- path setup so src imports work when run as a script ---
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from src.cell_type_transfer import RECOMMENDED_CELL_TYPES, run_cell_type_transfer
from src.utils import get_logger

logger = get_logger("cell_type_transfer")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Paths
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_MODELS = _ROOT / "data" / "models"
_PROCESSED = _ROOT / "data" / "processed"
_FEATURES = _ROOT / "data" / "features"
_RESULTS = _ROOT / "data" / "results" / "cell_type_transfer"

_MODEL_C_DEFAULT = _MODELS / "model_c.pkl"
_CANDIDATES_DEFAULT = _PROCESSED / "candidates_all.parquet"
_AG_CACHE_DEFAULT = _FEATURES / "ag_cache"

# Default cell types for transfer demo (all have AlphaGenome tracks)
_DEFAULT_CELL_TYPE_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    "K562": "EFO:0002067",         # Chronic myelogenous leukemia (full tracks)
    "HEK293": "EFO:0001182",       # Embryonic kidney (no DNase/ATAC tracks)
    "CD4_T_cell": "CL:0000624",    # CD4+ T cell (relevant for therapeutic CRISPR)
    "Hepatocyte": "CL:0000182",    # Hepatocyte (liver; relevant for in vivo delivery)
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _parse_cell_type_arg(cell_type_str: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Parse 'Name:OntologyTerm,...' argument into a dict.

    Handles both formats:
        K562:EFO:0002067          (ontology with colon in term)
        K562:EFO_0002067          (underscore separator)
    Strategy: split on first colon → name, then rest → ontology term.
    """
    result: dict[str, str] = {}
    for entry in cell_type_str.split(","):
        entry = entry.strip()
        if not entry:
            continue
        # Split on first colon only: "K562:EFO:0002067" → ("K562", "EFO:0002067")
        parts = entry.split(":", maxsplit=1)
        if len(parts) != 2:
            logger.warning("Could not parse cell type entry: %r (expected Name:Term)", entry)
            continue
        name, term = parts[0].strip(), parts[1].strip()
        result[name] = term
    return result


def _load_model_c(path: Path) -> tuple:
    """Load Model C artifact from pickle.

    Returns (model, feature_cols, metadata_dict).
    """
    if not path.exists():
        logger.error(
            "Model C not found: %s\n"
            "Run script 05 first: python scripts/05_train_models.py",
            path,
        )
        sys.exit(1)

    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        artifact = pickle.load(f)

    model = artifact["model"]
    feature_cols = artifact["feature_cols"]
    logger.info(
        "Loaded Model C: %d features, AUPRC=%.4f±%.4f",
        len(feature_cols),
        artifact.get("mean_auprc", float("nan")),
        artifact.get("std_auprc", float("nan")),
    )
    return model, feature_cols, artifact


def _load_positives_for_validation() -> dict[str, pd.DataFrame] | None:
    """Load K562 and HEK293 positives for AUPRC validation.

    Returns None if parquets are not found.
    """
    pos_by_ct: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}

    guide_seq_path = _PROCESSED / "guide_seq_tsai2015.parquet"
    deepcrispr_path = _PROCESSED / "deepcrispr_k562.parquet"
    discover_seq_path = _PROCESSED / "discover_seq_k562.parquet"

    if guide_seq_path.exists():
        gs = pd.read_parquet(guide_seq_path)
        hek_pos = gs[gs["cell_type"] == "HEK293"][["guide_id", "chrom", "start"]].copy()
        if len(hek_pos) > 0:
            pos_by_ct["HEK293"] = hek_pos

    k562_frames = []
    for path in [deepcrispr_path, discover_seq_path]:
        if path.exists():
            k562_frames.append(pd.read_parquet(path)[["guide_id", "chrom", "start"]])
    if k562_frames:
        pos_by_ct["K562"] = pd.concat(k562_frames, ignore_index=True).drop_duplicates()

    return pos_by_ct if pos_by_ct else None


def _load_ag_client():
    """Load AlphaGenome DnaClient from config.py."""
    try:
        import config as cfg  # type: ignore
    except ImportError:
        logger.error(
            "config.py not found.  Create config.py with ALPHAGENOME_API_KEY = '...'."
        )
        sys.exit(1)

    api_key = getattr(cfg, "ALPHAGENOME_API_KEY", None)
    if not api_key:
        logger.error("ALPHAGENOME_API_KEY not set in config.py.")
        sys.exit(1)

    import src.alphagenome_client as ag_mod  # type: ignore
    client = ag_mod.create_client(api_key)
    logger.info("AlphaGenome client created.")
    return client


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Cross-cell-type off-target risk scoring with trained Model C"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--model", type=Path, default=_MODEL_C_DEFAULT,
        help="Model C pickle from script 05 (default: data/models/model_c.pkl)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--candidates", type=Path, default=_CANDIDATES_DEFAULT,
        help="Candidates parquet from script 03 (default: data/processed/candidates_all.parquet)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--ag-cache-dir", type=Path, default=_AG_CACHE_DEFAULT,
        help="AlphaGenome cache directory (default: data/features/ag_cache)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-dir", type=Path, default=_RESULTS,
        help="Output directory (default: data/results/cell_type_transfer)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--cell-types", type=str, default=None,
        help=(
            "Comma-separated 'Name:OntologyTerm' pairs.  "
            "Default: K562:EFO:0002067,HEK293:EFO:0001182,"
            "CD4_T_cell:CL:0000624,Hepatocyte:CL:0000182"
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--guide-ids", type=str, default=None,
        help="Comma-separated guide_ids to score (default: all guides in candidates)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--top-n-heatmap", type=int, default=50,
        help="Number of top sites to display in the heatmap figure (default: 50)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--validate", action="store_true",
        help="Compute AUPRC against K562/HEK293 experimental positives",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    # ---- Parse cell-type map ------------------------------------------------
    if args.cell_types:
        cell_type_map = _parse_cell_type_arg(args.cell_types)
        if not cell_type_map:
            logger.error("No valid cell types parsed from: %r", args.cell_types)
            sys.exit(1)
    else:
        cell_type_map = _DEFAULT_CELL_TYPE_MAP

    logger.info("Cell types for transfer: %s", list(cell_type_map.keys()))

    # ---- Load model ---------------------------------------------------------
    model, feature_cols, _ = _load_model_c(args.model)

    # ---- Load candidates ----------------------------------------------------
    if not args.candidates.exists():
        logger.error(
            "Candidates parquet not found: %s\n"
            "Run script 03 first: python scripts/03_generate_candidates.py",
            args.candidates,
        )
        sys.exit(1)

    candidates = pd.read_parquet(args.candidates)
    logger.info(
        "Loaded %d candidates for %d guides",
        len(candidates), candidates["guide_id"].nunique(),
    )

    # Filter to specified guide_ids if requested
    if args.guide_ids:
        requested_ids = {g.strip() for g in args.guide_ids.split(",")}
        before = len(candidates)
        candidates = candidates[candidates["guide_id"].isin(requested_ids)].copy()
        logger.info(
            "Filtered to guide_ids=%s: %d → %d candidates",
            requested_ids, before, len(candidates),
        )
        if candidates.empty:
            logger.error(
                "No candidates remain after guide_id filter.  "
                "Available guide_ids: %s",
                sorted(pd.read_parquet(args.candidates)["guide_id"].unique()),
            )
            sys.exit(1)

    # ---- Load AlphaGenome client --------------------------------------------
    ag_client = _load_ag_client()

    # ---- Load positives for validation (optional) ---------------------------
    positives_by_ct: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] | None = None
    if args.validate:
        positives_by_ct = _load_positives_for_validation()
        if positives_by_ct:
            logger.info(
                "Validation positives loaded for: %s", list(positives_by_ct.keys())
            )
        else:
            logger.warning("No positives found for validation — skipping AUPRC computation.")

    # ---- Create output directories ------------------------------------------
    args.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.ag_cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # Create figures subdirectory within output_dir
    figures_dir = args.output_dir / "figures"
    figures_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ---- Run cell-type transfer ---------------------------------------------
    logger.info("Starting cell-type transfer analysis ...")
    logger.info(
        "Scoring %d candidates across %d cell types",
        len(candidates), len(cell_type_map),
    )

    results = run_cell_type_transfer(
        candidates=candidates,
        cell_type_map=cell_type_map,
        model=model,
        feature_cols=feature_cols,
        ag_client=ag_client,
        ag_cache_dir=args.ag_cache_dir,
        output_dir=args.output_dir,
        top_n_heatmap=args.top_n_heatmap,
        sort_heatmap_by="cfd_score",
        positives_by_cell_type=positives_by_ct,
    )

    # ---- Summary -----------------------------------------------------------
    transfer_df = results.get("transfer_df")
    score_matrix = results.get("score_matrix")
    rank_corr = results.get("rank_corr")
    validation = results.get("validation", {})

    if score_matrix is not None:
        logger.info(
            "Score matrix: %d sites × %d cell types",
            score_matrix.shape[0], score_matrix.shape[1],
        )

    if rank_corr is not None:
        logger.info("Rank correlation matrix:\n%s", rank_corr.round(3).to_string())

    if validation:
        print("\n" + "=" * 55)
        print("Validation AUPRC (Model C applied to training cell types)")
        print("=" * 55)
        for ct, metrics in validation.items():
            print(
                f"  {ct:<15} AUPRC={metrics.get('auprc', float('nan')):.4f}  "
                f"n_pos={metrics.get('n_positives', 'N/A')}"
            )
        print()
        print("Note: Validation uses sites/positives seen during training.")
        print("This is an in-sample check, not out-of-sample generalisation.")
        print("=" * 55 + "\n")

    logger.info(
        "Cell-type transfer complete.  Results saved → %s", args.output_dir
    )
    logger.info(
        "Key outputs:\n"
        "  %s/cell_type_transfer_scores.csv\n"
        "  %s/cell_type_score_matrix.csv\n"
        "  %s/figures/fig3_cell_type_heatmap.png",
        args.output_dir, args.output_dir, args.output_dir,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== scripts/07_paper_analyses.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Paper analyses: AG vs ENCODE correlation (Validation 1) and SHAP importance.

Outputs:
    data/results/ag_vs_encode_correlation.csv
    figures/fig5_ag_vs_encode_correlation.png
    figures/fig4_feature_importance.png
"""

import logging
import pickle
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import shap

# ── project imports ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))

from src.cell_type_transfer import compute_chromatin_correlation
from src.model import compute_shap_values, prepare_model_data

logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s",
    datefmt="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
)
logger = logging.getLogger("paper_analyses")

# ── paths ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
FEATURE_MATRIX_PATH = ROOT / "data" / "features" / "feature_matrix_combined.parquet"
MODEL_C_PATH = ROOT / "data" / "models" / "model_c.pkl"
RESULTS_DIR = ROOT / "data" / "results"
FIGURES_DIR = ROOT / "figures"

RESULTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
FIGURES_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# ── feature pairs for Validation 1 ──────────────────────────────────────
FEATURE_PAIRS: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = [
    ("ag_dnase_mean", "exp_dnase_mean", "DNase mean"),
    ("ag_dnase_max", "exp_dnase_max", "DNase max"),
    ("ag_dnase_at_site", "exp_dnase_at_site", "DNase at site"),
    ("ag_h3k4me3_mean", "exp_h3k4me3_mean", "H3K4me3"),
    ("ag_h3k27me3_mean", "exp_h3k27me3_mean", "H3K27me3"),
    ("ag_h3k9me3_mean", "exp_h3k9me3_mean", "H3K9me3"),
    ("ag_h3k27ac_mean", "exp_h3k27ac_mean", "H3K27ac"),
    ("ag_accessibility_binary", "exp_accessibility_binary", "Accessibility"),
]


def run_validation_1(fm: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Compute Spearman correlations between AG and ENCODE features at K562 sites."""
    logger.info("=== Validation 1: AlphaGenome vs ENCODE correlation (K562) ===")

    rows = []
    for ag_feat, exp_feat, label in FEATURE_PAIRS:
        result = compute_chromatin_correlation(
            fm, ag_feature=ag_feat, exp_feature=exp_feat, cell_type="K562"
        )
        rows.append(
            {
                "ag_feature": ag_feat,
                "exp_feature": exp_feat,
                "label": label,
                "spearman_r": result["spearman_r"],
                "p_value": result["p_value"],
                "n": result["n"],
            }
        )
        logger.info(
            "  %s: r=%.4f, p=%.2e, n=%d",
            label,
            result["spearman_r"],
            result["p_value"],
            result["n"],
        )

    corr_df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out_path = RESULTS_DIR / "ag_vs_encode_correlation.csv"
    corr_df.to_csv(out_path, index=False)
    logger.info("Saved correlation summary → %s", out_path)
    return corr_df


def plot_scatter_grid(fm: pd.DataFrame, corr_df: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    """Scatter plot grid: one panel per AG vs ENCODE feature pair."""
    k562 = fm[fm["cell_type"] == "K562"].copy()

    n_pairs = len(FEATURE_PAIRS)
    ncols = 4
    nrows = (n_pairs + ncols - 1) // ncols  # ceiling division
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(nrows, ncols, figsize=(4 * ncols, 4 * nrows))
    axes = axes.flatten()

    for idx, (ag_feat, exp_feat, label) in enumerate(FEATURE_PAIRS):
        ax = axes[idx]
        valid = k562[[ag_feat, exp_feat]].dropna()
        x = valid[exp_feat].values
        y = valid[ag_feat].values

        ax.scatter(x, y, alpha=0.15, s=6, color="#2563eb", rasterized=True)

        # Spearman r from precomputed table
        row = corr_df[corr_df["label"] == label].iloc[0]
        r = row["spearman_r"]
        n = int(row["n"])

        ax.set_xlabel(f"ENCODE ({exp_feat.replace('exp_', '')})", fontsize=9)
        ax.set_ylabel(f"AlphaGenome ({ag_feat.replace('ag_', '')})", fontsize=9)
        ax.set_title(f"{label}\nr={r:.3f}, n={n:,}", fontsize=10)
        ax.tick_params(labelsize=8)

    # hide unused axes
    for idx in range(n_pairs, len(axes)):
        axes[idx].set_visible(False)

    fig.suptitle(
        "Validation 1: AlphaGenome vs ENCODE Chromatin Features (K562)",
        fontsize=13,
        fontweight="bold",
        y=1.01,
    )
    fig.tight_layout()
    out_path = FIGURES_DIR / "fig5_ag_vs_encode_correlation.png"
    fig.savefig(out_path, dpi=200, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)
    logger.info("Saved scatter grid → %s", out_path)


def run_shap_analysis(fm: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    """Compute SHAP values for Model C and generate summary plot."""
    logger.info("=== SHAP Feature Importance (Model C) ===")

    # Load Model C
    with open(MODEL_C_PATH, "rb") as f:
        artifact = pickle.load(f)
    model = artifact["model"]
    feature_cols = artifact["feature_cols"]
    logger.info(
        "Loaded Model C: %d features, AUPRC=%.4f±%.4f",
        len(feature_cols),
        artifact["mean_auprc"],
        artifact["std_auprc"],
    )

    # Prepare K562 data with Model C features (including missingness indicators
    # to match training configuration)
    X, y, groups, feat_cols = prepare_model_data(
        fm, "C", cell_types=["K562"], include_missingness_indicators=True
    )
    logger.info("K562 Model C data: %d samples, %d features, %d positives", X.shape[0], X.shape[1], y.sum())

    # Compute SHAP values
    shap_values, importance_df = compute_shap_values(model, X, feat_cols)
    logger.info("SHAP values computed: shape=%s", shap_values.shape)

    # Save importance CSV
    importance_path = RESULTS_DIR / "shap_importance_model_C.csv"
    importance_df.to_csv(importance_path, index=False)
    logger.info("Saved SHAP importance → %s", importance_path)

    # Print top 15
    logger.info("Top 15 features by mean |SHAP|:")
