    for _, row in importance_df.head(15).iterrows():
        logger.info("  %-30s  %.6f", row["feature"], row["mean_abs_shap"])

    # SHAP summary plot (beeswarm)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 8))
    shap.summary_plot(
        shap_values,
        features=X,
        feature_names=feat_cols,
        show=False,
        max_display=20,
        plot_size=None,
    )
    plt.title("SHAP Feature Importance — Model C (K562)", fontsize=13, fontweight="bold")
    plt.tight_layout()
    out_path = FIGURES_DIR / "fig4_feature_importance.png"
    plt.savefig(out_path, dpi=200, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close("all")
    logger.info("Saved SHAP summary plot → %s", out_path)


def main() -> None:
    logger.info("Loading feature matrix from %s", FEATURE_MATRIX_PATH)
    fm = pd.read_parquet(FEATURE_MATRIX_PATH)
    logger.info("Feature matrix: %d rows × %d cols", fm.shape[0], fm.shape[1])

    # Validation 1
    corr_df = run_validation_1(fm)
    plot_scatter_grid(fm, corr_df)

    # SHAP
    run_shap_analysis(fm)

    logger.info("All paper analyses complete.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== scripts/08_benchmarks.py ===
"""
scripts/08_benchmarks.py — External tool benchmarks and held-out validation.

Two analyses:
1. CFD/MIT score baselines: compute AUPRC using raw CFD and MIT scores as
   predictors (no model), add to PR curve alongside Models A, B, C.
2. Held-out validation: train Models A, B, C on GUIDE-seq HEK293 only,
   test on DISCOVER-seq K562 as an independent held-out set.

Requires
--------
* data/features/feature_matrix_combined.parquet  (from script 04)
* data/models/cv_results.pkl                     (from script 05)

Outputs
-------
    figures/fig2_pr_curves.png       Updated PR curve with CFD/MIT baselines
    data/results/baseline_scores.csv CFD + MIT AUPRC results
    data/results/held_out_validation.csv  Held-out DISCOVER-seq results
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import pickle
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score, precision_recall_curve

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from src.feature_matrix import get_model_features
from src.model import (
    compute_metrics,
    compute_scale_pos_weight,
    make_xgb_classifier,
    prepare_model_data,
)
from src.utils import get_logger

logger = get_logger("benchmarks")

_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_FEATURES = _ROOT / "data" / "features"
_MODELS = _ROOT / "data" / "models"
_RESULTS = _ROOT / "data" / "results"
_FIGURES = _ROOT / "figures"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Task 2: CFD / MIT score baselines
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def compute_baseline_auprc(
    feature_matrix: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    cell_types: list[str] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
    """Compute AUPRC for raw CFD and MIT scores as off-target predictors.

    Parameters
    ----------
    feature_matrix : pd.DataFrame
        Must contain ``cfd_score``, ``mit_score``, ``label``, ``cell_type``.
    cell_types : list[str] | None
        If provided, filter to these cell types (to match Model B/C data).

    Returns
    -------
    dict[str, dict[str, float]]
        Mapping from score name to {auprc, n_sites, n_positives}.
    """
    df = feature_matrix.copy()
    if cell_types:
        df = df[df["cell_type"].isin(cell_types)]

    y = df["label"].to_numpy()
    n_pos = int(y.sum())
    logger.info(
        "Baseline scoring: %d sites, %d positives (%.1f%%)",
        len(df), n_pos, 100.0 * n_pos / len(df),
    )

    results: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    for score_name in ("cfd_score", "mit_score"):
        scores = df[score_name].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
        # Handle any NaN by filling with 0 (conservative)
        nan_mask = np.isnan(scores)
        if nan_mask.any():
            logger.warning(
                "%s: %d NaN values filled with 0.0", score_name, nan_mask.sum()
            )
            scores = np.where(nan_mask, 0.0, scores)

        auprc = float(average_precision_score(y, scores))
        metrics = compute_metrics(y, scores)
        results[score_name] = {
            "auprc": auprc,
            "auroc": metrics["auroc"],
            "precision_at_top": metrics["precision_at_top"],
            "recall_at_top": metrics["recall_at_top"],
            "n_sites": len(df),
            "n_positives": n_pos,
        }
        logger.info("  %s: AUPRC=%.4f, AUROC=%.4f", score_name, auprc, metrics["auroc"])

    return results


def plot_pr_curves_with_baselines(
    cv_results: dict,
    feature_matrix: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    cell_types: list[str] | None = None,
    output_path: Path | None = None,
    title: str = "SafeGenome: Precision-Recall Curves (LOGO CV)",
    figsize: tuple[float, float] = (7.5, 6.5),
):
    """Plot PR curves for Models A, B, C + CFD/MIT baselines.

    Parameters
    ----------
    cv_results : dict[str, CVResult]
        From script 05.
    feature_matrix : pd.DataFrame
        Feature matrix with cfd_score, mit_score, label.
    cell_types : list[str] | None
        Filter feature matrix to match Model B/C data for baselines.
    output_path : Path | None
        Where to save the figure.
    title : str
        Figure title.
    figsize : tuple
        Figure size.

    Returns
    -------
    matplotlib.figure.Figure
    """
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=figsize)

    # --- Models A, B, C (pooled OOF) ---
    _COLORS = {"A": "#1f77b4", "B": "#2ca02c", "C": "#ff7f0e"}
    _LINESTYLES = {"A": "-", "B": "-", "C": "-"}
    _LINEWIDTHS = {"A": 1.8, "B": 1.8, "C": 2.5}
    _LABELS = {
        "A": "Model A \u2014 Sequence only",
        "B": "Model B \u2014 Seq + Experimental",
        "C": "Model C \u2014 Seq + AlphaGenome",
    }

    for model_label, result in cv_results.items():
        y_true = result.oof_y_true
        y_score = result.oof_y_score
        if len(y_true) == 0 or int(y_true.sum()) == 0:
            continue
        precision, recall, _ = precision_recall_curve(y_true, y_score)
        auprc = float(average_precision_score(y_true, y_score))
        ax.plot(
            recall, precision,
            color=_COLORS.get(model_label, "gray"),
            linestyle=_LINESTYLES.get(model_label, "-"),
            linewidth=_LINEWIDTHS.get(model_label, 1.8),
            label=f"{_LABELS.get(model_label, model_label)} (AUPRC={auprc:.3f})",
        )

    # --- CFD and MIT baselines ---
    df = feature_matrix.copy()
    if cell_types:
        df = df[df["cell_type"].isin(cell_types)]
    y_all = df["label"].to_numpy()

    _BASELINE_COLORS = {"cfd_score": "#d62728", "mit_score": "#9467bd"}
    _BASELINE_LABELS = {"cfd_score": "CFD score (raw)", "mit_score": "MIT score (raw)"}

    for score_name in ("cfd_score", "mit_score"):
        scores = df[score_name].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
        scores = np.where(np.isnan(scores), 0.0, scores)
        precision, recall, _ = precision_recall_curve(y_all, scores)
        auprc = float(average_precision_score(y_all, scores))
        ax.plot(
            recall, precision,
            color=_BASELINE_COLORS[score_name],
            linestyle="--",
            linewidth=1.5,
            alpha=0.8,
            label=f"{_BASELINE_LABELS[score_name]} (AUPRC={auprc:.3f})",
        )

    # --- Random baseline ---
    if cv_results:
        any_result = next(iter(cv_results.values()))
        baseline = float(any_result.oof_y_true.mean())
        ax.axhline(
            baseline, color="gray", linestyle=":", linewidth=1.0,
            label=f"Random (P={baseline:.3f})",
        )

    ax.set_xlabel("Recall", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_ylabel("Precision", fontsize=12)
    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=13)
    ax.legend(loc="upper right", fontsize=8.5)
    ax.set_xlim([0.0, 1.0])
    ax.set_ylim([0.0, 1.05])
    fig.tight_layout()

    if output_path is not None:
        output_path = Path(output_path)
        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        fig.savefig(output_path, dpi=150, bbox_inches="tight")
        logger.info("PR curve figure saved \u2192 %s", output_path)

    return fig


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Task 3: Held-out DISCOVER-seq validation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def run_held_out_validation(
    feature_matrix: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    train_dataset: str = "guide_seq_tsai2015",
    test_dataset: str = "discover_seq_k562",
    include_missingness: bool = True,
    top_fraction: float = 0.10,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Train on GUIDE-seq, test on DISCOVER-seq K562.

    Parameters
    ----------
    feature_matrix : pd.DataFrame
        Combined feature matrix with ``dataset`` column.
    train_dataset : str
        Dataset name for training data.
    test_dataset : str
        Dataset name for test data.
    include_missingness : bool
        Include missingness indicator features for Models B/C.
    top_fraction : float
        Top fraction for precision/recall metrics.

    Returns
    -------
    pd.DataFrame
        Results table with columns: model, auprc, auroc, precision_at_top,
        recall_at_top, n_train, n_test, n_pos_train, n_pos_test.
    """
    train_df = feature_matrix[feature_matrix["dataset"] == train_dataset].copy()
    test_df = feature_matrix[feature_matrix["dataset"] == test_dataset].copy()

    logger.info(
        "Held-out split: train=%s (%d rows, %d pos), test=%s (%d rows, %d pos)",
        train_dataset, len(train_df), int(train_df["label"].sum()),
        test_dataset, len(test_df), int(test_df["label"].sum()),
    )

    # Report guide composition
    train_guides = sorted(train_df["guide_id"].unique())
    test_guides = sorted(test_df["guide_id"].unique())
    logger.info("  Train guides (%d): %s", len(train_guides), train_guides)
    logger.info("  Test guides (%d): %s", len(test_guides), test_guides)

    # Check for guide overlap (should be zero)
    overlap = set(train_guides) & set(test_guides)
    if overlap:
        logger.warning("Guide overlap between train and test: %s", overlap)
    else:
        logger.info("  No guide overlap between train and test (good)")

    results_rows: list[dict] = []

    for model_type in ("A", "B", "C"):
        feature_cols = get_model_features(
            model_type,
            include_missingness_indicators=include_missingness,
        )
        # Filter to columns that exist
        feature_cols = [c for c in feature_cols if c in feature_matrix.columns]

        if not feature_cols:
            logger.warning("Model %s: no feature columns found, skipping", model_type)
            continue

        X_train = train_df[feature_cols].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)
        y_train = train_df["label"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int32)
        X_test = test_df[feature_cols].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32)
        y_test = test_df["label"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int32)

        n_pos_train = int(y_train.sum())
        n_pos_test = int(y_test.sum())

        if n_pos_train == 0:
            logger.warning("Model %s: no positives in training data, skipping", model_type)
            continue
        if n_pos_test == 0:
            logger.warning("Model %s: no positives in test data, skipping", model_type)
            continue

        spw = compute_scale_pos_weight(y_train)
        clf = make_xgb_classifier(spw, random_state=42)
        clf.fit(X_train, y_train)
        y_score = clf.predict_proba(X_test)[:, 1]

        metrics = compute_metrics(y_test, y_score, top_fraction=top_fraction)
        results_rows.append({
            "model": f"Model {model_type}",
            "auprc": metrics["auprc"],
            "auroc": metrics["auroc"],
            "precision_at_top": metrics["precision_at_top"],
            "recall_at_top": metrics["recall_at_top"],
            "enrichment_at_top": metrics["enrichment_at_top"],
            "n_train": len(y_train),
            "n_test": len(y_test),
            "n_pos_train": n_pos_train,
            "n_pos_test": n_pos_test,
            "n_features": len(feature_cols),
            "train_guides": ",".join(train_guides),
            "test_guides": ",".join(test_guides),
        })
        logger.info(
            "  Model %s: AUPRC=%.4f, AUROC=%.4f, P@10%%=%.4f, R@10%%=%.4f",
            model_type, metrics["auprc"], metrics["auroc"],
            metrics["precision_at_top"], metrics["recall_at_top"],
        )

    # Also add CFD/MIT baselines on the held-out test set
    for score_name in ("cfd_score", "mit_score"):
        scores = test_df[score_name].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)
        scores = np.where(np.isnan(scores), 0.0, scores)
        y_test = test_df["label"].to_numpy(dtype=np.int32)
        n_pos_test = int(y_test.sum())
        metrics = compute_metrics(y_test, scores)
        results_rows.append({
            "model": f"{score_name} (raw)",
            "auprc": metrics["auprc"],
            "auroc": metrics["auroc"],
            "precision_at_top": metrics["precision_at_top"],
            "recall_at_top": metrics["recall_at_top"],
            "enrichment_at_top": metrics["enrichment_at_top"],
            "n_train": 0,
            "n_test": len(y_test),
            "n_pos_train": 0,
            "n_pos_test": n_pos_test,
            "n_features": 1,
            "train_guides": "",
            "test_guides": ",".join(test_guides),
        })

    return pd.DataFrame(results_rows)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def main() -> None:
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    _RESULTS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    _FIGURES.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ---- Load data -----------------------------------------------------------
    fm_path = _FEATURES / "feature_matrix_combined.parquet"
    cv_path = _MODELS / "cv_results.pkl"

    logger.info("Loading feature matrix: %s", fm_path)
    feature_matrix = pd.read_parquet(fm_path)
    logger.info("Feature matrix: %d rows x %d cols", len(feature_matrix), len(feature_matrix.columns))

    logger.info("Loading CV results: %s", cv_path)
    with open(cv_path, "rb") as f:
        cv_results = pickle.load(f)
    logger.info("CV results loaded: models %s", list(cv_results.keys()))

    # ---- Task 2: CFD/MIT baselines -------------------------------------------
    print("\n" + "=" * 72)
    print("Task 2: External Tool Benchmarks (CFD / MIT Score Baselines)")
    print("=" * 72)

    cell_types = ["K562", "HEK293"]
    baseline_results = compute_baseline_auprc(feature_matrix, cell_types=cell_types)

    print(f"\n{'Score':<15} {'AUPRC':>10} {'AUROC':>10} {'P@10%':>10} {'R@10%':>10}")
    print("-" * 60)
    for name, res in baseline_results.items():
        print(
            f"{name:<15} {res['auprc']:>10.4f} {res['auroc']:>10.4f} "
            f"{res['precision_at_top']:>10.4f} {res['recall_at_top']:>10.4f}"
        )
    for mt, cvr in cv_results.items():
        print(f"Model {mt:<9} {cvr.mean_auprc:>10.4f}")
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f"  Evaluated on {baseline_results['cfd_score']['n_sites']} sites, "
          f"{baseline_results['cfd_score']['n_positives']} positives")
    print("  Note: CFD/MIT use ALL data (no train/test split needed)")
    print("  Models A/B/C use pooled out-of-fold predictions from LOGO CV")

    # Save baseline CSV
    baseline_df = pd.DataFrame([
        {"score": name, **res} for name, res in baseline_results.items()
    ])
    baseline_path = _RESULTS / "baseline_scores.csv"
    baseline_df.to_csv(baseline_path, index=False)
    logger.info("Baseline scores saved -> %s", baseline_path)

    # Plot updated PR curves
    fig = plot_pr_curves_with_baselines(
        cv_results,
        feature_matrix,
        cell_types=cell_types,
        output_path=_FIGURES / "fig2_pr_curves.png",
        title="SafeGenome: Precision-Recall Curves (LOGO CV)",
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"\n  Updated PR curve saved -> {_FIGURES / 'fig2_pr_curves.png'}")

    # ---- Task 3: Held-out DISCOVER-seq validation ----------------------------
    print("\n" + "=" * 72)
    print("Task 3: Held-Out Dataset Validation")
    print("     Train: GUIDE-seq HEK293 | Test: DISCOVER-seq K562")
    print("=" * 72)

    held_out_df = run_held_out_validation(
        feature_matrix,
        train_dataset="guide_seq_tsai2015",
        test_dataset="discover_seq_k562",
    )

    print(f"\n{'Model':<20} {'AUPRC':>8} {'AUROC':>8} {'P@10%':>8} {'R@10%':>8} "
          f"{'N_train':>8} {'N_test':>7} {'Pos_test':>9}")
    print("-" * 90)
    for _, row in held_out_df.iterrows():
        print(
            f"{row['model']:<20} {row['auprc']:>8.4f} {row['auroc']:>8.4f} "
            f"{row['precision_at_top']:>8.4f} {row['recall_at_top']:>8.4f} "
            f"{int(row['n_train']):>8} {int(row['n_test']):>7} {int(row['n_pos_test']):>9}"
        )
    print("-" * 90)
    print(f"  Train guides: {held_out_df.iloc[0]['train_guides']}")
    print(f"  Test guides:  {held_out_df.iloc[0]['test_guides']}")
    print("  Note: Small test set — interpret with caution")

    # Save
    held_out_path = _RESULTS / "held_out_validation.csv"
    held_out_df.to_csv(held_out_path, index=False)
    logger.info("Held-out validation saved -> %s", held_out_path)
    print(f"  Saved -> {held_out_path}")

    print("\n" + "=" * 72)
    print("Script 08 complete.")
    print("=" * 72)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== scripts/09_bootstrap_test.py ===
"""
scripts/09_bootstrap_test.py — Paired bootstrap significance test for AUPRC.

Uses pooled out-of-fold predictions from LOGO CV to compare Models A, B, C.
Resamples sites with replacement (10,000 iterations), computes AUPRC on each
resample, and reports p-value as the fraction of resamples where the inferior
model's AUPRC >= the superior model's.

Requires
--------
* data/models/cv_results.pkl  (from script 05)

Outputs
-------
    data/results/bootstrap_test.csv
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pickle
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
from src.utils import get_logger

logger = get_logger("bootstrap_test")

_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
_MODELS = _ROOT / "data" / "models"
_RESULTS = _ROOT / "data" / "results"

N_BOOTSTRAP = 10_000
RANDOM_STATE = 42


def paired_bootstrap_auprc(
    y_true: np.ndarray,
    scores_a: np.ndarray,
    scores_b: np.ndarray,
    *,
    n_bootstrap: int = N_BOOTSTRAP,
    random_state: int = RANDOM_STATE,
) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Paired bootstrap test comparing AUPRC of two score vectors.

    Parameters
    ----------
    y_true : np.ndarray
        Shared ground truth labels (binary).
    scores_a : np.ndarray
        Predicted scores from model A (the inferior / reference model).
    scores_b : np.ndarray
        Predicted scores from model B (the superior / test model).
    n_bootstrap : int
        Number of bootstrap resamples.
    random_state : int
        Seed for reproducibility.

    Returns
    -------
    dict with keys:
        auprc_a : float — observed AUPRC for model A
        auprc_b : float — observed AUPRC for model B
        delta : float — auprc_b - auprc_a (positive = B better)
        p_value : float — fraction of resamples where A's AUPRC >= B's AUPRC
        n_resamples : int
        n_sites : int
    """
    n = len(y_true)
    assert len(scores_a) == n and len(scores_b) == n

    observed_a = float(average_precision_score(y_true, scores_a))
    observed_b = float(average_precision_score(y_true, scores_b))

    rng = np.random.RandomState(random_state)
    count_a_geq_b = 0

    for _ in range(n_bootstrap):
        idx = rng.randint(0, n, size=n)
        y_boot = y_true[idx]

        # Skip degenerate resamples (all one class)
        if y_boot.sum() == 0 or y_boot.sum() == len(y_boot):
            continue

        ap_a = average_precision_score(y_boot, scores_a[idx])
        ap_b = average_precision_score(y_boot, scores_b[idx])

        if ap_a >= ap_b:
            count_a_geq_b += 1

    p_value = count_a_geq_b / n_bootstrap

    return {
        "auprc_a": observed_a,
        "auprc_b": observed_b,
        "delta": observed_b - observed_a,
        "p_value": p_value,
        "n_resamples": n_bootstrap,
        "n_sites": n,
    }


def main() -> None:
    _RESULTS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # Load CV results
    cv_path = _MODELS / "cv_results.pkl"
    logger.info("Loading CV results: %s", cv_path)
    with open(cv_path, "rb") as f:
        cv_results = pickle.load(f)

    # Extract OOF predictions
    oof = {}
    for key in ("A", "B", "C"):
        r = cv_results[key]
        oof[key] = {
            "y_true": r.oof_y_true,
            "y_score": r.oof_y_score,
        }
        logger.info(
            "Model %s OOF: %d sites, %d positives, AUPRC=%.4f",
            key, len(r.oof_y_true), int(r.oof_y_true.sum()),
            float(average_precision_score(r.oof_y_true, r.oof_y_score)),
        )

    # Verify OOF arrays are aligned (same y_true across models)
    assert np.array_equal(oof["A"]["y_true"], oof["B"]["y_true"]), \
        "OOF y_true mismatch between A and B"
    assert np.array_equal(oof["A"]["y_true"], oof["C"]["y_true"]), \
        "OOF y_true mismatch between A and C"
    logger.info("OOF y_true arrays verified identical across all models")

    y_true = oof["A"]["y_true"]

    # Run bootstrap tests
    comparisons = [
        ("A", "C"),  # A is inferior, C is superior
        ("B", "C"),  # B is inferior, C is superior
        ("A", "B"),  # A is inferior, B is superior
    ]

    rows: list[dict] = []
    for ref_key, test_key in comparisons:
        logger.info(
            "Bootstrap test: %s vs %s (%d resamples) ...",
            ref_key, test_key, N_BOOTSTRAP,
        )
        result = paired_bootstrap_auprc(
            y_true,
            oof[ref_key]["y_score"],
            oof[test_key]["y_score"],
            n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP,
            random_state=RANDOM_STATE,
        )
        rows.append({
            "comparison": f"{ref_key}_vs_{test_key}",
            "auprc_inferior": result["auprc_a"],
            "auprc_superior": result["auprc_b"],
            "delta_auprc": result["delta"],
            "bootstrap_p": result["p_value"],
            "n_resamples": result["n_resamples"],
            "n_sites": result["n_sites"],
            "significant_005": result["p_value"] < 0.05,
        })
        logger.info(
            "  %s vs %s: delta=%.4f, p=%.4f (%s)",
            ref_key, test_key, result["delta"], result["p_value"],
            "significant" if result["p_value"] < 0.05 else "not significant",
        )

    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    out_path = _RESULTS / "bootstrap_test.csv"
    df.to_csv(out_path, index=False)
    logger.info("Results saved -> %s", out_path)

    # Print summary
    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("Paired Bootstrap Significance Test (AUPRC, 10,000 resamples, seed=42)")
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f"  Sites: {len(y_true)}, Positives: {int(y_true.sum())} ({100*y_true.mean():.1f}%)")
    print()
    print(f"{'Comparison':<12} {'AUPRC_ref':>10} {'AUPRC_test':>11} {'Delta':>8} {'p-value':>10} {'Sig':>5}")
    print("-" * 62)
    for _, row in df.iterrows():
        sig = "*" if row["significant_005"] else ""
        print(
            f"{row['comparison']:<12} "
            f"{row['auprc_inferior']:>10.4f} "
            f"{row['auprc_superior']:>11.4f} "
            f"{row['delta_auprc']:>+8.4f} "
            f"{row['bootstrap_p']:>10.4f} "
            f"{sig:>5}"
        )
    print("-" * 62)
    print("  p = fraction of resamples where inferior model AUPRC >= superior")
    print("  * = p < 0.05")
    print("=" * 80)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== scripts/translate_cfd_matrix.py ===
"""
translate_cfd_matrix.py - translate Doench 2016 CFD mismatch scores
into SafeGenome internal format and print new _CFD_MM_SCORES.

Conventions confirmed by user test case (rG:dA pos6 = 0.67):
  - dX in reference = TEMPLATE strand base (Watson-Crick pairing with guide RNA)
  - Position 1 = PAM-proximal  =>  idx = ref_pos - 1
  - KEY_MAP: template-strand keys -> protospacer-strand keys (our internal format)
"""

# Reference data: Doench 2016 / GuideMaker (template-strand / rU notation, PAM-proximal pos 1)
REF = {

    "rG:dG,16": 0.0, "rG:dG,17": 0.235294118, "rG:dG,18": 0.476190476,

    "rG:dT,1": 0.9, "rG:dT,2": 0.846153846, "rG:dT,3": 0.75,
    "rG:dT,4": 0.9, "rG:dT,5": 0.866666667, "rG:dT,6": 1.0,
    "rG:dT,7": 1.0, "rG:dT,8": 1.0, "rG:dT,9": 0.642857143,
    "rG:dT,10": 0.933333333, "rG:dT,11": 1.0, "rG:dT,12": 0.933333333,
    "rG:dT,13": 0.923076923, "rG:dT,14": 0.75, "rG:dT,15": 0.941176471,
    "rG:dT,16": 1.0, "rG:dT,17": 0.933333333, "rG:dT,18": 0.692307692,
    "rG:dT,19": 0.714285714, "rG:dT,20": 0.9375,
}

# KEY_MAP: reference (template-strand dX) -> internal (protospacer-strand dX)
# template A <-> protospacer T, template C <-> protospacer G, etc.
KEY_MAP = {
    "rA:dA": "rA:dT",
    "rA:dC": "rA:dG",
    "rA:dG": "rA:dC",
    "rC:dA": "rC:dT",
    "rC:dC": "rC:dG",
    "rC:dT": "rC:dA",
    "rG:dA": "rG:dT",
    "rG:dG": "rG:dC",
    "rG:dT": "rG:dA",
    "rU:dT": "rT:dA",
    "rU:dG": "rT:dC",
    "rU:dC": "rT:dG",
}

INTERNAL_KEYS = [
    "rA:dC", "rA:dG", "rA:dT",
    "rC:dA", "rC:dG", "rC:dT",
    "rG:dA", "rG:dC", "rG:dT",
    "rT:dA", "rT:dC", "rT:dG",
]

new_matrix = {k: [0.0] * 20 for k in INTERNAL_KEYS}
populated = set()

for entry, value in REF.items():
    ref_key, ref_pos_str = entry.rsplit(",", 1)
    ref_pos = int(ref_pos_str)
    if ref_key not in KEY_MAP:
        print(f"WARNING: unknown ref key {ref_key!r}")
        continue
    internal_key = KEY_MAP[ref_key]
    idx = ref_pos - 1  # PAM-proximal pos 1 = index 0
    new_matrix[internal_key][idx] = value
    populated.add((internal_key, idx))

print("=== MISSING POSITIONS (will remain 0.0) ===")
total_missing = 0
for k in INTERNAL_KEYS:
    missing = [i + 1 for i in range(20) if (k, i) not in populated]
    if missing:
        print(f"  {k}: positions {missing}")
        total_missing += len(missing)
print(f"  Total missing: {total_missing}/240\n")

print("=== VALIDATION TEST CASES ===")
# Test 1: user says "rG:dA at pos6, NGG -> 0.67"
# ref rG:dA,6 (template A = protospacer T) -> internal rG:dT[idx5]
v1 = new_matrix["rG:dT"][5]
ok1 = abs(v1 - 0.666666667) < 0.001
print(f"Test 1: rG:dT[5] (ref rG:dA pos6) = {v1:.9f}  expected ~0.6667  {'PASS' if ok1 else 'FAIL'}")

# Test 2: ref rG:dA pos7 -> rG:dT[6]; ref rC:dT pos10 -> rC:dA[9]
v2a = new_matrix["rG:dT"][6]
v2b = new_matrix["rC:dA"][9]
combined = v2a * v2b  # NGG PAM factor = 1.0
ok2a = abs(v2a - 0.571428571) < 0.001
ok2b = abs(v2b - 0.875) < 0.01
print(f"Test 2a: rG:dT[6] (ref rG:dA pos7) = {v2a:.9f}  expected ~0.5714  {'PASS' if ok2a else 'FAIL'}")
print(f"Test 2b: rC:dA[9] (ref rC:dT pos10) = {v2b:.9f}  expected ~0.8750  {'PASS' if ok2b else 'FAIL - DATA MISSING'}")
print(f"  Combined = {combined:.4f}  expected 0.50\n")

print("=== NEW _CFD_MM_SCORES (Python source) ===")
print("_CFD_MM_SCORES: dict[str, list[float]] = {")
for k in INTERNAL_KEYS:
    vals = new_matrix[k]
    parts = []
    for v in vals:
        if v == 0.0:
            parts.append("0.0")
        else:
            s = f"{v:.9f}".rstrip("0")
            if s.endswith("."):
                s += "0"
            parts.append(s)
    row1 = ", ".join(parts[:10])
    row2 = ", ".join(parts[10:])
    print(f'    "{k}": [{row1},')
    print(f'             {row2}],')
print("}")

"""conftest.py — pytest configuration for SafeGenome tests.

Ensures the project root is on sys.path so that ``from src.X import Y``
works regardless of how pytest is invoked.
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# Project root is one level above this file (tests/)
ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

"""Tests for src/utils.py"""

import logging
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from src.utils import (
    VALID_CHROMS_HG38,
    GenomicInterval,
    get_logger,
    is_valid_hg38_chrom,
    load_cache,
    log_filter,
    liftover_hg19_to_hg38,
    normalize_chrom,
    normalize_guide,
    save_cache,
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Logging helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestGetLogger:
    def test_returns_named_logger(self) -> None:
        logger = get_logger("test.utils.a")
        assert isinstance(logger, logging.Logger)
        assert logger.name == "test.utils.a"

    def test_has_stream_handler(self) -> None:
        logger = get_logger("test.utils.b")
        assert any(
            isinstance(h, logging.StreamHandler) for h in logger.handlers
        )

    def test_no_duplicate_handlers_on_repeated_calls(self) -> None:
        get_logger("test.utils.c")
        get_logger("test.utils.c")
        logger = get_logger("test.utils.c")
        assert len(logger.handlers) == 1

    def test_default_level_is_info(self) -> None:
        logger = get_logger("test.utils.d")
        assert logger.level == logging.INFO

    def test_custom_level(self) -> None:
        logger = get_logger("test.utils.e", level=logging.DEBUG)
        assert logger.level == logging.DEBUG


class TestLogFilter:
    def test_logs_dropped_count(self, caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture) -> None:
        logger = get_logger("test.filter.a")
        with caplog.at_level(logging.INFO, logger="test.filter.a"):
            log_filter(logger, "remove on-target sites", 100, 95)
        assert "5" in caplog.text          # dropped
        assert "100" in caplog.text        # before
        assert "95" in caplog.text         # after
        assert "remove on-target sites" in caplog.text

    def test_zero_before_does_not_raise(self, caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture) -> None:
        logger = get_logger("test.filter.b")
        with caplog.at_level(logging.INFO, logger="test.filter.b"):
            log_filter(logger, "empty dataset", 0, 0)  # no ZeroDivisionError

    def test_all_dropped(self, caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture) -> None:
        logger = get_logger("test.filter.c")
        with caplog.at_level(logging.INFO, logger="test.filter.c"):
            log_filter(logger, "all filtered", 50, 0)
        assert "50" in caplog.text
        assert "0" in caplog.text


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# GenomicInterval
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestGenomicIntervalCreation:
    def test_basic_fields(self) -> None:
        iv = GenomicInterval("chr1", 100, 200, "+")
        assert iv.chrom == "chr1"
        assert iv.start == 100
        assert iv.end == 200
        assert iv.strand == "+"

    def test_default_strand_is_dot(self) -> None:
        iv = GenomicInterval("chr1", 0, 100)
        assert iv.strand == "."

    def test_negative_start_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="start must be >= 0"):
            GenomicInterval("chr1", -1, 100)

    def test_end_less_than_start_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="end.*must be >= start"):
            GenomicInterval("chr1", 200, 100)

    def test_zero_length_interval_is_valid(self) -> None:
        iv = GenomicInterval("chr1", 50, 50)
        assert iv.length == 0

    def test_invalid_strand_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="strand"):
            GenomicInterval("chr1", 0, 100, "F")

    def test_frozen_prevents_mutation(self) -> None:
        iv = GenomicInterval("chr1", 0, 100)
        with pytest.raises(Exception):
            iv.start = 999  # type: ignore[misc]


class TestGenomicIntervalProperties:
    def test_length(self) -> None:
        assert GenomicInterval("chr1", 100, 200).length == 100

    def test_center_even(self) -> None:
        assert GenomicInterval("chr1", 100, 200).center == 150

    def test_center_odd(self) -> None:
        assert GenomicInterval("chr1", 0, 21).center == 10

    def test_str_representation(self) -> None:
        iv = GenomicInterval("chr1", 100, 200, "+")
        assert str(iv) == "chr1:100-200(+)"


class TestGenomicIntervalConversions:
    def test_from_1based_decrements_start(self) -> None:
        iv = GenomicInterval.from_1based("chr1", 1, 100, "+")
        assert iv.start == 0
        assert iv.end == 100

    def test_from_1based_to_1based_round_trip(self) -> None:
        iv = GenomicInterval.from_1based("chrX", 500, 600, "-")
        chrom, s, e, strand = iv.to_1based()
        assert chrom == "chrX"
        assert s == 500
        assert e == 600
        assert strand == "-"

    def test_to_1based_increments_start(self) -> None:
        iv = GenomicInterval("chr1", 0, 50, "+")
        _, s, e, _ = iv.to_1based()
        assert s == 1
        assert e == 50

    def test_expand_symmetric(self) -> None:
        # center of [100, 200] = 150; expand by 50 → [100, 200]
        iv = GenomicInterval("chr1", 100, 200, "+")
        expanded = iv.expand(50)
        assert expanded.start == 100
        assert expanded.end == 200

    def test_expand_larger(self) -> None:
        iv = GenomicInterval("chr1", 100, 200, "+")  # center = 150
        expanded = iv.expand(100)
        assert expanded.start == 50
        assert expanded.end == 250

    def test_expand_clamps_at_zero(self) -> None:
        iv = GenomicInterval("chr1", 0, 10, "+")  # center = 5
        expanded = iv.expand(1000)
        assert expanded.start == 0

class TestNormalizeChrom:
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("raw, expected", [
        ("chr1",   "chr1"),
        ("1",      "chr1"),
        ("Chr1",   "chr1"),
        ("CHR1",   "chr1"),
        ("chr22",  "chr22"),
        ("22",     "chr22"),
        ("chrX",   "chrX"),
        ("X",      "chrX"),
        ("chrx",   "chrX"),
        ("chrY",   "chrY"),
        ("Y",      "chrY"),
        ("chrM",   "chrM"),
        ("M",      "chrM"),
        ("MT",     "chrM"),
        ("chrMT",  "chrM"),
        ("CHRMT",  "chrM"),
    ])
    def test_normalisation(self, raw: str, expected: str) -> None:
        assert normalize_chrom(raw) == expected

    def test_invalid_chr99_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not a valid hg38"):
            normalize_chrom("chr99")

    def test_scaffold_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            normalize_chrom("chr1_random_scaffold")

    def test_empty_string_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            normalize_chrom("")


class TestIsValidHg38Chrom:
    def test_valid_with_prefix(self) -> None:
        assert is_valid_hg38_chrom("chr1") is True

    def test_valid_without_prefix(self) -> None:
        assert is_valid_hg38_chrom("1") is True

    def test_valid_sex_chroms(self) -> None:
        assert is_valid_hg38_chrom("chrX") is True
        assert is_valid_hg38_chrom("chrY") is True

    def test_invalid_returns_false(self) -> None:
        assert is_valid_hg38_chrom("chr99") is False
        assert is_valid_hg38_chrom("scaffold_1") is False

    def test_all_standard_hg38_chroms_valid(self) -> None:
        for chrom in VALID_CHROMS_HG38:
            assert is_valid_hg38_chrom(chrom) is True, f"Expected {chrom} to be valid"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Guide RNA normalisation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestNormalizeGuide:
    VALID_20NT = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA"

    def test_uppercase_conversion(self) -> None:
        assert normalize_guide(self.VALID_20NT.lower()) == self.VALID_20NT

    def test_rna_to_dna_u_to_t(self) -> None:
        rna = "GAGUCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA"
        assert normalize_guide(rna) == self.VALID_20NT

    def test_strips_leading_trailing_whitespace(self) -> None:
        assert normalize_guide(f"  {self.VALID_20NT}  ") == self.VALID_20NT

    def test_strips_pam_from_23nt(self) -> None:
        with_pam = self.VALID_20NT + "NGG"  # 23 nt
        result = normalize_guide(with_pam)
        assert len(result) == 20
        assert result == self.VALID_20NT

    def test_21nt_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="20 nt"):
            normalize_guide(self.VALID_20NT + "A")

    def test_19nt_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="20 nt"):
            normalize_guide(self.VALID_20NT[:19])

    def test_n_character_raises(self) -> None:
        seq_with_n = "ACGTACGTACGTACGTACGN"
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="invalid characters"):
            normalize_guide(seq_with_n)

    def test_empty_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            normalize_guide("")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Disk-based caching
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestCache:
    def test_miss_returns_none(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        result = load_cache(tmp_path, "chr1", "1000", "2000", "K562")
        assert result is None

    def test_save_and_load_round_trip(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        data = {"signal": [1.0, 2.0, 3.0], "label": "K562"}
        save_cache(tmp_path, data, "chr1", "1000", "2000", "K562")
        loaded = load_cache(tmp_path, "chr1", "1000", "2000", "K562")
        assert loaded == data

    def test_different_keys_no_collision(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        save_cache(tmp_path, "result_A", "chr1", "100")
        save_cache(tmp_path, "result_B", "chr2", "100")
        assert load_cache(tmp_path, "chr1", "100") == "result_A"
        assert load_cache(tmp_path, "chr2", "100") == "result_B"

    def test_creates_missing_cache_dir(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        cache_dir = tmp_path / "subdir" / "cache"
        assert not cache_dir.exists()
        save_cache(cache_dir, {"x": 1}, "mykey")
        assert cache_dir.exists()

    def test_save_returns_existing_pkl_path(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        path = save_cache(tmp_path, {"x": 1}, "mykey")
        assert isinstance(path, Path)
        assert path.exists()
        assert path.suffix == ".pkl"

    def test_overwrite_existing_entry(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        save_cache(tmp_path, "first", "key")
        save_cache(tmp_path, "second", "key")
        assert load_cache(tmp_path, "key") == "second"

    def test_numpy_array_survives_round_trip(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        pytest.importorskip("numpy")
        import numpy as np

        arr = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 3.0])
        save_cache(tmp_path, arr, "arr_key")
        loaded = load_cache(tmp_path, "arr_key")
        assert loaded is not None
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(loaded, arr)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# hg19 → hg38 liftover  (pyliftover mocked — no network calls in tests)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestLiftover:
    def _make_mock_lo(self, return_value: list) -> MagicMock:
        mock_lo = MagicMock()
        mock_lo.convert_coordinate.return_value = return_value
        return mock_lo

    def test_successful_conversion(self) -> None:
        mock_lo = self._make_mock_lo([("chr1", 925951, "+", 0)])
        with patch("src.utils._get_liftover_instance", return_value=mock_lo):
            result = liftover_hg19_to_hg38("chr1", 930000)
        assert result == ("chr1", 925951)

    def test_unmappable_position_returns_none(self) -> None:
        mock_lo = self._make_mock_lo([])
        with patch("src.utils._get_liftover_instance", return_value=mock_lo):
            result = liftover_hg19_to_hg38("chr1", 0)
        assert result is None

    def test_adds_chr_prefix_before_lookup(self) -> None:
        mock_lo = self._make_mock_lo([("chr1", 100, "+", 0)])
        with patch("src.utils._get_liftover_instance", return_value=mock_lo):
            liftover_hg19_to_hg38("1", 100)  # no "chr" prefix
        mock_lo.convert_coordinate.assert_called_once_with("chr1", 100)

    def test_chr_prefix_not_doubled(self) -> None:
        mock_lo = self._make_mock_lo([("chr1", 100, "+", 0)])
        with patch("src.utils._get_liftover_instance", return_value=mock_lo):
            liftover_hg19_to_hg38("chr1", 100)
        mock_lo.convert_coordinate.assert_called_once_with("chr1", 100)

    def test_import_error_propagates(self) -> None:
        with patch(
            "src.utils._get_liftover_instance",
            side_effect=ImportError("pyliftover"),
        ):
            with pytest.raises(ImportError, match="pyliftover"):
                liftover_hg19_to_hg38("chr1", 100)


=== tests/test_sequence_features.py ===
"""Tests for src/sequence_features.py

Success criteria from PROJECT.md:
  [x] CFD score of on-target site (0 mismatches) = 1.0
  [x] CFD scores decrease monotonically with more mismatches (on average)
  [x] Seed-region mismatches produce lower CFD scores than non-seed mismatches
  [x] Feature matrix has no NaN values for any candidate site
  [x] MIT score = 100 for perfect match
  [x] All features present and in expected ranges
"""

import math
from typing import Any

import pytest

from src.sequence_features import (
    PROTOSPACER_LENGTH,
    SEED_END,
    _CFD_MM_SCORES,
    _CFD_PAM_SCORES,
    _MIT_POS_WEIGHTS,
    _gc_content,
    _get_mismatches,
    _max_consecutive_run,
    cfd_score,
    compute_sequence_features,
    mit_score,
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Reference sequences
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# EMX1 guide used in Doench 2016 / GUIDE-seq experiments.
# 20-nt protospacer, DNA notation, 5'→3', position 1 = PAM-proximal.
GUIDE_EMX1 = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA"

# Perfect match (on-target) = same as guide
ON_TARGET = GUIDE_EMX1

# One mismatch: PAM-proximal (position 1, seed region)
# Change the 1st character (A at pos 1) to C  → rA:dC mismatch at pos 1
ONE_MM_SEED = "CAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA"

# One mismatch: PAM-distal (position 20)
# Change the last character (A at pos 20) to C → rA:dC mismatch at pos 20
ONE_MM_NONSEED = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAC"

# Two mismatches in seed
TWO_MM_SEED = "CAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA"  # pos 1 only — use different variant
TWO_MM_SEED_ACTUAL = "CCGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA"  # pos 1 + pos 2

# Three mismatches: all non-seed
THREE_MM_NONSEED = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGCAGCC"  # positions 17, 19, 20

CANONICAL_PAM = "TGG"   # NGG → GG → PAM score 1.0
NAG_PAM = "TAG"          # NAG → AG → PAM score 0.259...
NONCANOICAL_PAM = "TAA"  # NAA → AA → PAM score 0.0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _all_features_finite(features: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """Return True if all numeric feature values are finite (no NaN/Inf)."""
    for k, v in features.items():
        if isinstance(v, float) and not math.isfinite(v):
            return False
    return True


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Matrix sanity checks
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestScoringMatrices:
    def test_pam_gg_is_1(self) -> None:
        assert _CFD_PAM_SCORES["GG"] == 1.0

    def test_pam_ag_approx(self) -> None:
        assert abs(_CFD_PAM_SCORES["AG"] - 0.259_259_259) < 1e-6

    def test_pam_gt_approx(self) -> None:
        assert abs(_CFD_PAM_SCORES["GT"] - 0.107_142_857) < 1e-6

    def test_pam_tg_approx(self) -> None:
        assert abs(_CFD_PAM_SCORES["TG"] - 0.069_444_444) < 1e-6

    def test_pam_scores_all_in_unit_interval(self) -> None:
        for k, v in _CFD_PAM_SCORES.items():
            assert 0.0 <= v <= 1.0, f"PAM score out of range for {k}: {v}"

    def test_mm_matrix_has_12_types(self) -> None:
        assert len(_CFD_MM_SCORES) == 12

    def test_mm_matrix_each_type_has_20_positions(self) -> None:
        for key, vals in _CFD_MM_SCORES.items():
            assert len(vals) == 20, f"Expected 20 values for {key}, got {len(vals)}"

    def test_mm_matrix_values_in_unit_interval(self) -> None:
        for key, vals in _CFD_MM_SCORES.items():
            for i, v in enumerate(vals):
                assert 0.0 <= v <= 1.0, (
                    f"CFD matrix value out of [0,1] at {key}[{i}]: {v}"
                )

    def test_mit_weights_correct_length(self) -> None:
        assert len(_MIT_POS_WEIGHTS) == PROTOSPACER_LENGTH

    def test_mit_weights_in_unit_interval(self) -> None:
        for i, w in enumerate(_MIT_POS_WEIGHTS):
            assert 0.0 <= w <= 1.0, f"MIT weight out of [0,1] at index {i}: {w}"

    def test_no_match_types_in_mm_matrix(self) -> None:
        """rA:dA, rC:dC, rG:dG, rT:dT should NOT be in the mismatch table."""
        for base in "ACGT":
            match_key = f"r{base}:d{base}"
            assert match_key not in _CFD_MM_SCORES, (
                f"Match type {match_key!r} should not appear in mismatch matrix"
            )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _get_mismatches
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestGetMismatches:
    def test_perfect_match_no_mismatches(self) -> None:
        assert _get_mismatches(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET) == []

    def test_single_mismatch_position_and_type(self) -> None:
        # GUIDE_EMX1[0] = 'G' (position 1, PAM-proximal).
        # ONE_MM_SEED changes pos-1 from G→C → mismatch type = rG:dC
        result = _get_mismatches(GUIDE_EMX1, ONE_MM_SEED)
        assert len(result) == 1
        pos, key = result[0]
        assert pos == 1
        assert key == "rG:dC"

    def test_position_20_mismatch(self) -> None:
        result = _get_mismatches(GUIDE_EMX1, ONE_MM_NONSEED)
        assert len(result) == 1
        pos, key = result[0]
        assert pos == 20
        assert key == "rA:dC"

    def test_two_mismatches(self) -> None:
        result = _get_mismatches(GUIDE_EMX1, TWO_MM_SEED_ACTUAL)
        assert len(result) == 2
        positions = {p for p, _ in result}
        assert positions == {1, 2}

    def test_all_mismatch(self) -> None:
        # All positions differ
        guide = "AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA"[:20]
        ot    = "CCCCCCCCCCCCCCCCCCCC"
        result = _get_mismatches(guide, ot)
        assert len(result) == 20


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# CFD score
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestCfdScore:
    # Project success criterion: on-target = 1.0
    def test_perfect_match_canonical_pam_is_1(self) -> None:
        score = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert score == pytest.approx(1.0)

    def test_perfect_match_nag_pam(self) -> None:
        # Still 0-mismatch product = 1, then × PAM score
        score = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, NAG_PAM)
        assert score == pytest.approx(0.259_259_259)

    def test_noncanonical_pam_gives_zero(self) -> None:
        score = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, NONCANOICAL_PAM)
        assert score == pytest.approx(0.0)

    def test_score_in_unit_interval(self) -> None:
        for ot in [ON_TARGET, ONE_MM_SEED, ONE_MM_NONSEED, THREE_MM_NONSEED]:
            score = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ot, CANONICAL_PAM)
            assert 0.0 <= score <= 1.0, f"CFD out of range for {ot}: {score}"

    def test_more_mismatches_lower_score_average(self) -> None:
        """Project criterion: CFD decreases monotonically with more mismatches."""
        score_0mm = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, CANONICAL_PAM)
        # Build sites with increasing mismatches all in PAM-distal positions
        # to avoid zeros from seed penalties
        ot_1mm = GUIDE_EMX1[:19] + "C"        # pos 20 mismatch (rA:dC)
        ot_2mm = GUIDE_EMX1[:18] + "CC"       # pos 19, 20 mismatch
        score_1mm = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ot_1mm, CANONICAL_PAM)
        score_2mm = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ot_2mm, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert score_0mm >= score_1mm, (
            f"0-mm ({score_0mm}) should be >= 1-mm ({score_1mm})"
        )
        assert score_1mm >= score_2mm, (
            f"1-mm ({score_1mm}) should be >= 2-mm ({score_2mm})"
        )

    def test_invalid_guide_length_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="20 nt"):
            cfd_score("ACGT", ON_TARGET, CANONICAL_PAM)

    def test_invalid_pam_length_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="PAM must be 3"):
            cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, "GG")

    def test_invalid_base_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="invalid bases"):
            cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, "NAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA", CANONICAL_PAM)

    def test_case_insensitive_input(self) -> None:
        lower = cfd_score(
            GUIDE_EMX1.lower(), ON_TARGET.lower(), CANONICAL_PAM.lower()
        )
        upper = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert lower == pytest.approx(upper)

    def test_pam_first_base_n_ignored(self) -> None:
        """NGG and AGG should give same result (N position ignored)."""
        score_ngg = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, "TGG")
        score_agg = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, "AGG")
        score_cgg = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, "CGG")
        assert score_ngg == pytest.approx(score_agg)
        assert score_agg == pytest.approx(score_cgg)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MIT score
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestMitScore:
    def test_perfect_match_is_100(self) -> None:
        assert mit_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET) == pytest.approx(100.0)

    def test_score_in_range(self) -> None:
        for ot in [ON_TARGET, ONE_MM_SEED, ONE_MM_NONSEED, THREE_MM_NONSEED]:
            s = mit_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ot)
            assert 0.0 <= s <= 100.0, f"MIT score out of range for {ot}: {s}"

    def test_mismatch_lowers_score(self) -> None:
        perfect = mit_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET)
        one_mm = mit_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ONE_MM_NONSEED)
        assert perfect > one_mm, (
            f"Perfect match ({perfect}) should beat 1-mismatch ({one_mm})"
        )

    def test_two_mismatches_lower_than_one(self) -> None:
        one = mit_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ONE_MM_NONSEED)
        # Two mismatches at positions 19 and 20
        ot_2mm = GUIDE_EMX1[:18] + "CC"
        two = mit_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ot_2mm)
        assert one > two, f"1-mm ({one}) should beat 2-mm ({two})"

    def test_clustered_mismatches_lower_than_spread(self) -> None:
        """Clustered mismatches should score lower due to distance penalty."""
        # 2 mismatches close together (positions 19, 20)
        ot_clustered = GUIDE_EMX1[:18] + "CC"       # gap = 1
        # 2 mismatches spread apart (positions 1, 20) — rA:dC for guide A
        ot_spread = "C" + GUIDE_EMX1[1:19] + "C"   # gap = 19
        score_clustered = mit_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ot_clustered)
        score_spread = mit_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ot_spread)
        # Spread → larger mean distance → larger d_factor → lower score
        assert score_spread <= score_clustered, (
            f"Spread ({score_spread}) should be <= clustered ({score_clustered})"
        )

    def test_invalid_length_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            mit_score(GUIDE_EMX1, "ACGT")

class TestComputeSequenceFeatures:
    def _perfect_match_features(self) -> dict:
        return compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, CANONICAL_PAM)

    def test_returns_dict(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        assert isinstance(f, dict)

    def test_cfd_score_present_and_correct(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        assert "cfd_score" in f
        assert f["cfd_score"] == pytest.approx(1.0)

    def test_mit_score_present_and_correct(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        assert "mit_score" in f
        assert f["mit_score"] == pytest.approx(100.0)

    def test_num_mismatches_perfect_match(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        assert f["num_mismatches"] == 0

    def test_num_mismatches_one_mm(self) -> None:
        f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ONE_MM_SEED, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert f["num_mismatches"] == 1

    def test_mm_pos_flags_correct_for_perfect_match(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        for pos in range(1, PROTOSPACER_LENGTH + 1):
            assert f[f"mm_pos_{pos}"] == 0

    def test_mm_pos_flags_correct_for_seed_mismatch(self) -> None:
        f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ONE_MM_SEED, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert f["mm_pos_1"] == 1
        for pos in range(2, PROTOSPACER_LENGTH + 1):
            assert f[f"mm_pos_{pos}"] == 0

    def test_mm_type_correct_for_seed_mismatch(self) -> None:
        # GUIDE_EMX1[0] = 'G'; ONE_MM_SEED changes it to 'C' → rG:dC
        f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ONE_MM_SEED, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert f["mm_type_1"] == "rG:dC"
        assert f["mm_type_2"] == ""  # match

    def test_mm_type_empty_string_on_match(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        for pos in range(1, PROTOSPACER_LENGTH + 1):
            assert f[f"mm_type_{pos}"] == ""

    def test_gc_content_guide(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        expected = (GUIDE_EMX1.count("G") + GUIDE_EMX1.count("C")) / 20
        assert f["gc_content_guide"] == pytest.approx(expected)

    def test_gc_content_offtarget(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        assert f["gc_content_offtarget"] == pytest.approx(f["gc_content_guide"])

    def test_pam_match_canonical(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        assert f["pam_match"] == 1

    def test_pam_match_nag(self) -> None:
        f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ON_TARGET, NAG_PAM)
        assert f["pam_match"] == 0

    def test_seed_mismatches_correct(self) -> None:
        # ONE_MM_SEED: mismatch at position 1 (seed)
        f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ONE_MM_SEED, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert f["seed_mismatches"] == 1
        assert f["nonseed_mismatches"] == 0

    def test_nonseed_mismatches_correct(self) -> None:
        # ONE_MM_NONSEED: mismatch at position 20 (non-seed)
        f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ONE_MM_NONSEED, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert f["seed_mismatches"] == 0
        assert f["nonseed_mismatches"] == 1

    def test_seed_end_boundary(self) -> None:
        """Position 12 is seed, position 13 is non-seed."""
        # Mismatch at position 12 (seed boundary)
        ot_pos12 = GUIDE_EMX1[:11] + "C" + GUIDE_EMX1[12:]
        f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ot_pos12, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert f["seed_mismatches"] == 1
        assert f["nonseed_mismatches"] == 0

        # Mismatch at position 13 (non-seed boundary)
        ot_pos13 = GUIDE_EMX1[:12] + "C" + GUIDE_EMX1[13:]
        f2 = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ot_pos13, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert f2["seed_mismatches"] == 0
        assert f2["nonseed_mismatches"] == 1

    def test_consecutive_mm_no_mismatches(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        assert f["consecutive_mm"] == 0

    def test_consecutive_mm_single(self) -> None:
        f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ONE_MM_SEED, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert f["consecutive_mm"] == 1

    def test_consecutive_mm_run_of_3(self) -> None:
        # Mismatches at positions 18, 19, 20
        ot = GUIDE_EMX1[:17] + "CCC"
        f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ot, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert f["consecutive_mm"] == 3

    def test_consecutive_mm_nonconsecutive(self) -> None:
        # Mismatches at positions 1 and 20 (not consecutive)
        ot = "C" + GUIDE_EMX1[1:19] + "C"
        f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ot, CANONICAL_PAM)
        assert f["consecutive_mm"] == 1

    def test_no_nan_values(self) -> None:
        """Project criterion: feature matrix has no NaN values."""
        for ot in [ON_TARGET, ONE_MM_SEED, ONE_MM_NONSEED, THREE_MM_NONSEED,
                   TWO_MM_SEED_ACTUAL]:
            f = compute_sequence_features(GUIDE_EMX1, ot, CANONICAL_PAM)
            assert _all_features_finite(f), f"NaN/Inf in features for {ot}"

    def test_all_expected_keys_present(self) -> None:
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        expected_keys = (
            ["cfd_score", "mit_score", "num_mismatches"]
            + [f"mm_pos_{p}" for p in range(1, 21)]
            + [f"mm_type_{p}" for p in range(1, 21)]
            + ["gc_content_guide", "gc_content_offtarget",
               "pam_match", "seed_mismatches",
               "nonseed_mismatches", "consecutive_mm"]
        )
        for key in expected_keys:
            assert key in f, f"Missing feature key: {key!r}"

    def test_feature_count(self) -> None:
        """Total features = 3 + 20 + 20 + 6 = 49."""
        f = self._perfect_match_features()
        assert len(f) == 49

    def test_lowercase_input_accepted(self) -> None:
        f = compute_sequence_features(
            GUIDE_EMX1.lower(), ON_TARGET.lower(), CANONICAL_PAM.lower()
        )
        assert f["cfd_score"] == pytest.approx(1.0)

        assert _gc_content("GCGCGCGCGCGCGCGCGCGC") == pytest.approx(1.0)
