        assert _gc_content("ACGT") == pytest.approx(0.5)

    def test_empty_returns_zero(self) -> None:
        assert _gc_content("") == pytest.approx(0.0)


class TestMaxConsecutiveRun:
    def test_empty(self) -> None:
        assert _max_consecutive_run(set()) == 0

    def test_single(self) -> None:
        assert _max_consecutive_run({5}) == 1

    def test_two_consecutive(self) -> None:
        assert _max_consecutive_run({3, 4}) == 2

    def test_three_consecutive(self) -> None:
        assert _max_consecutive_run({1, 2, 3}) == 3

    def test_non_consecutive(self) -> None:
        assert _max_consecutive_run({1, 3, 5}) == 1

    def test_mixed_consecutive_and_not(self) -> None:
        # 8,9,10 form a run of 3; 1 is isolated
        assert _max_consecutive_run({1, 8, 9, 10}) == 3


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Seed vs non-seed qualitative check (PROJECT.md success criterion)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestSeedVsNonSeedEffect:
    """Project criterion: seed mismatches produce lower CFD scores than
    non-seed mismatches (checked for rG:dA, the most tolerated type)."""

    def test_rGdA_seed_below_nonseed(self) -> None:
        """A rG:dA mismatch at a deep-seed position should have a lower
        CFD mismatch factor than the same type at the most PAM-distal
        position, because seed-region activity should be more disrupted.

        Uses the mismatch matrix values for rG:dA specifically, since that
        type has non-zero values at PAM-distal positions in the canonical
        matrix.
        """
        # Position 1 (PAM-proximal, deep seed), index 0
        seed_factor = _CFD_MM_SCORES["rG:dA"][0]
        # Position 20 (PAM-distal, non-seed), index 19
        nonseed_factor = _CFD_MM_SCORES["rG:dA"][19]
        assert seed_factor <= nonseed_factor, (
            f"Seed factor ({seed_factor}) should be <= non-seed factor "
            f"({nonseed_factor}) for rG:dA"
        )

    def test_cfd_seed_mismatch_le_nonseed_mismatch_rGdA(self) -> None:
        """End-to-end: single rG:dT mismatch in seed has lower CFD than non-seed.

        Uses canonical Doench 2016 matrix values.
        Guide: GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA
          idx 11 = G (position 12, PAM-proximal seed)  → rG:dT[11] = 0.385
          idx 17 = G (position 18, PAM-distal non-seed) → rG:dT[17] = 0.667

        Biological expectation: seed mismatch (pos 12) is more disruptive
        (lower CFD) than PAM-distal mismatch (pos 18) for rG:dA mismatches
        (reference notation; our internal rG:dT).
        """
        # Position 12 (idx 11, last seed position): guide G → off-target T
        ot_seed = GUIDE_EMX1[:11] + "T" + GUIDE_EMX1[12:]   # rG:dT at pos 12

        # Position 18 (idx 17, PAM-distal non-seed): guide G → off-target T
        ot_nonseed = GUIDE_EMX1[:17] + "T" + GUIDE_EMX1[18:]  # rG:dT at pos 18

        cfd_seed    = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ot_seed,    CANONICAL_PAM)
        cfd_nonseed = cfd_score(GUIDE_EMX1, ot_nonseed, CANONICAL_PAM)

        assert cfd_seed <= cfd_nonseed, (
            f"Seed CFD ({cfd_seed}) should be <= non-seed CFD ({cfd_nonseed})"
        )


=== tests/test_offtarget_finder.py ===
"""
tests/test_offtarget_finder.py — Tests for src/offtarget_finder.py

Strategy
--------
- _write_input, _parse_output, _compute_mismatch_positions:
    tested directly against real files / pure logic (no mocking).
- find_offtargets:
    binary_path / genome_path existence is checked first, so we test those
    error paths directly.  The full happy-path is tested by patching
    subprocess.run and writing a fake output file via a patched _run_binary.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from src.offtarget_finder import (
    _SPACER_LENGTH,
    _compute_mismatch_positions,
    _empty_dataframe,
    _parse_output,
    _write_input,
    find_offtargets,
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fixtures / helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

GUIDE = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA"  # 20 nt, no PAM


def _make_output_file(tmp_path: Path, lines: list[str]) -> Path:
    """Write a fake Cas-OFFinder output file and return its path."""
    p = tmp_path / "output.txt"
    p.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return p


# One real off-target line: single mismatch at position 1 (T instead of G)
# Actual Cas-OFFinder v2 output format: guide_pam  chrom  pos  DNA+PAM  strand  num_mm
_OT_LINE = (
    f"{GUIDE}NGG\tchr7\t117548628\t"
    f"TAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAANGG\t+\t1"
)

# Perfect-match (on-target) line: 0 mismatches
_ONTARGET_LINE = (
    f"{GUIDE}NGG\tchr7\t117548628\t"
    f"{GUIDE}NGG\t+\t0"
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _write_input
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class TestWriteInput:
    def test_creates_file(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "input.txt"
        _write_input(p, GUIDE, Path("/genome/hg38.fa"), "NGG", 4)
        assert p.exists()

    def test_line_count(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "input.txt"
        _write_input(p, GUIDE, Path("/genome/hg38.fa"), "NGG", 4)
        lines = p.read_text().splitlines()
        assert len(lines) == 3

    def test_line1_is_genome_path(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        genome = tmp_path / "hg38.fa"
        genome.touch()
        p = tmp_path / "input.txt"
        _write_input(p, GUIDE, genome, "NGG", 4)
        lines = p.read_text().splitlines()
        assert str(genome.resolve()) == lines[0]

    def test_line2_crispr_pattern_length(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "input.txt"
        _write_input(p, GUIDE, Path("/g/hg38.fa"), "NGG", 4)
        pattern = p.read_text().splitlines()[1]
        # 20 N's + 3-char PAM = 23 chars
        assert len(pattern) == _SPACER_LENGTH + 3

    def test_line2_starts_with_n_spacer(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "input.txt"
        _write_input(p, GUIDE, Path("/g/hg38.fa"), "NGG", 4)
        pattern = p.read_text().splitlines()[1]
        assert pattern[:_SPACER_LENGTH] == "N" * _SPACER_LENGTH

    def test_line2_ends_with_pam(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "input.txt"
        _write_input(p, GUIDE, Path("/g/hg38.fa"), "NGG", 4)
        pattern = p.read_text().splitlines()[1]
        assert pattern.endswith("NGG")

    def test_line3_contains_guide(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "input.txt"
        _write_input(p, GUIDE, Path("/g/hg38.fa"), "NGG", 4)
        entry = p.read_text().splitlines()[2]
        assert entry.startswith(GUIDE)

    def test_line3_contains_max_mismatches(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "input.txt"
        _write_input(p, GUIDE, Path("/g/hg38.fa"), "NGG", 4)
        entry = p.read_text().splitlines()[2]
        assert entry.endswith("4")

    def test_custom_pam_reflected(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "input.txt"
        _write_input(p, GUIDE, Path("/g/hg38.fa"), "NRG", 4)
        lines = p.read_text().splitlines()
        assert lines[1].endswith("NRG")
        assert lines[2].startswith(GUIDE + "NRG")

    def test_lowercase_pam_uppercased(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "input.txt"
        _write_input(p, GUIDE, Path("/g/hg38.fa"), "ngg", 4)
        lines = p.read_text().splitlines()
        assert lines[1].endswith("NGG")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _compute_mismatch_positions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class TestComputeMismatchPositions:
    def test_no_mismatch_returns_empty(self) -> None:
        assert _compute_mismatch_positions(GUIDE, GUIDE) == []

    def test_single_mismatch_position_1(self) -> None:
        ot = "T" + GUIDE[1:]
        assert _compute_mismatch_positions(GUIDE, ot) == [1]

    def test_single_mismatch_position_20(self) -> None:
        ot = GUIDE[:-1] + "C"
        assert _compute_mismatch_positions(GUIDE, ot) == [20]

    def test_two_mismatches(self) -> None:
        # GUIDE[5] = "C"; replace index 0 with "T" and index 5 with "T"
        ot = "T" + GUIDE[1:5] + "T" + GUIDE[6:]
        result = _compute_mismatch_positions(GUIDE, ot)
        assert result == [1, 6]

    def test_all_mismatches(self) -> None:
        ot = "T" * _SPACER_LENGTH
        result = _compute_mismatch_positions(GUIDE, ot)
        # All positions that differ (G→T)
        expected = [i + 1 for i, b in enumerate(GUIDE) if b != "T"]
        assert result == expected

    def test_case_insensitive(self) -> None:
        assert _compute_mismatch_positions(GUIDE, GUIDE.lower()) == []

    def test_returns_sorted_positions(self) -> None:
        ot = GUIDE[:2] + "T" + GUIDE[3:10] + "C" + GUIDE[11:]
        result = _compute_mismatch_positions(GUIDE, ot)
        assert result == sorted(result)

    def test_partial_overlap_uses_zip(self) -> None:
        # zip stops at shorter sequence — positions beyond min length are not reported
        result = _compute_mismatch_positions("AAAAAAAAAA", "TTTTTTTTTT")
        assert len(result) == 10


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _empty_dataframe
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class TestEmptyDataframe:
    def test_zero_rows(self) -> None:
        assert len(_empty_dataframe()) == 0

    def test_expected_columns(self) -> None:
        df = _empty_dataframe()
        expected = {
            "guide",
            "chrom",
            "start",
            "strand",
            "off_target_seq",
            "pam_seq",
            "num_mismatches",
            "mismatch_positions",
        }
        assert set(df.columns) == expected


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _parse_output
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class TestParseOutput:
    def test_empty_file_returns_empty_df(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "out.txt"
        p.write_text("")
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert len(df) == 0
        assert set(df.columns) >= {"guide", "chrom", "start", "num_mismatches"}

    def test_missing_file_returns_empty_df(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = tmp_path / "does_not_exist.txt"
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert len(df) == 0

    def test_one_hit_parsed(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [_OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert len(df) == 1

    def test_chrom_normalized(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        # Cas-OFFinder might return "7" instead of "chr7"
        line = _OT_LINE.replace("\tchr7\t", "\t7\t")
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [line])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert df.loc[0, "chrom"] == "chr7"

    def test_start_is_integer(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [_OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert df["start"].dtype.kind == "i"

    def test_num_mismatches_is_integer(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [_OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert df["num_mismatches"].dtype.kind == "i"

    def test_strand_preserved(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [_OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert df.loc[0, "strand"] == "+"

    def test_minus_strand_preserved(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        minus_line = _OT_LINE.replace("\t+\t", "\t-\t")
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [minus_line])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert df.loc[0, "strand"] == "-"

    def test_pam_seq_extracted(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [_OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        # off-target sequence in _OT_LINE ends with "AAGAANGG"
        assert len(df.loc[0, "pam_seq"]) == 3

    def test_off_target_seq_is_20nt(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [_OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert len(df.loc[0, "off_target_seq"]) == 20

    def test_mismatch_positions_computed(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [_OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        # off-target spacer starts with T, guide starts with G → mismatch at 1
        assert 1 in df.loc[0, "mismatch_positions"]

    def test_guide_column_populated(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [_OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert df.loc[0, "guide"] == GUIDE

    def test_malformed_line_skipped(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, ["only\ttwo\tfields", _OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert len(df) == 1

    def test_scaffold_chrom_skipped(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        scaffold_line = _OT_LINE.replace("\tchr7\t", "\tchrUn_gl000220\t")
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [scaffold_line, _OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        # scaffold site dropped, real chr7 site kept
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert df.loc[0, "chrom"] == "chr7"

    def test_multiple_hits(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        line2 = _OT_LINE.replace("117548628", "200000000")
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [_OT_LINE, line2])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert len(df) == 2

    def test_all_expected_columns_present(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [_OT_LINE])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        for col in (
            "guide",
            "chrom",
            "start",
            "strand",
            "off_target_seq",
            "pam_seq",
            "num_mismatches",
            "mismatch_positions",
        ):
            assert col in df.columns, f"Missing column: {col}"

    def test_blank_lines_ignored(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, ["", _OT_LINE, ""])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert len(df) == 1

    def test_non_integer_fields_skipped(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        bad_line = _OT_LINE.replace("\t117548628\t", "\tNOT_A_NUM\t")
        p = _make_output_file(tmp_path, [bad_line])
        df = _parse_output(p, GUIDE)
        assert len(df) == 0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# find_offtargets — error paths (no binary needed)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class TestFindOfftargetsErrors:
    def test_missing_genome_raises(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="Genome not found"):
            find_offtargets(
                GUIDE,
                tmp_path / "no_genome.fa",
                binary_path=tmp_path / "fake_bin",
            )

    def test_missing_binary_raises(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        genome = tmp_path / "hg38.fa"
        genome.touch()
        with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="Cas-OFFinder binary not found"):
            find_offtargets(
                GUIDE,
                genome,
                binary_path=tmp_path / "no_binary",
            )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# find_offtargets — happy path (mocked binary)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class TestFindOfftargetsHappyPath:
    """Tests that mock _run_binary so no real Cas-OFFinder is needed."""

    def _mock_run_binary(self, output_lines: list[str]):
        """Return a mock for _run_binary that writes fake output."""

        def _fake_run(
            input_path: Path,
            output_path: Path,
            binary_path: Path,
            mode: str,
        ) -> None:
            output_path.write_text(
                "\n".join(output_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
            )

        return _fake_run

    def test_returns_dataframe(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        genome = tmp_path / "hg38.fa"
        genome.touch()
        binary = tmp_path / "cas-offinder"
        binary.touch()

        with patch(
            "src.offtarget_finder._run_binary",
            side_effect=self._mock_run_binary([_OT_LINE]),
        ):
            df = find_offtargets(GUIDE, genome, binary_path=binary)

        assert len(df) == 1

    def test_ontarget_removed_by_default(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        genome = tmp_path / "hg38.fa"
        genome.touch()
        binary = tmp_path / "cas-offinder"
        binary.touch()

        with patch(
            "src.offtarget_finder._run_binary",
            side_effect=self._mock_run_binary([_ONTARGET_LINE, _OT_LINE]),
        ):
            df = find_offtargets(GUIDE, genome, binary_path=binary)

        # on-target (0 mm) should be removed
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert (df["num_mismatches"] > 0).all()

    def test_ontarget_kept_when_disabled(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        genome = tmp_path / "hg38.fa"
        genome.touch()
        binary = tmp_path / "cas-offinder"
        binary.touch()

        with patch(
            "src.offtarget_finder._run_binary",
            side_effect=self._mock_run_binary([_ONTARGET_LINE, _OT_LINE]),
        ):
            df = find_offtargets(
                GUIDE, genome, binary_path=binary, remove_ontarget=False
            )

        assert len(df) == 2

    def test_guide_normalized_23nt_accepted(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        """find_offtargets should strip PAM from 23-nt input."""
        genome = tmp_path / "hg38.fa"
        genome.touch()
        binary = tmp_path / "cas-offinder"
        binary.touch()

        with patch(
            "src.offtarget_finder._run_binary",
            side_effect=self._mock_run_binary([_OT_LINE]),
        ):
            df = find_offtargets(
                GUIDE + "NGG",  # 23-nt with PAM
                genome,
                binary_path=binary,
            )

        assert df.loc[0, "guide"] == GUIDE

    def test_empty_output_returns_empty_df(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        genome = tmp_path / "hg38.fa"
        genome.touch()
        binary = tmp_path / "cas-offinder"
        binary.touch()

        with patch(
            "src.offtarget_finder._run_binary",
            side_effect=self._mock_run_binary([]),
        ):
            df = find_offtargets(GUIDE, genome, binary_path=binary)

        assert len(df) == 0
        assert set(df.columns) >= {"guide", "chrom", "start", "num_mismatches"}

    def test_binary_called_with_correct_mode(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        genome = tmp_path / "hg38.fa"
        genome.touch()
        binary = tmp_path / "cas-offinder"
        binary.touch()

        captured_mode: list[str] = []

        def _capture(input_path, output_path, binary_path, mode):
            captured_mode.append(mode)
            output_path.write_text("")

        with patch("src.offtarget_finder._run_binary", side_effect=_capture):
            find_offtargets(GUIDE, genome, binary_path=binary, mode="C")

        assert captured_mode == ["C"]

    def test_casoffinder_error_propagates(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        genome = tmp_path / "hg38.fa"
        genome.touch()
        binary = tmp_path / "cas-offinder"
        binary.touch()

        def _failing_run(input_path, output_path, binary_path, mode):
            raise subprocess.CalledProcessError(1, ["cas-offinder"])

        with patch("src.offtarget_finder._run_binary", side_effect=_failing_run):
            with pytest.raises(subprocess.CalledProcessError):
                find_offtargets(GUIDE, genome, binary_path=binary)


=== tests/test_parse_datasets.py ===
"""
tests/test_parse_datasets.py — Tests for scripts/02_parse_datasets.py

Strategy
--------
All tests use synthetic data written to temporary files so they run without
any real supplementary data downloaded.

DeepCRISPR tests cover both paths:
  * Two-sheet Excel format  (actual Chuai 2018 file: 'target' + 'off-target')
  * Flat-file CSV format    (fallback path with alias-based column detection)

Liftover is bypassed by using src_genome_build="hg38" throughout (the hg19
liftover path is tested with a mock patch on the module-level function).

DISCOVER-seq tests use synthetic Excel files that replicate the actual three-
sheet structure of wienert2019_discover_seq_S1.xlsx:
  * DISCOVER VEGFA  (header=2)
  * DISCOVER RNF2   (header=1)
  * DISCOVER HBB    (header=1, with NaN separator rows)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import sys
import types
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd
import pytest
from unittest.mock import patch

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dynamic import: scripts/02_parse_datasets.py can't use "from scripts.02..."
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
_SCRIPT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts" / "02_parse_datasets.py"


def _load_module() -> types.ModuleType:
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("parse_datasets_mod", _SCRIPT)
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


_PD = _load_module()

# Pull out the symbols under test
_pick_col = _PD._pick_col
_load_tabular = _PD._load_tabular
_apply_normalise_chrom = _PD._apply_normalise_chrom
parse_deepcrispr_k562 = _PD.parse_deepcrispr_k562
parse_discover_seq_k562 = _PD.parse_discover_seq_k562

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Shared constants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

GUIDE   = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA"   # 20-nt protospacer
OT_1MM  = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAACAA"   # 1 mismatch at pos 18 (G→C)
OT_2MM  = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGATCAA"   # 2 mismatches
OT_23NT = OT_1MM + "GGG"           # 23-nt with PAM

_SCHEMA_COLS = [
    "guide_id", "chrom", "start", "end", "strand",
    "guide_seq", "offtarget_seq", "pam_seq",
    "num_mismatches", "read_count", "cell_type", "dataset", "genome_build",
]
_SCHEMA_DTYPES: dict[str, str] = {
    "start": "int64",
    "end": "int64",
    "num_mismatches": "int64",
    "read_count": "float64",
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers — generic
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _write_csv(tmp_path: Path, name: str, rows: list[dict]) -> Path:
    p = tmp_path / name
    pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(p, sep="\t", index=False)
    return p


def _write_xlsx(
    tmp_path: Path, name: str, data: dict[str, list[dict]]
) -> Path:
    """Write named sheets to an Excel workbook (all sheets: rows as dicts)."""
    p = tmp_path / name
    with pd.ExcelWriter(p, engine="openpyxl") as writer:
        for sheet, rows in data.items():
            pd.DataFrame(rows).to_excel(writer, sheet_name=sheet, index=False)
    return p


def _assert_schema(df: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    assert list(df.columns) == _SCHEMA_COLS, f"Column mismatch: {list(df.columns)}"
    for col, dtype in _SCHEMA_DTYPES.items():
        assert df[col].dtype == dtype, (
            f"'{col}': expected {dtype}, got {df[col].dtype}"
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers — DeepCRISPR two-sheet Excel format
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Actual column names from chuai2018_deepcrispr_k562.xlsx
_DC_TARGET_COLS = ["Target ID", "Chromosome", "Start", "End", "Strand", "Target sgRNA"]
_DC_OT_COLS = [
    "OT-ID", "Target ID", "Chromosome", "Start", "End", "Strand",
    "OT", "Cleavage Frequency",
]


def _make_dc_target_row(
    tid: str = "sg1",
    chrom: str = "chr1",
    start: int = 1000,
    end: int = 1023,
    strand: str = "+",
    guide_seq_23: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """One row for the 'target' sheet.  guide_seq_23 defaults to GUIDE+NGG."""
    return {
        "Target ID": tid,
        "Chromosome": chrom,
        "Start": start,
        "End": end,
        "Strand": strand,
        "Target sgRNA": guide_seq_23 or (GUIDE + "NGG"),
    }


def _make_dc_ot_row(
    ot_id: str = "sg1_ot1",
    tid: str = "sg1",
    chrom: str = "chr1",
    start: int = 2000,
    end: int = 2023,
    strand: str = "+",
    ot_seq: str = OT_1MM,
    freq: float = 0.05,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """One row for the 'off-target' sheet."""
    return {
        "OT-ID": ot_id,
        "Target ID": tid,
        "Chromosome": chrom,
        "Start": start,
        "End": end,
        "Strand": strand,
        "OT": ot_seq,
        "Cleavage Frequency": freq,
    }


def _write_dc_excel(
    tmp_path: Path,
    target_rows: list[dict] | None = None,
    ot_rows: list[dict] | None = None,
    name: str = "dc.xlsx",
) -> Path:
    """Write a two-sheet DeepCRISPR Excel file."""
    p = tmp_path / name
    target = pd.DataFrame(target_rows or [_make_dc_target_row()])
    ot = pd.DataFrame(ot_rows or [_make_dc_ot_row()])
    with pd.ExcelWriter(p, engine="openpyxl") as w:
        target.to_excel(w, sheet_name="target", index=False)
        ot.to_excel(w, sheet_name="off-target", index=False)
    return p


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers — DeepCRISPR flat-file (CSV) format  (backward-compat / fallback)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _make_dc_row(
    *,
    cell_type: str | None = "K562",
    ot_seq: str = OT_1MM,
    n_mm: int | None = 1,
    freq: float = 0.05,
    chrom: str = "chr1",
    start: int = 1000,
    end: int = 1023,
    strand: str = "+",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Minimal DeepCRISPR flat-file row using canonical column names."""
    row: dict[str, Any] = {
        "Target":             "VEGFA_site2",
        "Target_Sequence":    GUIDE,
        "Offtarget_Sequence": ot_seq,
        "Chromosome":         chrom,
        "Start":              start,
        "End":                end,
        "Strand":             strand,
        "Cleavage_Frequency": freq,
    }
    if cell_type is not None:
        row["Cell_type"] = cell_type
    if n_mm is not None:
        row["Mismatches"] = n_mm
    return row


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers — DISCOVER-seq S1 Excel (actual three-sheet format)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# --- DISCOVER VEGFA sheet helpers ---
# header=2: rows 0,1 are title/span; row 2 is the actual column header.
_VEGFA_COLS = [
    None, "Chr:Start-End", "Cutsite", "Strand", "PAM",
    "Guide sequence", "Mismatches", "Rep1", "Rep2",
]


def _vegfa_raw_rows(data_rows: list[list]) -> list[list]:
    """Build the full set of rows for the VEGFA sheet (incl. 2 dummy rows)."""
    dummy0 = ["Table S1: DISCOVER scores"] + [None] * (len(_VEGFA_COLS) - 1)
    dummy1 = [None] * len(_VEGFA_COLS)
    header = _VEGFA_COLS  # this row becomes the pandas column names at header=2
    return [dummy0, dummy1, header] + data_rows


def _make_vegfa_data_row(
    gene: str = "PAPD7",
    coord: str = "chr5:6714989-6715008",
    cutsite: int = 6715005,
    strand: str = "sense",
    pam: str = "NGG",
    guide_seq: str = GUIDE,
    mismatches: int = 1,
    rep1: float = 100.0,
    rep2: float = 50.0,
) -> list:
    return [gene, coord, cutsite, strand, pam, guide_seq, mismatches, rep1, rep2]


def _make_vegfa_ontarget_row(guide_seq: str = GUIDE) -> list:
    return ["VEGFA", "chr6:43770822-43770841", 43770824, "antisense",
            "NGG", guide_seq, 0, 417, 321]


# --- DISCOVER RNF2 sheet helpers ---
# header=1: row 0 is title; row 1 is the column header.
_RNF2_COLS = [
    "gRNA", "gene", "Hg38 coordinates", "cut site", "Strand",
    "PAM", "Guide sequence", "mismatches", "Rep1", "Rep2",
]


def _rnf2_raw_rows(data_rows: list[list]) -> list[list]:
    dummy0 = ["Table S1: DISCOVER scores"] + [None] * (len(_RNF2_COLS) - 1)
    header = _RNF2_COLS
    return [dummy0, header] + data_rows


def _make_rnf2_ontarget_row(guide_seq: str = GUIDE) -> list:
    return ["RNF2", "RNF2", "chr1:185087638-185087657",
            185087640, "antisense", "NGG", guide_seq, 0, 44, 56]


def _make_rnf2_data_row(
    guide_id: str = "RNF2",
    coord: str = "chr1:100000-100019",
    strand: str = "sense",
    pam: str = "NGG",
    guide_seq: str = GUIDE,
    mismatches: int = 2,
    rep1: float = 10.0,
    rep2: float = 5.0,
) -> list:
    return [guide_id, "gene", coord, 100010, strand, pam, guide_seq,
            mismatches, rep1, rep2]


# --- DISCOVER HBB sheet helpers ---
# header=1: row 0 is title; row 1 is column header.
# An extra unnamed column (Unnamed: 5) appears between 'cut site' and 'Strand'.
_HBB_COLS = [
    "gRNA", "gene", "Cas9", "Hg38 coordinates", "cut site", None,
    "Strand", "PAM", "Guide sequence", "mismatches", "Rep1", "Rep2",
]


def _hbb_raw_rows(data_rows: list[list]) -> list[list]:
    dummy0 = ["Table S1: DISCOVER scores"] + [None] * (len(_HBB_COLS) - 1)
    header = _HBB_COLS
    return [dummy0, header] + data_rows


def _make_hbb_ontarget_row(guide_seq: str = GUIDE) -> list:
    return ["HBB", "HBB", "WT", "chr11:5226968-5226987",
            5226984, None, "sense", "NGG", guide_seq, 0, 124, 372]


def _make_hbb_data_row(
    coord: str = "chr9:101833584-101833603",
    strand: str = "sense",
    pam: str = "NGG",
    guide_seq: str = GUIDE,
    mismatches: int = 3,
    rep1: float = 49.0,
    rep2: float = 97.0,
    cas9: str = "WT",
) -> list:
    return ["HBB", "OT1 (NC)", cas9, coord, 101833600,
            None, strand, pam, guide_seq, mismatches, rep1, rep2]


def _hbb_separator_rows() -> list[list]:
    """Two NaN separator rows between WT and HiFi Cas9 sections."""
    nan_row = [None] * len(_HBB_COLS)
    return [nan_row, nan_row]


def _write_discover_s1(
    tmp_path: Path,
    vegfa_data: list[list] | None = None,
    rnf2_data: list[list] | None = None,
    hbb_data: list[list] | None = None,
    name: str = "s1.xlsx",
    include_vegfa: bool = True,
    include_rnf2: bool = True,
    include_hbb: bool = True,
) -> Path:
    """Write a synthetic S1 Excel with DISCOVER sheets matching the real format."""
    p = tmp_path / name

    # Default synthetic data
    if vegfa_data is None:
        vegfa_data = [_make_vegfa_ontarget_row(), _make_vegfa_data_row()]
    if rnf2_data is None:
        rnf2_data = [_make_rnf2_ontarget_row()]  # on-target only → 0 OTs
    if hbb_data is None:
        hbb_data = (
            [_make_hbb_ontarget_row(), _make_hbb_data_row()]
            + _hbb_separator_rows()
        )

    with pd.ExcelWriter(p, engine="openpyxl") as writer:
        if include_vegfa:
            raw_vegfa = pd.DataFrame(_vegfa_raw_rows(vegfa_data))
            raw_vegfa.to_excel(
                writer, sheet_name="DISCOVER VEGFA", index=False, header=False
            )
        if include_rnf2:
            raw_rnf2 = pd.DataFrame(_rnf2_raw_rows(rnf2_data))
            raw_rnf2.to_excel(
                writer, sheet_name="DISCOVER RNF2", index=False, header=False
            )
        if include_hbb:
            raw_hbb = pd.DataFrame(_hbb_raw_rows(hbb_data))
            raw_hbb.to_excel(
                writer, sheet_name="DISCOVER HBB", index=False, header=False
            )

    return p


# ===========================================================================
# _pick_col
# ===========================================================================

class TestPickCol:

    def test_finds_first_matching_alias(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"B": [1], "C": [2]})
        assert _pick_col(df, ["A", "B", "C"], "x") == "B"

    def test_prefers_earlier_alias(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"A": [1], "B": [2]})
        assert _pick_col(df, ["A", "B"], "x") == "A"

    def test_raises_when_no_alias_matches(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"X": [1]})
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Actual columns"):
            _pick_col(df, ["A", "B"], "myfield")

    def test_error_lists_tried_aliases(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"Z": [1]})
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Tried aliases"):
            _pick_col(df, ["alpha", "beta"], "foo")


# ===========================================================================
# _load_tabular
# ===========================================================================

class TestLoadTabular:

    def test_loads_tsv(self, tmp_path):
        p = tmp_path / "data.tsv"
        p.write_text("A\tB\n1\t2\n3\t4\n")
        df = _load_tabular(p)
        assert list(df.columns) == ["A", "B"]
        assert len(df) == 2

    def test_loads_csv(self, tmp_path):
        p = tmp_path / "data.csv"
        p.write_text("A,B\n1,2\n3,4\n")
        df = _load_tabular(p)
        assert list(df.columns) == ["A", "B"]
        assert len(df) == 2

    def test_loads_xlsx(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_xlsx(tmp_path, "data.xlsx", {"Sheet1": [{"A": 1, "B": 2}]})
        df = _load_tabular(p)
        assert "A" in df.columns
        assert len(df) == 1


# ===========================================================================
# _apply_normalise_chrom
# ===========================================================================

class TestApplyNormaliseChrom:

    def test_valid_chroms_pass_through(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"chrom": ["chr1", "chrX", "chr22"]})
        df, mask = _apply_normalise_chrom(df, "chrom")
        assert all(mask)
        assert list(df["chrom"]) == ["chr1", "chrX", "chr22"]

    def test_bare_numbers_get_chr_prefix(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"chrom": ["1", "22", "X"]})
        df, mask = _apply_normalise_chrom(df, "chrom")
        assert all(mask)
        assert df["chrom"].tolist() == ["chr1", "chr22", "chrX"]

    def test_invalid_contig_flagged_false(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"chrom": ["chr1", "scaffold_99999"]})
        df, mask = _apply_normalise_chrom(df, "chrom")
        assert bool(mask.iloc[0]) is True
        assert bool(mask.iloc[1]) is False

    def test_returns_same_length(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"chrom": ["chr1", "chr2", "bad"]})
        df, mask = _apply_normalise_chrom(df, "chrom")
        assert len(mask) == 3


# ===========================================================================
# parse_deepcrispr_k562 — two-sheet Excel format (actual Chuai 2018 file)
# ===========================================================================

class TestParseDeepCRISPRK562Excel:
    """Tests for the two-sheet Excel path (_parse_deepcrispr_twosheets)."""

    # --- Error handling ---

    def test_missing_file_raises_with_download_url(self, tmp_path):
        with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="genomebiology"):
            parse_deepcrispr_k562(tmp_path / "nonexistent.xlsx")

    # --- Happy path ---

    def test_basic_two_sheet_excel_correct_schema(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(tmp_path)
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        _assert_schema(df)
        assert len(df) == 1

    def test_metadata_fields(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(tmp_path)
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["cell_type"].iloc[0] == "K562"
        assert df["dataset"].iloc[0] == "deepcrispr_k562"
        assert df["genome_build"].iloc[0] == "hg38"

    def test_guide_id_is_target_id(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(
            tmp_path,
            target_rows=[_make_dc_target_row(tid="sg42")],
            ot_rows=[_make_dc_ot_row(tid="sg42")],
        )
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["guide_id"].iloc[0] == "sg42"

    # --- Sequence handling ---

    def test_guide_seq_trimmed_to_20nt_from_23nt_target_sgrna(self, tmp_path):
        """'Target sgRNA' is 23-nt; parser must take only first 20 nt."""
        p = _write_dc_excel(
            tmp_path,
            target_rows=[_make_dc_target_row(guide_seq_23=GUIDE + "NGG")],
            ot_rows=[_make_dc_ot_row(ot_seq=OT_1MM)],
        )
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert len(df["guide_seq"].iloc[0]) == 20
        assert df["guide_seq"].iloc[0] == GUIDE

    def test_23nt_ot_extracts_pam(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(
            tmp_path,
            ot_rows=[_make_dc_ot_row(ot_seq=OT_23NT)],
        )
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["pam_seq"].iloc[0] == "GGG"
        assert len(df["offtarget_seq"].iloc[0]) == 20

    def test_20nt_ot_gets_nnn_pam(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(
            tmp_path,
            ot_rows=[_make_dc_ot_row(ot_seq=OT_1MM)],
        )
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["pam_seq"].iloc[0] == "NNN"

    def test_offtarget_seq_normalised_to_uppercase(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(
            tmp_path,
            ot_rows=[_make_dc_ot_row(ot_seq=OT_1MM.lower())],
        )
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["offtarget_seq"].iloc[0] == OT_1MM

    # --- Mismatch count (computed from sequences in two-sheet format) ---

    def test_mismatch_count_computed_from_sequences(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(
            tmp_path,
            ot_rows=[_make_dc_ot_row(ot_seq=OT_2MM)],
        )
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["num_mismatches"].iloc[0] == 2

    # --- On-target filtering ---

    def test_on_target_row_removed(self, tmp_path):
        """Row where OT matches guide exactly (0 mismatches) must be dropped."""
        on_target_ot = _make_dc_ot_row(ot_id="sg1_on", ot_seq=GUIDE + "NGG")
        off_target_ot = _make_dc_ot_row(ot_id="sg1_ot1", ot_seq=OT_1MM)
        p = _write_dc_excel(tmp_path, ot_rows=[on_target_ot, off_target_ot])
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert df["num_mismatches"].iloc[0] > 0

    # --- Read count ---

    def test_read_count_equals_cleavage_frequency(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(
            tmp_path,
            ot_rows=[_make_dc_ot_row(freq=0.314)],
        )
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["read_count"].iloc[0] == pytest.approx(0.314)

    # --- Coordinates ---

    def test_coordinates_stored_correctly(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(
            tmp_path,
            ot_rows=[_make_dc_ot_row(chrom="chr7", start=5000, end=5023)],
        )
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["chrom"].iloc[0] == "chr7"
        assert df["start"].iloc[0] == 5000
        assert df["end"].iloc[0] == 5023

    # --- Multiple guides ---

    def test_multiple_guides_from_two_target_rows(self, tmp_path):
        targets = [
            _make_dc_target_row(tid="sg1"),
            _make_dc_target_row(tid="sg2"),
        ]
        ots = [
            _make_dc_ot_row(ot_id="sg1_ot1", tid="sg1", ot_seq=OT_1MM),
            _make_dc_ot_row(ot_id="sg1_ot2", tid="sg1", ot_seq=OT_2MM),
            _make_dc_ot_row(ot_id="sg2_ot1", tid="sg2", ot_seq=OT_1MM),
        ]
        p = _write_dc_excel(tmp_path, target_rows=targets, ot_rows=ots)
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert len(df) == 3
        assert df["guide_id"].nunique() == 2

    def test_unknown_target_id_in_ot_sheet_dropped(self, tmp_path):
        targets = [_make_dc_target_row(tid="sg1")]
        ots = [
            _make_dc_ot_row(tid="sg1", ot_seq=OT_1MM),
            _make_dc_ot_row(tid="sg99", ot_seq=OT_2MM),  # not in target sheet
        ]
        p = _write_dc_excel(tmp_path, target_rows=targets, ot_rows=ots)
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert df["guide_id"].iloc[0] == "sg1"

    # --- hg19 liftover triggered ---

    def test_hg19_triggers_liftover(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(tmp_path)
        lifted = ("chr1", 2000)
        with patch.object(_PD, "liftover_hg19_to_hg38", return_value=lifted):
            df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg19")
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert df["start"].iloc[0] == 2000

    def test_hg19_liftover_failure_drops_row(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_dc_excel(tmp_path)
        with patch.object(_PD, "liftover_hg19_to_hg38", return_value=None):
            df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(p, src_genome_build="hg19")
        assert len(df) == 0


# ===========================================================================
# parse_deepcrispr_k562 — flat-file CSV format (fallback path)
# ===========================================================================

class TestParseDeepCRISPRK562FlatFile:
    """Tests for the flat-file fallback path (CSV / TSV format)."""

    def test_missing_required_column_raises_with_actual_columns(self, tmp_path):
        # Row present but guide_seq and offtarget_seq are missing
        rows = [{
            "Target": "X", "Chromosome": "chr1",
            "Start": 0, "End": 23, "Strand": "+",
            "Cleavage_Frequency": 0.1, "Mismatches": 1,
        }]
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", rows)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Actual columns"):
            parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")

    def test_basic_valid_csv_produces_correct_schema(self, tmp_path):
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", [_make_dc_row()])
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")
        _assert_schema(df)
        assert len(df) == 1

    def test_k562_filter_removes_hek293_rows(self, tmp_path):
        rows = [
            _make_dc_row(cell_type="K562"),
            _make_dc_row(cell_type="HEK293", ot_seq=OT_2MM),
        ]
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", rows)
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert df["cell_type"].iloc[0] == "K562"

    def test_no_cell_type_col_keeps_all_rows(self, tmp_path):
        rows = [
            _make_dc_row(cell_type=None),
            _make_dc_row(cell_type=None, ot_seq=OT_2MM),
        ]
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", rows)
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert len(df) == 2

    def test_on_target_row_is_removed(self, tmp_path):
        on_target  = _make_dc_row(ot_seq=GUIDE, n_mm=0)
        off_target = _make_dc_row()
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", [on_target, off_target])
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert df["num_mismatches"].iloc[0] > 0

    def test_20nt_ot_seq_gets_nnn_pam(self, tmp_path):
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", [_make_dc_row(ot_seq=OT_1MM)])
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["pam_seq"].iloc[0] == "NNN"

    def test_23nt_ot_seq_extracts_pam(self, tmp_path):
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", [_make_dc_row(ot_seq=OT_23NT)])
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["pam_seq"].iloc[0] == "GGG"
        assert len(df["offtarget_seq"].iloc[0]) == 20

    def test_mismatch_count_from_column(self, tmp_path):
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", [_make_dc_row(n_mm=2)])
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["num_mismatches"].iloc[0] == 2

    def test_mismatch_computed_from_sequences_when_col_absent(self, tmp_path):
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", [_make_dc_row(n_mm=None)])
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["num_mismatches"].iloc[0] == 1

    def test_alternative_column_names_accepted(self, tmp_path):
        rows = [{
            "sgRNA":         "RNF2",
            "guide_seq":     GUIDE,
            "offtarget_seq": OT_2MM,
            "chrom":         "chr1",
            "start":         5000,
            "end":           5023,
            "strand":        "-",
            "score":         0.02,
            "Mismatches":    2,
        }]
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", rows)
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert df["guide_id"].iloc[0] == "RNF2"

    def test_read_count_equals_cleavage_freq(self, tmp_path):
        path = _write_csv(tmp_path, "dc.tsv", [_make_dc_row(freq=0.123)])
        df = parse_deepcrispr_k562(path, src_genome_build="hg38")
        assert df["read_count"].iloc[0] == pytest.approx(0.123)


# ===========================================================================
# parse_discover_seq_k562 — actual S1 three-sheet format
# ===========================================================================

class TestParseDiscoverSeqK562:

    # --- Error handling ---

    def test_missing_file_raises_with_download_url(self, tmp_path):
        with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="PMC6589096"):
            parse_discover_seq_k562(tmp_path / "nonexistent.xlsx")

    def test_missing_required_column_raises_with_actual_columns(self, tmp_path):
        """VEGFA sheet missing 'Guide sequence' → ValueError with actual cols."""
        bad_vegfa_rows = [
            # Data row: has coord but no Guide sequence column
            _vegfa_raw_rows([[
                "PAPD7", "chr5:6714989-6715008", 6715005, "sense",
                "NGG", "MISSING_GUIDE", 1, 100.0, 50.0
            ]])
        ]
        # Write a VEGFA sheet with wrong column names by using raw DataFrame
        p = tmp_path / "s1.xlsx"
        # Sheet with no 'Guide sequence' column at all
        raw = pd.DataFrame([
            ["Title"] + [None]*4,
            [None]*5,
            [None, "Chr:Start-End", "Cutsite", "Strand", "PAM"],  # missing Guide sequence
            ["PAPD7", "chr5:100-200", 150, "sense", "NGG"],
        ])
        with pd.ExcelWriter(p, engine="openpyxl") as w:
            raw.to_excel(w, sheet_name="DISCOVER VEGFA", index=False, header=False)
            pd.DataFrame([["RNF2"]]).to_excel(w, sheet_name="DISCOVER RNF2", index=False)
            pd.DataFrame([["HBB"]]).to_excel(w, sheet_name="DISCOVER HBB", index=False)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Actual columns"):
            parse_discover_seq_k562(p)

    def test_no_discover_sheets_raises_value_error(self, tmp_path):
        """File with no DISCOVER sheets at all → ValueError."""
        p = _write_xlsx(tmp_path, "s1.xlsx", {"Sheet1": [{"A": 1}]})
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            parse_discover_seq_k562(p)

    # --- Happy path: VEGFA sheet ---

    def test_vegfa_sheet_basic_output(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[_make_vegfa_ontarget_row(), _make_vegfa_data_row()],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert df["guide_id"].iloc[0] == "VEGFA"

    def test_vegfa_schema_correct(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[_make_vegfa_ontarget_row(), _make_vegfa_data_row()],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        _assert_schema(df)

    def test_metadata_fields(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[_make_vegfa_ontarget_row(), _make_vegfa_data_row()],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert df["cell_type"].iloc[0] == "K562"
        assert df["dataset"].iloc[0] == "discover_seq_k562"
        assert df["genome_build"].iloc[0] == "hg38"

    def test_vegfa_on_target_removed(self, tmp_path):
        """Row with Mismatches=0 must be dropped."""
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            # on-target (mismatches=0) + 1 off-target
            vegfa_data=[_make_vegfa_ontarget_row(), _make_vegfa_data_row()],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert df["num_mismatches"].iloc[0] > 0

    def test_vegfa_offtarget_seq_is_nnn_placeholder(self, tmp_path):
        """DISCOVER sheets have no genomic OT sequence → 'N'*20."""
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[_make_vegfa_ontarget_row(), _make_vegfa_data_row()],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert df["offtarget_seq"].iloc[0] == "N" * 20

    def test_vegfa_pam_from_column(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[
                _make_vegfa_ontarget_row(),
                _make_vegfa_data_row(pam="AGG"),
            ],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert df["pam_seq"].iloc[0] == "AGG"

    def test_vegfa_read_count_is_sum_of_reps(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[
                _make_vegfa_ontarget_row(),
                _make_vegfa_data_row(rep1=100.0, rep2=50.0),
            ],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert df["read_count"].iloc[0] == pytest.approx(150.0)

    def test_vegfa_guide_seq_trimmed_to_20nt(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[
                _make_vegfa_ontarget_row(),
                _make_vegfa_data_row(guide_seq=GUIDE + "NGG"),
            ],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert len(df["guide_seq"].iloc[0]) == 20
        assert df["guide_seq"].iloc[0] == GUIDE

    def test_vegfa_strand_normalised_sense_to_plus(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[
                _make_vegfa_ontarget_row(),
                _make_vegfa_data_row(strand="sense"),
            ],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert df["strand"].iloc[0] == "+"

    def test_vegfa_strand_normalised_antisense_to_minus(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[
                _make_vegfa_ontarget_row(),
                _make_vegfa_data_row(strand="antisense"),
            ],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert df["strand"].iloc[0] == "-"

    def test_vegfa_coordinate_parsed_from_string(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[
                _make_vegfa_ontarget_row(),
                _make_vegfa_data_row(coord="chr5:6714989-6715008"),
            ],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert df["chrom"].iloc[0] == "chr5"
        assert df["start"].iloc[0] == 6714989
        assert df["end"].iloc[0] == 6715008

    def test_vegfa_mismatch_count_from_column(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[
                _make_vegfa_ontarget_row(),
                _make_vegfa_data_row(mismatches=4),
            ],
            rnf2_data=[],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert df["num_mismatches"].iloc[0] == 4

    # --- RNF2 sheet: 0 off-targets ---

    def test_rnf2_returns_empty_after_filtering_on_targets(self, tmp_path):
        """RNF2 has only on-target (mismatches=0) → 0 rows returned."""
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[],
            rnf2_data=[_make_rnf2_ontarget_row()],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        # Only RNF2 sheet populated but it has 0 off-targets
        assert len(df) == 0
        _assert_schema(df)

    def test_rnf2_with_off_target_row_included(self, tmp_path):
        """If RNF2 actually has an off-target, it should appear in output."""
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[],
            rnf2_data=[
                _make_rnf2_ontarget_row(),
                _make_rnf2_data_row(mismatches=2),
            ],
            hbb_data=[],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert len(df) == 1
        assert df["guide_id"].iloc[0] == "RNF2"

    # --- HBB sheet: NaN separator rows ---

    def test_hbb_nan_separator_rows_dropped(self, tmp_path):
        """Rows with NaN in coordinate column (separator rows) must be dropped."""
        hbb_data = [
            _make_hbb_ontarget_row(),    # on-target (filtered)
            _make_hbb_data_row(),         # off-target WT (kept)
            *_hbb_separator_rows(),       # NaN rows (dropped)
            _make_hbb_data_row(cas9="HiFi", rep1=4.0, rep2=2.0),  # HiFi (kept)
        ]
        p = _write_discover_s1(tmp_path, vegfa_data=[], rnf2_data=[], hbb_data=hbb_data)
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert len(df) == 2
        assert df["guide_id"].unique().tolist() == ["HBB"]

    def test_hbb_read_count_is_sum_of_reps(self, tmp_path):
        hbb_data = [
            _make_hbb_ontarget_row(),
            _make_hbb_data_row(rep1=49.0, rep2=97.0),
        ]
        p = _write_discover_s1(tmp_path, vegfa_data=[], rnf2_data=[], hbb_data=hbb_data)
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert df["read_count"].iloc[0] == pytest.approx(146.0)

    # --- Combined sheets ---

    def test_multiple_guides_combined(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[_make_vegfa_ontarget_row(), _make_vegfa_data_row()],
            rnf2_data=[_make_rnf2_ontarget_row()],
            hbb_data=[_make_hbb_ontarget_row(), _make_hbb_data_row()],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert len(df) == 2
        assert set(df["guide_id"].unique()) == {"VEGFA", "HBB"}

    def test_schema_with_combined_sheets(self, tmp_path):
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[_make_vegfa_ontarget_row(), _make_vegfa_data_row()],
            rnf2_data=[_make_rnf2_ontarget_row()],
            hbb_data=[_make_hbb_ontarget_row(), _make_hbb_data_row()],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        _assert_schema(df)

    def test_all_three_guides_all_on_targets_returns_empty(self, tmp_path):
        """If all sheets have only on-target rows, output is empty."""
        p = _write_discover_s1(
            tmp_path,
            vegfa_data=[_make_vegfa_ontarget_row()],
            rnf2_data=[_make_rnf2_ontarget_row()],
            hbb_data=[_make_hbb_ontarget_row()],
        )
        df = parse_discover_seq_k562(p)
        assert len(df) == 0
        _assert_schema(df)


=== tests/test_alphagenome_client.py ===
"""Tests for src/alphagenome_client.py.

Test strategy
-------------
All tests in this file run without any network access or API key.  The
AlphaGenome SDK types are mimicked by lightweight mock objects that match
the attribute shapes produced by real API calls (verified on 2026-02-24).

Live integration tests (requiring a real API key) are in a separate block
marked ``@pytest.mark.live`` and skipped by default.  Run them with::

    pytest tests/test_alphagenome_client.py -m live

Mock output structure (confirmed from real K562 call)
------------------------------------------------------
- dnase.values  : (131072, 1)   — 1 bp resolution, 1 track
- atac.values   : (131072, 1)   — 1 bp resolution, 1 track
- chip_histone.values  : (1024, n_histone)  — 128 bp/bin
- chip_histone.metadata: DataFrame with 'name' column
- chip_tf.values       : (1024, n_tf)
- chip_tf.metadata     : DataFrame with 'name' column
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import NamedTuple
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from src.alphagenome_client import (
    BIN_SIZE,
    COURTESY_SLEEP_S,
    DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP,
    DNASE_OPEN_THRESHOLD,
    FEATURE_NAMES,
    INTERVAL_SIZE,
    N_BINS,
    NARROW_WINDOW_BP,
    TrackAvailability,
    _extract_bin_signal,
    _extract_bp_signal,
    _extract_chromatin_features,
    _nan_features,
    _predict_with_retry,
    _site_to_tile,
    batch_get_chromatin_features,
    get_chromatin_features,
    get_track_availability,
    list_cell_types,
)
from src.utils import GenomicInterval


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Mock helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _MockTrackData(NamedTuple):
    """Mimics the TrackData object returned by the AlphaGenome SDK."""
    values: np.ndarray
    metadata: pd.DataFrame


class _MockOutput:
    """Picklable mock of an AlphaGenome prediction output.

    Uses plain namedtuple TrackData objects with real numpy arrays and
    DataFrames — these pickle cleanly without the ``MagicMock`` problem.
    """
    __slots__ = ("dnase", "atac", "chip_histone", "chip_tf")

    def __init__(
        self,
        dnase: _MockTrackData,
        atac: _MockTrackData,
        chip_histone: _MockTrackData,
        chip_tf: _MockTrackData,
    ) -> None:
        self.dnase = dnase
        self.atac = atac
        self.chip_histone = chip_histone
        self.chip_tf = chip_tf


def _make_mock_output(
    dnase_shape: tuple[int, int] = (INTERVAL_SIZE, 1),
    atac_shape: tuple[int, int] = (INTERVAL_SIZE, 1),
    histone_marks: list[str] | None = None,
    has_ctcf: bool = True,
    seed: int = 42,
) -> _MockOutput:
    """Build a picklable mock AlphaGenome prediction output.

    Parameters
    ----------
    dnase_shape, atac_shape : tuple[int, int]
        Shape of DNase / ATAC arrays.
    histone_marks : list[str] | None
        Mark names to include; default is K562-like set (no H3K9me3).
    has_ctcf : bool
        Whether to include a CTCF TF track.
    seed : int
        RNG seed for reproducibility.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if histone_marks is None:
        # K562 actual marks — H3K9me3 is NOT present
        histone_marks = [
            "H2AFZ", "H3K27ac", "H3K27me3", "H3K36me3",
            "H3K4me1", "H3K4me2", "H3K4me3", "H3K79me2",
            "H3K9ac", "H4K20me1",
        ]

    # DNase / ATAC: 1-bp resolution, values in [0, 15)
    dnase_vals = rng.uniform(0, 15, size=dnase_shape).astype(np.float32)
    atac_vals = rng.uniform(0, 10, size=atac_shape).astype(np.float32)

    # Plant a clearly high-signal spike at position 65536 (middle of interval)
    spike_pos = INTERVAL_SIZE // 2
    dnase_vals[spike_pos, 0] = 40.0
    atac_vals[spike_pos, 0] = 30.0

    # Histone: 128-bp bin resolution
    n_histone = len(histone_marks)
    histone_vals = rng.uniform(0, 8, size=(N_BINS, n_histone)).astype(np.float32)
    histone_meta = pd.DataFrame({
        "name": [f"EFO:0002067 Histone ChIP-seq {m}" for m in histone_marks],
        "ontology_curie": ["EFO:0002067"] * n_histone,
    })

    # TF ChIP: 128-bp bin resolution, include CTCF if requested
    tf_names = ["EFO:0002067 TF ChIP-seq CTCF"] if has_ctcf else ["EFO:0002067 TF ChIP-seq OTHER"]
    n_tf = len(tf_names)
    tf_vals = rng.uniform(0, 5, size=(N_BINS, n_tf)).astype(np.float32)
    tf_meta = pd.DataFrame({
        "name": tf_names,
        "ontology_curie": ["EFO:0002067"] * n_tf,
    })

    return _MockOutput(
        dnase=_MockTrackData(values=dnase_vals, metadata=pd.DataFrame()),
        atac=_MockTrackData(values=atac_vals, metadata=pd.DataFrame()),
        chip_histone=_MockTrackData(values=histone_vals, metadata=histone_meta),
        chip_tf=_MockTrackData(values=tf_vals, metadata=tf_meta),
    )


def _full_avail(ontology_term: str = "EFO:0002067") -> TrackAvailability:
    """Return a TrackAvailability representing K562.

    Matches confirmed K562 track inventory: DNase, ATAC, 10 histone marks
    (H3K9me3 is absent), and CTCF.
    """
    return TrackAvailability(
        ontology_term=ontology_term,
        has_dnase=True,
        has_atac=True,
        histone_marks=[
            # H3K9me3 intentionally absent — not available for K562 in AlphaGenome
            "H2AFZ", "H3K27ac", "H3K27me3", "H3K36me3",
            "H3K4me1", "H3K4me2", "H3K4me3", "H3K79me2",
            "H3K9ac", "H4K20me1",
        ],
        has_ctcf=True,
    )


def _no_tracks_avail(ontology_term: str = "UNKNOWN:0000000") -> TrackAvailability:
    """Return a TrackAvailability with no tracks (e.g. U2OS)."""
    return TrackAvailability(
        ontology_term=ontology_term,
        has_dnase=False,
        has_atac=False,
        histone_marks=[],
        has_ctcf=False,
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Constants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestConstants:
    def test_interval_size_is_valid_power_of_two(self):
        # 131072 = 2**17
        assert INTERVAL_SIZE == 131_072
        assert math.log2(INTERVAL_SIZE) == int(math.log2(INTERVAL_SIZE))

    def test_bin_size_divides_interval_evenly(self):
        assert INTERVAL_SIZE % BIN_SIZE == 0
        assert INTERVAL_SIZE // BIN_SIZE == N_BINS

    def test_feature_names_complete(self):
        assert len(FEATURE_NAMES) == 12
        expected = {
            "ag_dnase_mean", "ag_dnase_max", "ag_dnase_at_site",
            "ag_dnase_mean_narrow", "ag_atac_mean", "ag_atac_at_site",
            "ag_h3k4me3_mean", "ag_h3k27me3_mean", "ag_h3k9me3_mean",
            "ag_h3k27ac_mean", "ag_ctcf_mean", "ag_accessibility_binary",
        }
        assert set(FEATURE_NAMES) == expected

    def test_feature_names_no_duplicates(self):
        assert len(FEATURE_NAMES) == len(set(FEATURE_NAMES))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TrackAvailability
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestTrackAvailability:
    def test_has_any_tracks_true_when_dnase(self):
        avail = TrackAvailability("X", has_dnase=True, has_atac=False,
                                  histone_marks=[], has_ctcf=False)
        assert avail.has_any_tracks is True

    def test_has_any_tracks_true_when_histone(self):
        avail = TrackAvailability("X", has_dnase=False, has_atac=False,
                                  histone_marks=["H3K4me3"], has_ctcf=False)
        assert avail.has_any_tracks is True

    def test_has_any_tracks_false_when_empty(self):
        avail = _no_tracks_avail()
        assert avail.has_any_tracks is False

    def test_requested_output_types_k562(self):
        from alphagenome.models import dna_client
        avail = _full_avail()
        types = avail.requested_output_types
        assert dna_client.OutputType.DNASE in types
        assert dna_client.OutputType.ATAC in types
        assert dna_client.OutputType.CHIP_HISTONE in types
        assert dna_client.OutputType.CHIP_TF in types
        assert len(types) == 4

    def test_requested_output_types_no_dnase_atac(self):
        from alphagenome.models import dna_client
        avail = TrackAvailability("EFO:0001182", has_dnase=False, has_atac=False,
                                  histone_marks=["H3K4me3"], has_ctcf=True)
        types = avail.requested_output_types
        assert dna_client.OutputType.DNASE not in types
        assert dna_client.OutputType.ATAC not in types
        assert dna_client.OutputType.CHIP_HISTONE in types
        assert dna_client.OutputType.CHIP_TF in types

    def test_requested_output_types_empty(self):
        avail = _no_tracks_avail()
        assert avail.requested_output_types == []


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _site_to_tile
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestSiteToTile:
    def test_tile_start_aligned_to_interval_size(self):
        chrom, ts, te = _site_to_tile(200_000, "chr1")
        assert ts % INTERVAL_SIZE == 0

    def test_tile_length_is_interval_size(self):
        _, ts, te = _site_to_tile(0, "chr1")
        assert te - ts == INTERVAL_SIZE

    def test_site_at_zero(self):
        chrom, ts, te = _site_to_tile(0, "chr1")
        assert ts == 0
        assert te == INTERVAL_SIZE

    def test_site_at_tile_boundary(self):
        # Exactly at a boundary → belongs to the NEXT tile
        site_pos = INTERVAL_SIZE
        _, ts, te = _site_to_tile(site_pos, "chr1")
        assert ts == INTERVAL_SIZE
        assert te == 2 * INTERVAL_SIZE

    def test_site_in_middle_of_tile(self):
        site_pos = INTERVAL_SIZE + INTERVAL_SIZE // 4
        _, ts, te = _site_to_tile(site_pos, "chr1")
        assert ts == INTERVAL_SIZE
        assert te == 2 * INTERVAL_SIZE

    def test_site_offset_within_tile(self):
        site_pos = INTERVAL_SIZE + 5000
        _, ts, _ = _site_to_tile(site_pos, "chr1")
        assert site_pos - ts == 5000

    def test_chrom_passthrough(self):
        chrom, _, _ = _site_to_tile(100, "chr7")
        assert chrom == "chr7"

    def test_large_position(self):
        site_pos = 200_000_000
        chrom, ts, te = _site_to_tile(site_pos, "chr1")
        assert ts % INTERVAL_SIZE == 0
        assert ts <= site_pos < te
        assert te - ts == INTERVAL_SIZE


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _extract_bp_signal
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestExtractBpSignal:
    def _uniform_signal(self, value: float, length: int = INTERVAL_SIZE, n_tracks: int = 1) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.full((length, n_tracks), value, dtype=np.float32)

    def test_mean_of_uniform_signal(self):
        signal = self._uniform_signal(3.0)
        mean, _, _, _ = _extract_bp_signal(signal, 65536, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP, NARROW_WINDOW_BP)
        assert pytest.approx(mean, abs=1e-4) == 3.0

    def test_max_of_uniform_signal(self):
        signal = self._uniform_signal(3.0)
        _, sig_max, _, _ = _extract_bp_signal(signal, 65536, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP, NARROW_WINDOW_BP)
        assert pytest.approx(sig_max, abs=1e-4) == 3.0

    def test_at_site_exact_value(self):
        signal = np.zeros((INTERVAL_SIZE, 1), dtype=np.float32)
        signal[65536, 0] = 99.0
        _, _, at_site, _ = _extract_bp_signal(signal, 65536, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP, NARROW_WINDOW_BP)
        assert pytest.approx(at_site, abs=1e-4) == 99.0

    def test_spike_captured_by_max(self):
        signal = np.zeros((INTERVAL_SIZE, 1), dtype=np.float32)
        signal[65536, 0] = 50.0
        _, sig_max, _, _ = _extract_bp_signal(signal, 65536, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP, NARROW_WINDOW_BP)
        assert sig_max == pytest.approx(50.0, abs=1e-4)

    def test_multi_track_averaging(self):
        # Two tracks with values 2.0 and 4.0 → mean of means = 3.0
        signal = np.stack([
            np.full(INTERVAL_SIZE, 2.0),
            np.full(INTERVAL_SIZE, 4.0),
        ], axis=1).astype(np.float32)
        mean, _, _, _ = _extract_bp_signal(signal, 65536, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP, NARROW_WINDOW_BP)
        assert pytest.approx(mean, abs=1e-4) == 3.0

    def test_boundary_clamping_near_start(self):
        signal = self._uniform_signal(1.0)
        # site_offset = 10, window_bp = 500 → window clamps at 0
        mean, _, _, _ = _extract_bp_signal(signal, 10, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP, NARROW_WINDOW_BP)
        assert not math.isnan(mean)

    def test_narrow_mean_smaller_window(self):
        # All zeros except the spike at offset 65536; narrow mean should include it
        signal = np.zeros((INTERVAL_SIZE, 1), dtype=np.float32)
        signal[65536, 0] = 10.0
        wide_mean, _, _, narrow_mean = _extract_bp_signal(
            signal, 65536, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP, NARROW_WINDOW_BP
        )
        # Spike raises narrow_mean more than wide_mean
        assert narrow_mean > wide_mean

    def test_out_of_bounds_offset_returns_nan(self):
        signal = self._uniform_signal(1.0, length=100, n_tracks=1)
        _, _, at_site, _ = _extract_bp_signal(signal, 200, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP, NARROW_WINDOW_BP)
        assert math.isnan(at_site)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _extract_bin_signal
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestExtractBinSignal:
    def _uniform_bins(self, value: float, n_tracks: int = 1) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.full((N_BINS, n_tracks), value, dtype=np.float32)

    def test_uniform_signal_returns_value(self):
        signal = self._uniform_bins(5.0)
        result = _extract_bin_signal(signal, INTERVAL_SIZE // 2, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP)
        assert pytest.approx(result, abs=1e-4) == 5.0

    def test_track_mask_selects_correct_column(self):
        signal = np.zeros((N_BINS, 3), dtype=np.float32)
        signal[:, 1] = 7.0  # only column 1 has signal
        mask = np.array([False, True, False])
        result = _extract_bin_signal(signal, INTERVAL_SIZE // 2, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP, mask)
        assert pytest.approx(result, abs=1e-4) == 7.0

    def test_empty_mask_returns_nan(self):
        signal = self._uniform_bins(1.0, n_tracks=2)
        mask = np.array([False, False])
        result = _extract_bin_signal(signal, INTERVAL_SIZE // 2, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP, mask)
        assert math.isnan(result)

    def test_site_at_tile_start_uses_first_bin(self):
        signal = self._uniform_bins(3.0)
        result = _extract_bin_signal(signal, 0, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP)
        assert not math.isnan(result)

    def test_site_offset_maps_to_correct_bin(self):
        signal = np.zeros((N_BINS, 1), dtype=np.float32)
        site_offset = 512 * BIN_SIZE  # maps to bin 512
        # Set entire signal to 0 except a narrow region around bin 512
        signal[511:513, 0] = 10.0
        result = _extract_bin_signal(signal, site_offset, DEFAULT_WINDOW_BP)
        assert result > 0.0

class TestNanFeatures:
    def test_all_keys_present(self):
        feats = _nan_features()
        assert set(feats.keys()) == set(FEATURE_NAMES)

    def test_all_values_nan(self):
        feats = _nan_features()
        assert all(math.isnan(v) for v in feats.values())


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _extract_chromatin_features
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestExtractChromatinFeatures:
    def test_k562_output_all_features_present(self):
        output = _make_mock_output()
        avail = _full_avail()
        feats = _extract_chromatin_features(output, 65536, avail)
        assert set(feats.keys()) == set(FEATURE_NAMES)

    def test_k562_no_nan_features_except_h3k9me3(self):
        """K562 mock has all tracks except H3K9me3, which is absent in AlphaGenome."""
        output = _make_mock_output()
        avail = _full_avail()
        feats = _extract_chromatin_features(output, 65536, avail)
        for name, val in feats.items():
            if name == "ag_h3k9me3_mean":
                assert math.isnan(val), "H3K9me3 should be NaN for K562"
            else:
                assert not math.isnan(val), f"{name} is NaN but should not be"

    def test_dnase_at_site_matches_spike(self):
        output = _make_mock_output(seed=99)
        # Spike is planted at position 65536 = INTERVAL_SIZE // 2
        avail = _full_avail()
        feats = _extract_chromatin_features(output, INTERVAL_SIZE // 2, avail)
        assert feats["ag_dnase_at_site"] == pytest.approx(40.0, abs=1e-3)

    def test_no_dnase_atac_returns_nan_dnase(self):
        output = _make_mock_output()
        avail = TrackAvailability(
            "EFO:0001182", has_dnase=False, has_atac=False,
            histone_marks=["H3K4me3"], has_ctcf=False,
        )
        feats = _extract_chromatin_features(output, 65536, avail)
        assert math.isnan(feats["ag_dnase_mean"])
        assert math.isnan(feats["ag_atac_mean"])
        assert math.isnan(feats["ag_accessibility_binary"])
        # Histone should still work
        assert not math.isnan(feats["ag_h3k4me3_mean"])

    def test_no_tracks_returns_all_nan(self):
        output = _make_mock_output()
        avail = _no_tracks_avail()
        feats = _extract_chromatin_features(output, 65536, avail)
        for name, val in feats.items():
            assert math.isnan(val), f"{name} should be NaN for no-tracks cell type"

    def test_accessibility_binary_high_signal(self):
        # Force dnase mean >> threshold by using all-high signal
        signal = np.full((INTERVAL_SIZE, 1), DNASE_OPEN_THRESHOLD * 5, dtype=np.float32)
        output = _make_mock_output()
        output.dnase = _MockTrackData(
            values=signal,
            metadata=pd.DataFrame(),
        )
        avail = _full_avail()
        feats = _extract_chromatin_features(output, 65536, avail)
        assert feats["ag_accessibility_binary"] == 1.0

    def test_accessibility_binary_low_signal(self):
        signal = np.full((INTERVAL_SIZE, 1), 0.0, dtype=np.float32)
        output = _make_mock_output()
        output.dnase = _MockTrackData(values=signal, metadata=pd.DataFrame())
        avail = _full_avail()
        feats = _extract_chromatin_features(output, 65536, avail)
        assert feats["ag_accessibility_binary"] == 0.0

    def test_missing_histone_mark_returns_nan(self):
        """H3K9me3 is absent from K562 — that column should be NaN."""
        output = _make_mock_output()   # K562 mock has no H3K9me3
        avail = _full_avail()
        feats = _extract_chromatin_features(output, 65536, avail)
        assert math.isnan(feats["ag_h3k9me3_mean"])

    def test_no_ctcf_returns_nan(self):
        output = _make_mock_output(has_ctcf=False)
        avail = TrackAvailability(
            "EFO:0002067", has_dnase=True, has_atac=True,
            histone_marks=["H3K4me3"], has_ctcf=False,
        )
        feats = _extract_chromatin_features(output, 65536, avail)
        assert math.isnan(feats["ag_ctcf_mean"])

    def test_narrow_mean_present_and_finite(self):
        output = _make_mock_output()
        avail = _full_avail()
        feats = _extract_chromatin_features(output, 65536, avail)
        assert not math.isnan(feats["ag_dnase_mean_narrow"])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _predict_with_retry (mock model)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestPredictWithRetry:
    def _make_interval(self):
        from alphagenome.data import genome as ag_genome
        return ag_genome.Interval(chromosome="chr1", start=0, end=INTERVAL_SIZE)

    def test_succeeds_on_first_attempt(self):
        mock_model = MagicMock()
        sentinel = object()
        mock_model.predict_interval.return_value = sentinel
        interval = self._make_interval()
        result = _predict_with_retry(mock_model, interval, [], ["EFO:0002067"])
        assert result is sentinel
        mock_model.predict_interval.assert_called_once()

    def test_retries_on_rate_limit_and_succeeds(self):
        mock_model = MagicMock()
        sentinel = object()
        mock_model.predict_interval.side_effect = [
            Exception("rate limit exceeded"),
            sentinel,
        ]
        interval = self._make_interval()
        with patch("time.sleep"):
            result = _predict_with_retry(mock_model, interval, [], ["EFO:0002067"],
                                         max_attempts=3)
        assert result is sentinel
        assert mock_model.predict_interval.call_count == 2

    def test_raises_after_max_attempts(self):
        mock_model = MagicMock()
        mock_model.predict_interval.side_effect = Exception("persistent error")
        interval = self._make_interval()
        with patch("time.sleep"):
            with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="attempts failed"):
                _predict_with_retry(mock_model, interval, [], ["EFO:0002067"],
                                    max_attempts=3)
        assert mock_model.predict_interval.call_count == 3

    def test_429_triggers_rate_limit_path(self):
        mock_model = MagicMock()
        sentinel = object()
        mock_model.predict_interval.side_effect = [
            Exception("HTTP 429 too many requests"),
            sentinel,
        ]
        interval = self._make_interval()
        sleep_calls = []
        with patch("time.sleep", side_effect=lambda s: sleep_calls.append(s)):
            _predict_with_retry(mock_model, interval, [], ["EFO:0002067"],
                                max_attempts=3)
        assert len(sleep_calls) >= 1
