# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _query_region
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestQueryRegion:
    def _make_bw(
        self,
        chrom_len: int = 1_000_000,
        signal: float | None = 3.0,
    ) -> _MockBigWig:
        return _MockBigWig({"chr1": chrom_len}, signal)

    def test_normal_query(self) -> None:
        bw = self._make_bw(signal=4.0)
        arr = _query_region(bw, "chr1", 1000, 1100)
        assert len(arr) == 100
        assert np.all(arr == 4.0)

    def test_none_becomes_nan(self) -> None:
        bw = self._make_bw(signal=None)
        arr = _query_region(bw, "chr1", 1000, 1100)
        assert len(arr) == 100
        assert np.all(np.isnan(arr))

    def test_missing_chrom_returns_nan(self) -> None:
        bw = self._make_bw()
        arr = _query_region(bw, "chr99", 1000, 2000)
        assert len(arr) == 1000
        assert np.all(np.isnan(arr))

    def test_empty_range_returns_empty(self) -> None:
        bw = self._make_bw()
        arr = _query_region(bw, "chr1", 500, 500)
        assert len(arr) == 0

    def test_start_at_zero(self) -> None:
        bw = self._make_bw(signal=7.0)
        arr = _query_region(bw, "chr1", 0, 200)
        assert len(arr) == 200
        assert np.all(arr == 7.0)

    def test_clamp_near_chrom_start(self) -> None:
        """Site with start < 0 should be NaN-padded on the left."""
        bw = self._make_bw(chrom_len=1_000_000, signal=2.0)
        # Request [-100, 100): only [0, 100) is valid
        arr = _query_region(bw, "chr1", -100, 100)
        assert len(arr) == 200
        # First 100 positions (chrom boundary region) → NaN
        assert np.all(np.isnan(arr[:100]))
        # Last 100 positions (valid) → 2.0
        assert np.all(arr[100:] == 2.0)

    def test_clamp_near_chrom_end(self) -> None:
        """Request that extends past chromosome end should be NaN-padded."""
        chrom_len = 500
        bw = self._make_bw(chrom_len=chrom_len, signal=9.0)
        # Request [400, 600): only [400, 500) is valid
        arr = _query_region(bw, "chr1", 400, 600)
        assert len(arr) == 200
        assert np.all(arr[:100] == 9.0)
        assert np.all(np.isnan(arr[100:]))

    def test_entirely_past_chrom_end(self) -> None:
        bw = self._make_bw(chrom_len=100, signal=1.0)
        arr = _query_region(bw, "chr1", 200, 300)
        assert len(arr) == 100
        assert np.all(np.isnan(arr))

    def test_dtype_is_float64(self) -> None:
        bw = self._make_bw(signal=1.0)
        arr = _query_region(bw, "chr1", 0, 10)
        assert arr.dtype == np.float64


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _extract_signal_stats
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestExtractSignalStats:
    def test_uniform_signal(self) -> None:
        arr = np.full(1000, 5.0)
        mean_v, max_v, at_site = _extract_signal_stats(arr, 500, 200)
        assert math.isclose(mean_v, 5.0)
        assert math.isclose(max_v, 5.0)
        assert math.isclose(at_site, 5.0)

    def test_at_site_value(self) -> None:
        arr = np.zeros(1000)
        arr[500] = 99.0
        _, _, at_site = _extract_signal_stats(arr, 500, 10)
        assert math.isclose(at_site, 99.0)

    def test_max_captures_peak(self) -> None:
        arr = np.full(1000, 1.0)
        arr[600] = 50.0
        mean_v, max_v, _ = _extract_signal_stats(arr, 600, 200)
        assert math.isclose(max_v, 50.0)
        assert mean_v < 50.0

    def test_all_nan_returns_nan(self) -> None:
        arr = np.full(100, np.nan)
        mean_v, max_v, at_site = _extract_signal_stats(arr, 50, 20)
        assert math.isnan(mean_v)
        assert math.isnan(max_v)
        assert math.isnan(at_site)

    def test_empty_array(self) -> None:
        arr = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
        mean_v, max_v, at_site = _extract_signal_stats(arr, 0, 100)
        assert math.isnan(mean_v)
        assert math.isnan(max_v)
        assert math.isnan(at_site)

    def test_site_offset_out_of_bounds(self) -> None:
        arr = np.full(100, 3.0)
        _, _, at_site = _extract_signal_stats(arr, 200, 50)
        assert math.isnan(at_site)

    def test_site_at_edge_zero(self) -> None:
        arr = np.full(100, 2.0)
        mean_v, max_v, at_site = _extract_signal_stats(arr, 0, 10)
        assert not math.isnan(at_site)
        assert math.isclose(at_site, 2.0)

    def test_window_clamps_to_array(self) -> None:
        """Window larger than array should not raise."""
        arr = np.full(50, 3.0)
        mean_v, max_v, at_site = _extract_signal_stats(arr, 25, 500)
        assert math.isclose(mean_v, 3.0)
        assert math.isclose(max_v, 3.0)

    def test_nan_ignored_in_mean(self) -> None:
        arr = np.array([1.0, np.nan, 3.0, np.nan, 5.0])
        mean_v, _, _ = _extract_signal_stats(arr, 2, 10)
        assert math.isclose(mean_v, 3.0)  # nanmean of [1, 3, 5]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# _extract_from_open_bws
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestExtractFromOpenBws:
    def _bw(
        self, signal: float | None = 5.0, chrom_len: int = 10_000_000
    ) -> _MockBigWig:
        return _MockBigWig({"chr7": chrom_len}, signal)

    def test_dnase_features_populated(self) -> None:
        open_bws = {"DNase": self._bw(signal=6.0)}
        feats = _extract_from_open_bws(open_bws, "chr7", 500_000, 500)
        assert not math.isnan(feats["exp_dnase_mean"])
        assert math.isclose(feats["exp_dnase_mean"], 6.0)
        assert math.isclose(feats["exp_dnase_max"], 6.0)
        assert math.isclose(feats["exp_dnase_at_site"], 6.0)

    def test_histones_populated(self) -> None:
        open_bws = {
            "H3K4me3": self._bw(signal=3.0),
            "H3K27me3": self._bw(signal=1.0),
            "H3K9me3": self._bw(signal=0.5),
            "H3K27ac": self._bw(signal=4.0),
        }
        feats = _extract_from_open_bws(open_bws, "chr7", 500_000, 500)
        assert math.isclose(feats["exp_h3k4me3_mean"], 3.0)
        assert math.isclose(feats["exp_h3k27me3_mean"], 1.0)
        assert math.isclose(feats["exp_h3k9me3_mean"], 0.5)
        assert math.isclose(feats["exp_h3k27ac_mean"], 4.0)

    def test_missing_assays_are_nan(self) -> None:
        """Only DNase present — histone features should be NaN."""
        open_bws = {"DNase": self._bw(signal=2.0)}
        feats = _extract_from_open_bws(open_bws, "chr7", 500_000, 500)
        assert math.isnan(feats["exp_h3k4me3_mean"])
        assert math.isnan(feats["exp_h3k27me3_mean"])
        assert math.isnan(feats["exp_h3k9me3_mean"])
        assert math.isnan(feats["exp_h3k27ac_mean"])

    def test_empty_bws_all_nan(self) -> None:
        feats = _extract_from_open_bws({}, "chr7", 500_000, 500)
        for v in feats.values():
            assert math.isnan(v)

    def test_accessibility_binary_above_threshold(self) -> None:
        signal = DNASE_OPEN_THRESHOLD + 1.0
        open_bws = {"DNase": self._bw(signal=signal)}
        feats = _extract_from_open_bws(open_bws, "chr7", 500_000, 500)
        assert feats["exp_accessibility_binary"] == 1.0

    def test_accessibility_binary_below_threshold(self) -> None:
        signal = DNASE_OPEN_THRESHOLD - 0.5
        open_bws = {"DNase": self._bw(signal=signal)}
        feats = _extract_from_open_bws(open_bws, "chr7", 500_000, 500)
        assert feats["exp_accessibility_binary"] == 0.0

    def test_accessibility_binary_nan_dnase(self) -> None:
        """All-NaN DNase signal → mean=NaN → accessibility binary = 0 (NaN > thresh is False)."""
        open_bws = {"DNase": self._bw(signal=None)}
        feats = _extract_from_open_bws(open_bws, "chr7", 500_000, 500)
        # nan > DNASE_OPEN_THRESHOLD evaluates to False in Python → 0.0
        assert feats["exp_accessibility_binary"] == 0.0

    def test_accessibility_binary_nan_when_no_dnase(self) -> None:
        """No DNase assay in open_bws → accessibility binary stays NaN."""
        open_bws = {"H3K4me3": self._bw(signal=5.0)}
        feats = _extract_from_open_bws(open_bws, "chr7", 500_000, 500)
        assert math.isnan(feats["exp_accessibility_binary"])

    def test_site_near_chrom_start(self) -> None:
        """Site near position 0 should not raise; boundary handled by _query_region."""
        open_bws = {"DNase": self._bw(signal=2.0)}
        feats = _extract_from_open_bws(open_bws, "chr7", 100, 500)
        # Should produce a value (not crash), though window is truncated
        assert not math.isnan(feats["exp_dnase_mean"])

    def test_result_keys_match_feature_names(self) -> None:
        open_bws = {"DNase": self._bw(), "H3K4me3": self._bw()}
        feats = _extract_from_open_bws(open_bws, "chr7", 500_000, 500)
        assert set(feats.keys()) == set(FEATURE_NAMES)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# get_chromatin_features (single-site, with patched _open_bigwig)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestGetChromatinFeatures:
    def _make_site(self, pos: int = 5_000_000) -> GenomicInterval:
        return GenomicInterval(chrom="chr7", start=pos, end=pos + 23)

    def test_no_files_returns_nan(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        manifest = build_manifest(tmp_path)  # empty dir
        feats = get_chromatin_features(self._make_site(), "K562", manifest)
        for v in feats.values():
            assert math.isnan(v)

    def test_wrong_cell_type_returns_nan(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        feats = get_chromatin_features(self._make_site(), "U2OS", k562_manifest)
        for v in feats.values():
            assert math.isnan(v)

    def test_correct_values_from_mock(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=4.5)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            feats = get_chromatin_features(self._make_site(), "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert math.isclose(feats["exp_dnase_mean"], 4.5)
        assert math.isclose(feats["exp_h3k4me3_mean"], 4.5)

    def test_chrom_normalisation(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        """Site with '7' (no chr prefix) should work via normalize_chrom."""
        site = GenomicInterval(chrom="7", start=5_000_000, end=5_000_023)
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=3.0)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            feats = get_chromatin_features(site, "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert not math.isnan(feats["exp_dnase_mean"])

    def test_all_feature_keys_present(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=1.0)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            feats = get_chromatin_features(self._make_site(), "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert set(feats.keys()) == set(FEATURE_NAMES)

    def test_bw_files_are_closed(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        """BigWig handles must be closed even on success."""
        closed_handles: list[_MockBigWig] = []

        def tracking_open(path: Path) -> _MockBigWig:
            bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, 2.0)
            closed_handles.append(bw)
            return bw

        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", side_effect=tracking_open):
            get_chromatin_features(self._make_site(), "K562", k562_manifest)

        for bw in closed_handles:
            assert bw.closed, "BigWig handle not closed after get_chromatin_features"

    def test_open_error_returns_nan_for_that_assay(
        self, tmp_path: Path
    ) -> None:
        """If one BigWig file cannot be opened, only that assay is NaN."""
        # Only DNase file exists
        (tmp_path / "K562_DNase_hg38.bigWig").write_bytes(b"")
        manifest = build_manifest(tmp_path)

        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=5.0)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            feats = get_chromatin_features(self._make_site(), "K562", manifest)

        assert not math.isnan(feats["exp_dnase_mean"])
        assert math.isnan(feats["exp_h3k4me3_mean"])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# batch_get_chromatin_features
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestBatchGetChromatinFeatures:
    def _make_sites(self, n: int = 5) -> pd.DataFrame:
        return pd.DataFrame({
            "chrom": ["chr7"] * n,
            "start": [i * 1_000_000 + 5_000_000 for i in range(n)],
            "guide_id": [f"g{i}" for i in range(n)],
        })

    def test_no_files_returns_nan_columns(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        manifest = build_manifest(tmp_path)  # empty
        sites = self._make_sites()
        result = batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", manifest)
        for feat in FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat in result.columns
            assert result[feat].isna().all()

    def test_wrong_cell_type_all_nan(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        sites = self._make_sites()
        result = batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "HEK293", k562_manifest)
        for feat in FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert result[feat].isna().all()

    def test_correct_values(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        sites = self._make_sites(3)
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=7.0)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            result = batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert (result["exp_dnase_mean"] == 7.0).all()
        assert (result["exp_h3k4me3_mean"] == 7.0).all()

    def test_preserves_input_columns(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        sites = self._make_sites(2)
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=1.0)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            result = batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert "guide_id" in result.columns
        assert list(result["guide_id"]) == list(sites["guide_id"])

    def test_row_order_preserved(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        sites = self._make_sites(4)
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=3.0)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            result = batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert list(result["start"]) == list(sites["start"])

    def test_output_has_feature_columns(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        sites = self._make_sites(2)
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=2.0)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            result = batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)
        for feat in FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat in result.columns

    def test_bw_files_opened_once(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        """Each BigWig file must be opened exactly once per batch call."""
        open_counts: dict[str, int] = {}

        def counting_open(path: Path) -> _MockBigWig:
            key = path.name
            open_counts[key] = open_counts.get(key, 0) + 1
            return _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, 1.0)

        sites = self._make_sites(10)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", side_effect=counting_open):
            batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)

        for count in open_counts.values():
            assert count == 1, f"File opened {count} times; expected 1"

    def test_bw_files_closed_after_batch(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        closed_handles: list[_MockBigWig] = []

        def tracking_open(path: Path) -> _MockBigWig:
            bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, 1.0)
            closed_handles.append(bw)
            return bw

        sites = self._make_sites(3)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", side_effect=tracking_open):
            batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)

        assert len(closed_handles) > 0
        for bw in closed_handles:
            assert bw.closed

    def test_single_site(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        sites = self._make_sites(1)
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=9.0)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            result = batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert len(result) == 1
        assert math.isclose(result["exp_dnase_mean"].iloc[0], 9.0)

    def test_empty_dataframe(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        sites = pd.DataFrame({"chrom": [], "start": []})
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=1.0)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            result = batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert len(result) == 0
        for feat in FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat in result.columns

    def test_input_dataframe_not_mutated(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        sites = self._make_sites(3)
        original_cols = list(sites.columns)
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, constant_signal=1.0)
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert list(sites.columns) == original_cols

    def test_accessibility_binary_threshold(
        self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest
    ) -> None:
        """Sites above/below threshold should get 1/0 respectively."""
        sites = pd.DataFrame({
            "chrom": ["chr7", "chr7"],
            "start": [5_000_000, 6_000_000],
        })
        signals = [DNASE_OPEN_THRESHOLD + 1.0, DNASE_OPEN_THRESHOLD - 0.5]
        call_idx = [0]

        def signal_bw(path: Path) -> _MockBigWig:
            sig = signals[call_idx[0] % 2]
            call_idx[0] += 1
            return _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, sig)

        # Use a single signal value BigWig for both sites
        mock_bw_high = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, DNASE_OPEN_THRESHOLD + 1.0)
        mock_bw_low = _MockBigWig({"chr7": 159_345_973}, DNASE_OPEN_THRESHOLD - 0.5)

        # Test high signal → binary = 1
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw_high):
            result_high = batch_get_chromatin_features(
                sites.head(1), "K562", k562_manifest, show_progress=False
            )
        assert result_high["exp_accessibility_binary"].iloc[0] == 1.0

        # Test low signal → binary = 0
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw_low):
            result_low = batch_get_chromatin_features(
                sites.tail(1), "K562", k562_manifest, show_progress=False
            )
        assert result_low["exp_accessibility_binary"].iloc[0] == 0.0

    def test_mixed_chromosomes(self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest) -> None:
        sites = pd.DataFrame({
            "chrom": ["chr7", "chr12", "chrX"],
            "start": [5_000_000, 3_000_000, 1_000_000],
        })
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig(
            {"chr7": 159_345_973, "chr12": 133_275_309, "chrX": 156_040_895},
            constant_signal=2.5,
        )
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            result = batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert (result["exp_dnase_mean"] == 2.5).all()

    def test_chrom_normalisation_in_batch(
        self, k562_manifest: BigWigManifest
    ) -> None:
        """DataFrame with unnormalised chrom names should work."""
        sites = pd.DataFrame({
            "chrom": ["7", "chr12"],  # "7" missing chr prefix
            "start": [5_000_000, 3_000_000],
        })
        mock_bw = _MockBigWig(
            {"chr7": 159_345_973, "chr12": 133_275_309}, constant_signal=1.0
        )
        with patch("src.encode_client._open_bigwig", return_value=mock_bw):
            result = batch_get_chromatin_features(sites, "K562", k562_manifest)
        assert not result["exp_dnase_mean"].isna().any()


=== tests/test_feature_matrix.py ===
"""Tests for src/feature_matrix.py.

Test strategy
-------------
- No AlphaGenome API calls or BigWig file reads occur.
- AlphaGenome and ENCODE batch functions are patched where needed.
- Sequence features are computed for real (small synthetic guides).
- All label assignment, imputation, and assembly logic is tested against
  controlled DataFrames with known expected outputs.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from src.feature_matrix import (
    AG_FEATURE_NAMES,
    DEFAULT_MATCH_WINDOW_BP,
    EXP_FEATURE_NAMES,
    METADATA_COLS,
    MISSING_INDICATOR_SUFFIX,
    MM_TYPE_TO_INT,
    MODEL_A_FEATURES,
    MODEL_B_FEATURES,
    MODEL_C_FEATURES,
    SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES,
    build_feature_matrix,
    extract_sequence_features_batch,
    get_model_features,
    impute_chromatin_features,
    label_candidates,
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Synthetic data helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Valid 20-nt guide sequences for testing.
_GUIDE = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGAA"
_OFFTARGET_1MM = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAACAA"  # 1 mismatch at pos 3 from 3' end
_OFFTARGET_2MM = "GAGTCCGAGCAGAAGAAGCC"  # 2 mismatches
_PAM = "TGG"


def _make_candidates(
    n: int = 5,
    chrom: str = "chr7",
    guide_id: str = "VEGFA_1",
    start_base: int = 5_000_000,
    num_mismatches: int = 1,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Return a small synthetic candidates DataFrame."""
    return pd.DataFrame({
        "guide_id": [guide_id] * n,
        "guide_seq": [_GUIDE] * n,
        "chrom": [chrom] * n,
        "start": [start_base + i * 1000 for i in range(n)],
        "strand": ["+"] * n,
        "off_target_seq": [_OFFTARGET_1MM] * n,
        "pam_seq": [_PAM] * n,
        "num_mismatches": [num_mismatches] * n,
    })


def _make_positives(
    chrom: str = "chr7",
    guide_id: str = "VEGFA_1",
    starts: list[int] | None = None,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Return a small synthetic positives DataFrame."""
    if starts is None:
        starts = [5_000_000]
    return pd.DataFrame({
        "guide_id": [guide_id] * len(starts),
        "chrom": [chrom] * len(starts),
        "start": starts,
        "read_count": [100] * len(starts),
    })


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TestConstants
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestConstants:
    def test_sequence_feature_names_count(self) -> None:
        # 3 scalar + 20 mm_pos + 20 mm_type + 6 aggregates = 49
        assert len(SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES) == 49

    def test_mm_type_to_int_match_is_zero(self) -> None:
        assert MM_TYPE_TO_INT[""] == 0

    def test_mm_type_to_int_has_12_mismatch_types(self) -> None:
        mismatch_values = {v for k, v in MM_TYPE_TO_INT.items() if k != ""}
        assert mismatch_values == set(range(1, 13))

    def test_model_a_equals_sequence_features(self) -> None:
        assert MODEL_A_FEATURES == SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES

    def test_model_b_contains_exp_features(self) -> None:
        for feat in EXP_FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat in MODEL_B_FEATURES

    def test_model_c_contains_ag_features(self) -> None:
        for feat in AG_FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat in MODEL_C_FEATURES

    def test_default_match_window_is_5(self) -> None:
        assert DEFAULT_MATCH_WINDOW_BP == 5

    def test_missing_suffix(self) -> None:
        assert MISSING_INDICATOR_SUFFIX == "_missing"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TestGetModelFeatures
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestGetModelFeatures:
    def test_model_a(self) -> None:
        feats = get_model_features("A")
        assert feats == SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES

    def test_model_b(self) -> None:
        feats = get_model_features("B")
        assert set(SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES).issubset(feats)
        assert set(EXP_FEATURE_NAMES).issubset(feats)
        assert not any(f in feats for f in AG_FEATURE_NAMES)

    def test_model_c(self) -> None:
        feats = get_model_features("C")
        assert set(SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES).issubset(feats)
        assert set(AG_FEATURE_NAMES).issubset(feats)
        assert not any(f in feats for f in EXP_FEATURE_NAMES)

    def test_case_insensitive(self) -> None:
        assert get_model_features("a") == get_model_features("A")
        assert get_model_features("b") == get_model_features("B")
        assert get_model_features("c") == get_model_features("C")

    def test_invalid_raises(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="model_type"):
            get_model_features("D")

    def test_model_b_with_indicators(self) -> None:
        feats = get_model_features("B", include_missingness_indicators=True)
        for f in EXP_FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert f + "_missing" in feats

    def test_model_c_with_indicators(self) -> None:
        feats = get_model_features("C", include_missingness_indicators=True)
        for f in AG_FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert f + "_missing" in feats

    def test_model_a_no_indicators_even_if_requested(self) -> None:
        feats = get_model_features("A", include_missingness_indicators=True)
        # Model A has no chromatin features, so no _missing columns added.
        assert not any(f.endswith("_missing") for f in feats)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TestLabelCandidates
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestLabelCandidates:
    def test_exact_position_match(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=2)
        # starts: 5_000_000, 5_001_000 — positive at exactly 5_000_000
        pos = _make_positives(starts=[5_000_000])
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos)
        assert result["label"].iloc[0] == 1
        assert result["label"].iloc[1] == 0

    def test_within_window_is_positive(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1)
        cands["start"] = [5_000_003]
        pos = _make_positives(starts=[5_000_000])
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos, match_window_bp=5)
        assert result["label"].iloc[0] == 1

    def test_just_outside_window_is_negative(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1)
        cands["start"] = [5_000_006]
        pos = _make_positives(starts=[5_000_000])
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos, match_window_bp=5)
        assert result["label"].iloc[0] == 0

    def test_exactly_at_window_boundary_is_positive(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1)
        cands["start"] = [5_000_005]
        pos = _make_positives(starts=[5_000_000])
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos, match_window_bp=5)
        assert result["label"].iloc[0] == 1

    def test_different_guide_id_no_match(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1, guide_id="VEGFA_1")
        pos = _make_positives(guide_id="VEGFA_2", starts=[5_000_000])
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos)
        assert result["label"].iloc[0] == 0

    def test_different_chrom_no_match(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1, chrom="chr7")
        pos = _make_positives(chrom="chr12", starts=[5_000_000])
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos)
        assert result["label"].iloc[0] == 0

    def test_empty_candidates(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=0)
        pos = _make_positives()
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos)
        assert len(result) == 0
        assert "label" in result.columns

    def test_empty_positives(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=3)
        pos = _make_positives(starts=[])
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos)
        assert (result["label"] == 0).all()

    def test_all_candidates_are_negative(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=5, start_base=1_000_000)
        pos = _make_positives(starts=[9_000_000])
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos)
        assert result["label"].sum() == 0

    def test_multiple_positives_any_match(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1)
        cands["start"] = [5_000_000]
        pos = _make_positives(starts=[4_000_000, 5_000_000, 6_000_000])
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos)
        assert result["label"].iloc[0] == 1

    def test_chrom_normalisation(self) -> None:
        """'7' in candidates and 'chr7' in positives should still match."""
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1, chrom="7")
        pos = _make_positives(chrom="chr7", starts=[5_000_000])
        result = label_candidates(cands, pos)
        assert result["label"].iloc[0] == 1

    def test_input_not_mutated(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=3)
        pos = _make_positives()
        orig_cols = list(cands.columns)
        label_candidates(cands, pos)
        assert list(cands.columns) == orig_cols

    def test_label_counts_logged(self, caplog) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=4)
        cands["start"] = [5_000_000, 5_000_001, 6_000_000, 7_000_000]
        pos = _make_positives(starts=[5_000_000])
        with caplog.at_level("INFO"):
            result = label_candidates(cands, pos)
        assert result["label"].sum() == 2  # both within default window=5


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TestExtractSequenceFeaturesBatch
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestExtractSequenceFeaturesBatch:
    def test_returns_all_feature_columns(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=2)
        result = extract_sequence_features_batch(cands)
        for feat in SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat in result.columns, f"Missing: {feat}"

    def test_cfd_score_in_range(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=3)
        result = extract_sequence_features_batch(cands)
        assert (result["cfd_score"] >= 0).all()
        assert (result["cfd_score"] <= 1).all()

    def test_mit_score_nonnegative(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=3)
        result = extract_sequence_features_batch(cands)
        assert (result["mit_score"] >= 0).all()

    def test_mm_type_columns_are_integers(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=2)
        result = extract_sequence_features_batch(cands)
        for i in range(1, 21):
            col = f"mm_type_{i}"
            assert pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(result[col]), f"{col} not numeric"

    def test_mm_type_match_position_is_zero(self) -> None:
        """Positions with no mismatch should encode as 0."""
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1)
        result = extract_sequence_features_batch(cands)
        # Identify matching positions from mm_pos columns
        for i in range(1, 21):
            if result[f"mm_pos_{i}"].iloc[0] == 0:
                assert result[f"mm_type_{i}"].iloc[0] == 0

    def test_num_mismatches_matches_mm_pos_sum(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1)
        result = extract_sequence_features_batch(cands)
        mm_pos_sum = sum(result[f"mm_pos_{i}"].iloc[0] for i in range(1, 21))
        assert int(result["num_mismatches"].iloc[0]) == int(mm_pos_sum)

    def test_preserves_input_columns(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=2)
        orig_cols = list(cands.columns)
        result = extract_sequence_features_batch(cands)
        for col in orig_cols:
            assert col in result.columns

    def test_bad_sequence_gets_nan(self) -> None:
        """Rows with sequences that fail validation should get NaN features."""
        cands = _make_candidates(n=2)
        cands.loc[cands.index[0], "off_target_seq"] = "BADSEQ"  # too short
        result = extract_sequence_features_batch(cands)
        # First row should be NaN
        assert math.isnan(result["cfd_score"].iloc[0])
        # Second row should be valid
        assert not math.isnan(result["cfd_score"].iloc[1])

    def test_pam_match_canonical(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1)
        cands["pam_seq"] = ["TGG"]
        result = extract_sequence_features_batch(cands)
        assert result["pam_match"].iloc[0] == 1

    def test_pam_match_noncanonical(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=1)
        cands["pam_seq"] = ["TAA"]
        result = extract_sequence_features_batch(cands)
        assert result["pam_match"].iloc[0] == 0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TestImpute
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestImpute:
    def test_partial_nan_filled_with_median(self) -> None:
        df = pd.DataFrame({"feat": [1.0, 2.0, np.nan, 4.0, np.nan]})
        result = impute_chromatin_features(df, ["feat"])
        # Median of [1, 2, 4] = 2.0
        assert result["feat"].isna().sum() == 0
        assert result["feat"].iloc[2] == pytest.approx(2.0)
        assert result["feat"].iloc[4] == pytest.approx(2.0)

    def test_non_nan_values_unchanged(self) -> None:
        df = pd.DataFrame({"feat": [1.0, 2.0, np.nan, 4.0]})
        result = impute_chromatin_features(df, ["feat"])
        assert result["feat"].iloc[0] == pytest.approx(1.0)
        assert result["feat"].iloc[1] == pytest.approx(2.0)
        assert result["feat"].iloc[3] == pytest.approx(4.0)

    def test_missingness_indicator_added(self) -> None:
        df = pd.DataFrame({"feat": [1.0, np.nan, 3.0]})
        result = impute_chromatin_features(df, ["feat"])
        assert "feat_missing" in result.columns
        assert result["feat_missing"].iloc[0] == 0
        assert result["feat_missing"].iloc[1] == 1
        assert result["feat_missing"].iloc[2] == 0

    def test_no_nan_values_unchanged(self) -> None:
        df = pd.DataFrame({"feat": [1.0, 2.0, 3.0]})
        result = impute_chromatin_features(df, ["feat"])
        assert list(result["feat"]) == pytest.approx([1.0, 2.0, 3.0])
        assert (result["feat_missing"] == 0).all()

    def test_all_nan_filled_with_zero(self) -> None:
        df = pd.DataFrame({"feat": [np.nan, np.nan, np.nan]})
        result = impute_chromatin_features(df, ["feat"])
        assert result["feat"].isna().sum() == 0
        assert (result["feat"] == 0.0).all()

    def test_missing_column_skipped(self) -> None:
        df = pd.DataFrame({"feat": [1.0, 2.0]})
        # Should not raise even though "nonexistent" is not in df.
        result = impute_chromatin_features(df, ["nonexistent"])
        assert list(result.columns) == ["feat"]

    def test_multiple_features(self) -> None:
        df = pd.DataFrame({
            "feat_a": [1.0, np.nan, 3.0],
            "feat_b": [np.nan, 2.0, np.nan],
        })
        result = impute_chromatin_features(df, ["feat_a", "feat_b"])
        assert result["feat_a"].isna().sum() == 0
        assert result["feat_b"].isna().sum() == 0
        assert "feat_a_missing" in result.columns
        assert "feat_b_missing" in result.columns

    def test_input_not_mutated(self) -> None:
        df = pd.DataFrame({"feat": [1.0, np.nan, 3.0]})
        orig = df.copy()
        impute_chromatin_features(df, ["feat"])
        pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(df, orig)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# TestBuildFeatureMatrix
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TestBuildFeatureMatrix:
    """Tests for build_feature_matrix — sequence-only path (no API calls)."""

    def _base_candidates(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """5 candidates, guide_id VEGFA_1, positions 5M–5.004M, 1 mismatch each."""
        return _make_candidates(n=5, num_mismatches=1)

    def _base_positives(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
        return _make_positives(starts=[5_000_000])

    def _add_on_target(self, cands: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Append a row with num_mismatches=0 (on-target)."""
        on_target = cands.iloc[[0]].copy()
        on_target["num_mismatches"] = 0
        return pd.concat([cands, on_target], ignore_index=True)

    # ---- Validation -------------------------------------------------------

    def test_missing_required_column_raises(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates().drop(columns=["guide_seq"])
        pos = self._base_positives()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="guide_seq"):
            build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")

    def test_ag_model_without_ontology_raises(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        mock_model = MagicMock()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ag_ontology_term"):
            build_feature_matrix(
                cands, pos, cell_type="K562",
                ag_model=mock_model, ag_cache_dir=Path("/tmp"),
            )

    def test_ag_model_without_cache_dir_raises(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        mock_model = MagicMock()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ag_cache_dir"):
            build_feature_matrix(
                cands, pos, cell_type="K562",
                ag_model=mock_model, ag_ontology_term="EFO:0002067",
            )

    # ---- On-target removal ------------------------------------------------

    def test_on_target_removed(self) -> None:
        cands = self._add_on_target(self._base_candidates())
        pos = self._base_positives()
        result = build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        assert (result["num_mismatches"] > 0).all()

    def test_on_target_count_logged(self, caplog) -> None:
        cands = self._add_on_target(self._base_candidates())
        pos = self._base_positives()
        with caplog.at_level("INFO"):
            build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        assert any("on-target" in msg.lower() for msg in caplog.messages)

    # ---- Label assignment -------------------------------------------------

    def test_label_column_present(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        result = build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        assert "label" in result.columns

    def test_correct_positive_count(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = _make_positives(starts=[5_000_000, 5_001_000])
        result = build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        # Two candidates fall within window of two positives
        assert result["label"].sum() == 2

    # ---- Sequence features ------------------------------------------------

    def test_sequence_features_present(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        result = build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        for feat in SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat in result.columns, f"Missing: {feat}"

    def test_cfd_score_in_range(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        result = build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        assert (result["cfd_score"] >= 0).all()
        assert (result["cfd_score"] <= 1).all()

    # ---- Metadata ---------------------------------------------------------

    def test_cell_type_column(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        result = build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        assert (result["cell_type"] == "K562").all()

    def test_dataset_column_default(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        result = build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        assert "dataset" in result.columns

    def test_preserves_extra_candidate_columns(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        cands["custom_col"] = "extra"
        pos = self._base_positives()
        result = build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        assert "custom_col" in result.columns

    # ---- AlphaGenome integration (mocked) ---------------------------------

    def test_ag_features_added_when_model_provided(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        mock_model = MagicMock()

        # Build a fake AG output: input DataFrame + ag_* columns
        def fake_ag_batch(sites, cell_type, model, cache_dir, **kwargs):
            out = sites.copy()
            for feat in AG_FEATURE_NAMES:
                out[feat] = 1.0
            return out

        with patch("src.feature_matrix._ag_mod.batch_get_chromatin_features", side_effect=fake_ag_batch):
            result = build_feature_matrix(
                cands, pos, cell_type="K562",
                ag_model=mock_model,
                ag_ontology_term="EFO:0002067",
                ag_cache_dir=tmp_path,
            )

        for feat in AG_FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat in result.columns
            assert (result[feat] == 1.0).all()

    def test_ag_missingness_indicators_added(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        mock_model = MagicMock()

        def fake_ag_batch(sites, cell_type, model, cache_dir, **kwargs):
            out = sites.copy()
            for feat in AG_FEATURE_NAMES:
                out[feat] = np.nan  # all NaN → imputed
            return out

        with patch("src.feature_matrix._ag_mod.batch_get_chromatin_features", side_effect=fake_ag_batch):
            result = build_feature_matrix(
                cands, pos, cell_type="K562",
                ag_model=mock_model,
                ag_ontology_term="EFO:0002067",
                ag_cache_dir=tmp_path,
            )

        for feat in AG_FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat + "_missing" in result.columns
            # All were NaN → all indicators should be 1
            assert (result[feat + "_missing"] == 1).all()

    # ---- ENCODE integration (mocked) --------------------------------------

    def test_encode_features_added_when_manifest_provided(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        mock_manifest = MagicMock()

        def fake_enc_batch(sites, cell_type, manifest, **kwargs):
            out = sites.copy()
            for feat in EXP_FEATURE_NAMES:
                out[feat] = 2.0
            return out

        with patch("src.feature_matrix._enc_mod.batch_get_chromatin_features", side_effect=fake_enc_batch):
            result = build_feature_matrix(
                cands, pos, cell_type="K562",
                encode_manifest=mock_manifest,
            )

        for feat in EXP_FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat in result.columns
            assert (result[feat] == 2.0).all()

    def test_encode_missingness_indicators_added(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        mock_manifest = MagicMock()

        def fake_enc_batch(sites, cell_type, manifest, **kwargs):
            out = sites.copy()
            for feat in EXP_FEATURE_NAMES:
                out[feat] = np.nan
            return out

        with patch("src.feature_matrix._enc_mod.batch_get_chromatin_features", side_effect=fake_enc_batch):
            result = build_feature_matrix(
                cands, pos, cell_type="K562",
                encode_manifest=mock_manifest,
            )

        for feat in EXP_FEATURE_NAMES:
            assert feat + "_missing" in result.columns

    # ---- Parquet save -----------------------------------------------------

    def test_saves_parquet(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        out_path = tmp_path / "fm.parquet"
        build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562", output_path=out_path)
        assert out_path.exists()
        loaded = pd.read_parquet(out_path)
        assert "label" in loaded.columns
        assert "cfd_score" in loaded.columns

    def test_creates_parent_dirs(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = self._base_positives()
        out_path = tmp_path / "nested" / "dir" / "fm.parquet"
        build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562", output_path=out_path)
        assert out_path.exists()

    # ---- Edge cases -------------------------------------------------------

    def test_all_on_target_returns_empty(self) -> None:
        cands = _make_candidates(n=3, num_mismatches=0)
        pos = self._base_positives()
        result = build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        assert len(result) == 0

    def test_zero_positives_all_negative(self) -> None:
        cands = self._base_candidates()
        pos = _make_positives(starts=[])
        result = build_feature_matrix(cands, pos, cell_type="K562")
        assert (result["label"] == 0).all()


=== tests/test_model.py ===
"""Tests for src/model.py.

Test strategy
-------------
- Metric helpers (compute_metrics, precision_at_k, etc.) are tested against
  controlled inputs with known expected values.
- XGBoost is trained for real on small synthetic datasets (fast: ~100 rows,
  10 estimators).  This validates the full CV pipeline without mocking.
- ``compare_models`` uses CVResult objects with fabricated fold metrics to
  test statistical comparisons.
- ``run_three_model_comparison`` is tested with a minimal synthetic feature
  matrix using n_splits=2 and n_estimators=10.
- No external API calls, BigWig files, or filesystem access are required.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from src.feature_matrix import (
    AG_FEATURE_NAMES,
    EXP_FEATURE_NAMES,
    MODEL_A_FEATURES,
    MODEL_B_FEATURES,
    MODEL_C_FEATURES,
    SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES,
)
from src.model import (
    CVResult,
    DEFAULT_N_SPLITS,
    DEFAULT_XGB_PARAMS,
    FoldMetrics,
    MODEL_BC_CELL_TYPES,
    ModelComparison,
    TOP_FRACTION,
    compare_models,
    compute_metrics,
    compute_scale_pos_weight,
    cross_validate_model,
    enrichment_at_top_fraction,
    make_xgb_classifier,
    precision_at_k,
    prepare_model_data,
    recall_at_k,
    run_ablation,
    run_three_model_comparison,
)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Synthetic data helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_FAST_XGB = {"n_estimators": 10, "max_depth": 3}


def _make_synthetic_feature_matrix(
    n_rows: int = 120,
    n_guides: int = 6,
    seed: int = 42,
    include_ag: bool = True,
    include_exp: bool = True,
    cell_types: list[str] | None = None,
    datasets: list[str] | None = None,
    pos_rate: float = 0.10,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Build a realistic synthetic feature matrix for testing.

    Each guide has at least one positive label to ensure GroupKFold can
    produce valid folds with positives in both train and test sets.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if cell_types is None:
        cell_types = ["K562"] * 50 + ["HEK293"] * 40 + ["T cells"] * 30
    if datasets is None:
        datasets = ["guide_seq"] * 80 + ["change_seq"] * 40

    # Trim/repeat to match n_rows.
    ct_arr = (cell_types * (n_rows // len(cell_types) + 1))[:n_rows]
    ds_arr = (datasets * (n_rows // len(datasets) + 1))[:n_rows]

    guide_ids = [f"guide_{i % n_guides}" for i in range(n_rows)]

    # Labels: at least 1 positive per guide, then ~pos_rate overall.
    labels = np.zeros(n_rows, dtype=np.int32)
    for g in range(n_guides):
        guide_idx = [i for i in range(n_rows) if i % n_guides == g]
        labels[guide_idx[0]] = 1  # guaranteed positive per guide

    # Random additional positives.
    extra = rng.random(n_rows) < pos_rate
    labels = np.clip(labels + extra.astype(np.int32), 0, 1)

    df = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "guide_id": guide_ids,
            "label": labels,
            "chrom": "chr1",
            "start": rng.integers(1_000_000, 5_000_000, size=n_rows),
            "strand": "+",
            "cell_type": ct_arr,
            "dataset": ds_arr,
        }
    )

    # Sequence features (all SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES).
    for col in SEQUENCE_FEATURE_NAMES:
        if "mm_type" in col:
            df[col] = rng.integers(0, 13, size=n_rows)
        elif col == "num_mismatches":
            df[col] = rng.integers(1, 5, size=n_rows)  # >0 (no on-targets)
        elif col in (
            "seed_mismatches",
            "nonseed_mismatches",
            "consecutive_mm",
        ) or "mm_pos" in col:
            df[col] = rng.integers(0, 4, size=n_rows)
        elif col in ("pam_match",):
            df[col] = rng.integers(0, 2, size=n_rows)
        else:
            df[col] = rng.random(n_rows).astype(np.float32)

    # AlphaGenome features.
    if include_ag:
        for col in AG_FEATURE_NAMES:
            df[col] = (rng.random(n_rows) * 10).astype(np.float32)

    # ENCODE experimental features.
    if include_exp:
        for col in EXP_FEATURE_NAMES:
            df[col] = (rng.random(n_rows) * 10).astype(np.float32)

    return df


def _make_cv_result(
    model_type: str,
    fold_auprcs: list[float],
    fold_aurocs: list[float] | None = None,
    n_test: int = 20,
    n_pos_test: int = 2,
) -> CVResult:
    """Build a CVResult with fabricated fold metrics (no XGBoost needed)."""
    if fold_aurocs is None:
        fold_aurocs = [0.8] * len(fold_auprcs)

    folds = [
        FoldMetrics(
            fold=i,
            n_train=80,
            n_test=n_test,
            n_pos_test=n_pos_test,
            auprc=ap,
            auroc=ar,
            precision_at_top=0.5,
            recall_at_top=0.5,
            enrichment_at_top=2.0,
        )
        for i, (ap, ar) in enumerate(zip(fold_auprcs, fold_aurocs))
    ]

    n_folds = len(fold_auprcs)
    valid_ap = [v for v in fold_auprcs if not math.isnan(v)]

    dummy_n = n_test * n_folds
    dummy_y = np.zeros(dummy_n, dtype=np.int32)
    dummy_y[:n_pos_test * n_folds] = 1
    dummy_score = np.linspace(0, 1, dummy_n)[::-1]

    return CVResult(
        model_type=model_type,
        feature_cols=["f1", "f2"],
        folds=folds,
        oof_y_true=dummy_y,
        oof_y_score=dummy_score,
        final_model=MagicMock(),
        mean_auprc=float(np.mean(valid_ap)) if valid_ap else float("nan"),
        std_auprc=float(np.std(valid_ap)) if len(valid_ap) > 1 else 0.0,
        mean_auroc=float(np.mean(fold_aurocs)),
        std_auroc=float(np.std(fold_aurocs)) if len(fold_aurocs) > 1 else 0.0,
    )


# ===========================================================================
# compute_scale_pos_weight
# ===========================================================================

class TestComputeScalePosWeight:
    def test_basic_ratio(self):
        y = np.array([0, 0, 0, 1], dtype=np.int32)
        assert compute_scale_pos_weight(y) == pytest.approx(3.0)

    def test_balanced(self):
        y = np.array([0, 1, 0, 1], dtype=np.int32)
        assert compute_scale_pos_weight(y) == pytest.approx(1.0)

    def test_no_positives_returns_one(self):
        y = np.zeros(10, dtype=np.int32)
        assert compute_scale_pos_weight(y) == pytest.approx(1.0)

    def test_all_positives(self):
        y = np.ones(5, dtype=np.int32)
        # n_neg=0, n_pos=5 → 0 / 5 = 0.0
        assert compute_scale_pos_weight(y) == pytest.approx(0.0)

    def test_large_imbalance(self):
        y = np.array([1] + [0] * 99)
        assert compute_scale_pos_weight(y) == pytest.approx(99.0)


# ===========================================================================
# precision_at_k
# ===========================================================================

class TestPrecisionAtK:
    def _scores(self):
        y_true = np.array([1, 0, 1, 0, 0])
        y_score = np.array([0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.3, 0.1])
        return y_true, y_score

    def test_top_1_is_correct(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._scores()
        # Top-1 score is 0.9 → label 1 → precision=1.0
        assert precision_at_k(y_true, y_score, k=1) == pytest.approx(1.0)

    def test_top_2(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._scores()
        # Top-2: scores 0.9 (label 1) and 0.8 (label 0) → precision=0.5
        assert precision_at_k(y_true, y_score, k=2) == pytest.approx(0.5)

    def test_top_3(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._scores()
        # Top-3: 0.9→1, 0.8→0, 0.7→1 → precision=2/3
        assert precision_at_k(y_true, y_score, k=3) == pytest.approx(2 / 3)

    def test_k_zero(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._scores()
        assert precision_at_k(y_true, y_score, k=0) == pytest.approx(0.0)

    def test_k_exceeds_length(self):
        y_true = np.array([1, 0])
        y_score = np.array([0.9, 0.1])
        # k=10 capped to 2; 1/2 = 0.5
        assert precision_at_k(y_true, y_score, k=10) == pytest.approx(0.5)

    def test_all_correct(self):
        y_true = np.array([1, 1, 1])
        y_score = np.array([0.9, 0.8, 0.7])
        assert precision_at_k(y_true, y_score, k=3) == pytest.approx(1.0)


# ===========================================================================
# recall_at_k
# ===========================================================================

class TestRecallAtK:
    def _scores(self):
        # 2 positives at index 0 and 2
        y_true = np.array([1, 0, 1, 0, 0])
        y_score = np.array([0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.3, 0.1])
        return y_true, y_score

    def test_top_1_recalls_half(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._scores()
        # Top-1: index 0 (label 1) → recall = 1/2
        assert recall_at_k(y_true, y_score, k=1) == pytest.approx(0.5)

    def test_top_3_recalls_all(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._scores()
        # Top-3 contains both positives → recall = 1.0
        assert recall_at_k(y_true, y_score, k=3) == pytest.approx(1.0)

    def test_no_positives_returns_zero(self):
        y_true = np.zeros(5, dtype=np.int32)
        y_score = np.array([0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.3, 0.1])
        assert recall_at_k(y_true, y_score, k=3) == pytest.approx(0.0)

    def test_k_exceeds_length(self):
        y_true = np.array([1, 0, 1])
        y_score = np.array([0.9, 0.5, 0.1])
        assert recall_at_k(y_true, y_score, k=100) == pytest.approx(1.0)


# ===========================================================================
# enrichment_at_top_fraction
# ===========================================================================

class TestEnrichmentAtTopFraction:
    def test_perfect_ranking_gives_high_enrichment(self):
        # All positives at top: 10% of 100 = 10 sites, 10 positives.
        y_true = np.array([1] * 10 + [0] * 90)
        y_score = np.array(list(range(100, 0, -1)), dtype=float)
        # overall rate = 10/100 = 0.10; top-10 precision = 10/10 = 1.0
        # enrichment = 1.0 / 0.10 = 10.0
        enr = enrichment_at_top_fraction(y_true, y_score, fraction=0.10)
        assert enr == pytest.approx(10.0)

    def test_random_ranking_gives_enrichment_near_one(self):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
        y_true = (rng.random(1000) < 0.10).astype(int)
        y_score = rng.random(1000)
        enr = enrichment_at_top_fraction(y_true, y_score, fraction=0.10)
        # Expected ~1.0; allow generous tolerance for small-sample randomness.
        assert 0.3 < enr < 3.0

    def test_no_positives(self):
        y_true = np.zeros(50, dtype=int)
        y_score = np.arange(50, dtype=float)
        assert enrichment_at_top_fraction(y_true, y_score) == pytest.approx(0.0)

    def test_empty_array(self):
        assert enrichment_at_top_fraction(np.array([]), np.array([])) == pytest.approx(0.0)


# ===========================================================================
# compute_metrics
# ===========================================================================

class TestComputeMetrics:
    def _perfect(self, n_pos=10, n_neg=90):
        y_true = np.array([1] * n_pos + [0] * n_neg)
        # Perfect: positives ranked first.
        y_score = np.array(
            [1.0] * n_pos + [0.0] * n_neg, dtype=float
        )
        return y_true, y_score

    def test_perfect_classifier_auprc(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._perfect()
        m = compute_metrics(y_true, y_score)
        assert m["auprc"] == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=1e-6)

    def test_perfect_classifier_auroc(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._perfect()
        m = compute_metrics(y_true, y_score)
        assert m["auroc"] == pytest.approx(1.0, abs=1e-6)

    def test_random_classifier_auprc_near_prevalence(self):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
        y_true = (rng.random(1000) < 0.05).astype(int)
        y_score = rng.random(1000)
        m = compute_metrics(y_true, y_score)
        assert 0.01 < m["auprc"] < 0.30  # near prevalence 5%

    def test_only_negatives_returns_nan(self):
        y_true = np.zeros(50, dtype=int)
        y_score = np.ones(50)
        m = compute_metrics(y_true, y_score)
        assert math.isnan(m["auprc"])
        assert math.isnan(m["auroc"])

    def test_only_positives_returns_nan(self):
        y_true = np.ones(50, dtype=int)
        y_score = np.ones(50)
        m = compute_metrics(y_true, y_score)
        assert math.isnan(m["auprc"])
        assert math.isnan(m["auroc"])

    def test_metric_keys_present(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._perfect()
        m = compute_metrics(y_true, y_score)
        for key in ("auprc", "auroc", "precision_at_top", "recall_at_top", "enrichment_at_top"):
            assert key in m

    def test_precision_recall_in_range(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._perfect()
        m = compute_metrics(y_true, y_score)
        assert 0.0 <= m["precision_at_top"] <= 1.0
        assert 0.0 <= m["recall_at_top"] <= 1.0
        assert m["enrichment_at_top"] >= 0.0

    def test_top_fraction_parameter(self):
        y_true, y_score = self._perfect(n_pos=5, n_neg=95)
        m10 = compute_metrics(y_true, y_score, top_fraction=0.10)
        m50 = compute_metrics(y_true, y_score, top_fraction=0.50)
        # With perfect ranking, top-10% (10 sites) captures all 5 positives.
        assert m10["recall_at_top"] == pytest.approx(1.0)
        # With 50% = 50 sites, recall is still 1.0 (all positives in top-10).
        assert m50["recall_at_top"] == pytest.approx(1.0)


# ===========================================================================
# FoldMetrics and CVResult dataclasses
# ===========================================================================

class TestDataclasses:
    def test_fold_metrics_construction(self):
        fm = FoldMetrics(
            fold=0,
            n_train=100,
            n_test=20,
            n_pos_test=2,
            auprc=0.45,
            auroc=0.80,
            precision_at_top=0.30,
            recall_at_top=0.60,
            enrichment_at_top=3.0,
        )
        assert fm.fold == 0
        assert fm.auprc == pytest.approx(0.45)

    def test_cv_result_construction(self):
        result = _make_cv_result("A", [0.3, 0.4, 0.35])
        assert result.model_type == "A"
        assert len(result.folds) == 3
        assert result.mean_auprc == pytest.approx(np.mean([0.3, 0.4, 0.35]))

    def test_model_comparison_construction(self):
        mc = ModelComparison(
            model_a_type="A",
            model_b_type="C",
            delta_auprc=0.05,
            p_value=0.03,
            significant=True,
        )
        assert mc.significant is True
        assert mc.delta_auprc == pytest.approx(0.05)
